# TEMPO App Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the working, local-first TEMPO app: a calm iPhone timeline planner with a brain-dump tray, manual placement, protected routines and breaks, overrun reflow, quiet carry-over, and an installable offline shell.

**Architecture:** React 19 + Vite + TypeScript. `domain` holds pure rules and algorithms; `application` holds use cases and ports; `infrastructure` implements Dexie, time, export, and platform adapters; `presentation` holds React components and hooks; `app` wires them. IndexedDB is the only source of truth. No backend, accounts, sync, push, analytics, or paid service.

**Tech Stack:** React 19, TypeScript, Vite, Dexie, `dexie-react-hooks`, CSS Modules, Vitest, Testing Library, fake-indexeddb, Playwright WebKit, axe-core, `vite-plugin-pwa`, Geist Sans (self-hosted Latin subset), Web Crypto (platform only).

**Security:** D-032–D-035 apply to every task. Read `AGENTS.md`, `docs/security/README.md`, `docs/security/threat-model.md`, `docs/security/controls.md`, and `docs/security/privacy.md` before storage, import/export, encryption, service-worker, or dependency work. A task is not complete until its abuse case is tested and its control ID is named in the commit.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-25-tempo-design.md` (approved 2026-10-25). Read the named section before each task. The app plan covers the product app and its installable shell; the operations plan covers CI, Pages, and the autonomous loop.

---

## Locked implementation choices

These are the §11.8/§23.2 choices, decided here so the tasks do not drift:

- **Dates:** hand-rolled local-date helpers. No date library in v1. Use local calendar components and noon anchors; never add 86,400,000 ms to a timestamp.
- **`.ics`:** hand-rolled minimal RFC 5545 writer. No `.ics` library unless the escaping/folding tests cannot pass.
- **PWA:** `vite-plugin-pwa` with `generateSW`, `registerType: 'prompt'`, and a custom update toast. No auto-reload mid-edit.
- **Visual regression:** Playwright screenshots, dark and light, at the three target widths. No paid visual service.
- **Transactions:** a small `UnitOfWork` port wraps Dexie `rw` transactions. Repositories stay thin. Multi-record writes always use it.
- **Live queries:** presentation calls a `DayPlanReader` port through `dexie-react-hooks`; presentation never imports Dexie directly.
- **Code splitting:** no route-level splitting in v1. The initial bundle budget is 180 KB gzipped; lazy-load Settings and Import/Export only if the budget fails.
- **Font:** copy the Latin variable WOFF2 from `@fontsource-variable/geist` into `src/assets/fonts/` and self-host it. The package is a dev dependency only.
- **IDs:** `crypto.randomUUID()` at the application edge. Domain factories accept an id so tests are deterministic.
- **Time format:** store minute-of-day integers. Format in presentation with `Intl.DateTimeFormat` using the user's locale and the `timeFormat` setting.
- **Placement:** spec §9.5.4 step 6 resolves every block at or after the resolved moving start, including blocks that originally sat between the new and old start. Blocks before the target are obstacles and never shift.
- **Security:** no third-party runtime origin; strict meta CSP with no inline script and no `unsafe-inline` style; a self-hosted `public/boot.css` for first paint; no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, `eval`, or `new Function`; strict import validation before any write; optional encrypted backup with Web Crypto (PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA-256 600,000 iterations, AES-256-GCM); live data protected by the OS only in v1.
- **Enforcement:** `security-static`, `security-supply-chain`, `security-workflows`, `security-secrets`, and `security-policy` are required checks. Security, dependency, workflow, storage, permission, and release changes need human review and never auto-merge.

## Milestone 0 — Foundation

### Task 0.1: Create the Vite React TypeScript project

**Files:**
- Create: `package.json`
- Create: `index.html`
- Create: `vite.config.ts`
- Create: `tsconfig.json`
- Create: `tsconfig.app.json`
- Create: `tsconfig.node.json`
- Create: `.gitignore`

- [ ] **Step 1: Initialize npm and install runtime dependencies**

Run:

```powershell
npm init -y
npm install react@19 react-dom@19 dexie dexie-react-hooks
npm install -D vite @vitejs/plugin-react typescript @types/react @types/react-dom @types/node
```

Expected: `package.json` and `package-lock.json` exist; no install errors.

- [ ] **Step 2: Replace `package.json` with the project scripts**

```json
{
  "name": "tempo",
  "private": true,
  "version": "0.1.0",
  "type": "module",
  "scripts": {
    "dev": "vite",
    "build": "tsc -b && vite build",
    "preview": "vite preview",
    "typecheck": "tsc -b --pretty false",
    "lint": "eslint .",
    "test": "vitest run",
    "test:watch": "vitest",
    "test:domain": "vitest run --config vitest.domain.config.ts",
    "test:coverage": "vitest run --coverage",
    "test:e2e": "playwright test",
    "test:a11y": "playwright test --grep @a11y",
    "format": "prettier --write ."
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `index.html`**

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#05070c" />
    <meta name="color-scheme" content="dark light" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="default" />
```

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  server: { port: 5173, strictPort: true },
  preview: { port: 4173, strictPort: true },
  build: { target: 'es2022', cssCodeSplit: false },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create the TypeScript configs**

`tsconfig.json`:

```json
{
  "files": [],
  "references": [
    { "path": "./tsconfig.app.json" },
    { "path": "./tsconfig.node.json" }
  ]
}
```

`tsconfig.app.json`:

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "ES2022",
    "useDefineForClassFields": true,
    "lib": ["ES2022", "DOM", "DOM.Iterable"],
    "module": "ESNext",
    "skipLibCheck": true,
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "allowImportingTsExtensions": false,
    "resolveJsonModule": true,
    "isolatedModules": true,
    "noEmit": true,
    "jsx": "react-jsx",
    "strict": true,
    "noUncheckedIndexedAccess": true,
    "noUnusedLocals": true,
    "noUnusedParameters": true,
    "noFallthroughCasesInSwitch": true,
    "baseUrl": ".",
    "paths": { "@/*": ["src/*"] },
    "types": ["vite/client", "vitest/globals"]
  },
  "include": ["src"]
}
```

`tsconfig.node.json`:

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "composite": true,
    "skipLibCheck": true,
    "module": "ESNext",
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "allowSyntheticDefaultImports": true,
    "strict": true,
    "noEmit": true
  },
  "include": ["vite.config.ts", "vitest.config.ts", "vitest.domain.config.ts", "playwright.config.ts"]
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `.gitignore`**

```text
node_modules/
dist/
coverage/
playwright-report/
test-results/
.vite/
*.local
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the entry files**

`src/main.tsx`:

```tsx
import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { App } from './app/App';
import './presentation/styles/tokens.css';
import './presentation/styles/global.css';

const root = document.getElementById('root');
if (!root) throw new Error('Root element not found');

createRoot(root).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

`src/app/App.tsx`:

```tsx
export function App() {
  return <main>TEMPO</main>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Verify the scaffold**

Run:

```powershell
npm run typecheck
npm run build
```

Expected: both pass; `dist/` is created.

- [ ] **Step 9: Commit**

```powershell
git add package.json package-lock.json index.html vite.config.ts tsconfig*.json src/main.tsx src/app/App.tsx .gitignore
git commit -m "chore: scaffold Vite React TypeScript app"
```

### Task 0.2: Add lint, formatting, boundaries, security rules, and unit-test setup

**Files:**
- Create: `eslint.config.js`
- Create: `.prettierrc.json`

- Create: `vitest.domain.config.ts`
- Create: `src/test/setup.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Install tooling**

```powershell
npm install -D eslint @eslint/js typescript-eslint eslint-plugin-react-hooks eslint-plugin-boundaries prettier vitest jsdom @testing-library/react @testing-library/user-event @testing-library/jest-dom fake-indexeddb @vitest/coverage-v8
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create `eslint.config.js`**

```js
import js from '@eslint/js';
import tseslint from 'typescript-eslint';
import reactHooks from 'eslint-plugin-react-hooks';
import boundaries from 'eslint-plugin-boundaries';

export default tseslint.config(
  { ignores: ['dist', 'coverage', 'playwright-report', 'test-results'] },
  js.configs.recommended,
  ...tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
  {
    files: ['**/*.js', '**/*.mjs', '**/*.cjs', '*.config.ts'],
    extends: [tseslint.configs.disableTypeChecked],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: { projectService: false },
      globals: {
        document: 'readonly',
        localStorage: 'readonly',
        matchMedia: 'readonly',
        window: 'readonly',
      },
    },
  },
  {
    files: ['src/**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: { projectService: true, tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname },
    },
    plugins: { 'react-hooks': reactHooks, boundaries },
    settings: {
      'boundaries/elements': [
        { type: 'domain', pattern: 'src/domain/**' },
        { type: 'application', pattern: 'src/application/**' },
        { type: 'infrastructure', pattern: 'src/infrastructure/**' },
        { type: 'presentation', pattern: 'src/presentation/**' },
        { type: 'app', pattern: 'src/app/**' },
        { type: 'test', pattern: 'src/test/**' },
      ],
    },
    rules: {
      ...reactHooks.configs.recommended.rules,
      '@typescript-eslint/consistent-type-imports': 'error',
      '@typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises': 'error',
      'no-eval': 'error',
      'no-implied-eval': 'error',
      'no-new-func': 'error',
      'no-restricted-syntax': [
        'error',
        {
          selector: "JSXAttribute[name.name='dangerouslySetInnerHTML']",
          message: 'Dynamic HTML is forbidden. Render text through React.',
        },
        {
          selector: "AssignmentExpression[left.property.name='innerHTML']",
          message: 'Dynamic HTML is forbidden. Use textContent or React rendering.',
        },
        {
          selector: "CallExpression[callee.name='eval']",
          message: 'eval is forbidden.',
        },
        {
          selector: "NewExpression[callee.name='Function']",
          message: 'new Function is forbidden.',
        },
      ],
      'boundaries/dependencies': [
        'error',
        {
          default: 'disallow',
          policies: [
            {
              from: { element: { type: 'application' } },
              allow: [{ to: { element: { type: 'domain' } } }],
            },
            {
              from: { element: { type: 'infrastructure' } },
              allow: [
                { to: { element: { type: 'domain' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'application' } } },
              ],
            },
            {
              from: { element: { type: 'presentation' } },
              allow: [
                { to: { element: { type: 'domain' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'application' } } },
              ],
            },
            {
              from: { element: { type: 'app' } },
              allow: [
                { to: { element: { type: 'domain' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'application' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'infrastructure' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'presentation' } } },
              ],
            },
            {
              from: { element: { type: 'test' } },
              allow: [
                { to: { element: { type: 'domain' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'application' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'infrastructure' } } },
                { to: { element: { type: 'presentation' } } },
              ],
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    },
  },
);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `vitest.config.ts`**

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    globals: true,
    setupFiles: ['./src/test/setup.ts'],
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    coverage: {
      provider: 'v8',
      reporter: ['text', 'html'],
      include: ['src/**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
      exclude: ['src/main.tsx', 'src/test/**', 'src/**/*.d.ts'],
      thresholds: { lines: 80, functions: 80, branches: 75, statements: 80 },
    },
  },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `vitest.domain.config.ts`**

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';

export default defineConfig({
  test: {
    environment: 'node',
    globals: true,
    include: ['src/domain/**/*.test.ts'],
    coverage: {
      provider: 'v8',
      reporter: ['text'],
      include: ['src/domain/**/*.ts'],
      thresholds: { lines: 95, functions: 95, branches: 90, statements: 95 },
    },
  },
});
```

```tsx
it('renders the app', () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the theme bootstrap**

Create `public/theme-boot.js`:

```js
(function () {
  var stored = localStorage.getItem('tempo.theme');
  var mode = stored === 'dark' || stored === 'light' ? stored : 'system';
  var dark = mode === 'dark' || (mode === 'system' && matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches);
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme = dark ? 'dark' : 'light';
})();
```

In `index.html`, load it before the module script:

```html
<script src="%BASE_URL%theme-boot.js"></script>
```

The bootstrap is a same-origin classic script, not inline. The production CSP can stay `script-src 'self'` with no hash and no `unsafe-inline`. It runs before first paint, so the theme does not flash. It only reads a validated enum from `localStorage` and writes a `data-theme` attribute.

```tsx
import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react';

export interface Services {
  appName: string;
}

const ServicesContext = createContext<Services | null>(null);

export function ServicesProvider({ services, children }: { services: Services; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <ServicesContext.Provider value={services}>{children}</ServicesContext.Provider>;
}

export function useServices(): Services {
  const value = useContext(ServicesContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('ServicesProvider is missing');
  return value;
}
```

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { AppShell } from '../presentation/components/AppShell';
import { useServices } from '../presentation/providers/services';
import { createContainer } from './container';
import { Providers } from './providers';
import styles from './App.module.css';

function Screen() {
  const { appName } = useServices();
  return (
    <AppShell header={<strong>{appName}</strong>}>
      <p className={styles.lead}>Plan the day, then let it move.</p>
    </AppShell>
  );
}

export function App() {
  const services = useMemo(() => createContainer(), []);
  return (
    <Providers services={services}>
      <Screen />
    </Providers>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { addLocalDays, type LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import { MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';

const KIND_LABEL: Record<Block['kind'], string> = { task: 'Task', focus: 'Focus', routine: 'Routine', break: 'Break' };

function stripControlCharacters(value: string): string {
  let result = '';
  for (const char of value) {
    const code = char.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    // Keep printable characters and newlines; drop every other control byte.
    if ((code >= 0x20 && code !== 0x7f) || code === 0x0a) result += char;
  }
  return result;
}

function escapeText(value: string): string {
  return stripControlCharacters(value)
    .replace(/\\/g, '\\\\')
    .replace(/;/g, '\\;')
    .replace(/,/g, '\\,')
    .replace(/\n/g, '\\n');
}

function safeUid(id: string): string {
  return id.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, '') || 'block';
}

function foldLine(line: string): string {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  for (const char of line) {
    const limit = parts.length === 0 ? 75 : 74;
    if (encoder.encode(current + char).length > limit) {
      parts.push(current);
      current = char;
    } else {
      current += char;
    }
  }
  parts.push(current);
  return parts.join('\r\n ');
}

function stamp(date: LocalDate, minuteOfDay: number): string {
  // A block may end exactly at midnight, which is a valid one-day range but an
  // invalid clock time. Roll that end to the next day's T000000.
  const endsAtMidnight = minuteOfDay === MINUTES_PER_DAY;
  const day = (endsAtMidnight ? addLocalDays(date, 1) : date).replaceAll('-', '');
  const withinDay = endsAtMidnight ? 0 : minuteOfDay;
  const hours = String(Math.floor(withinDay / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const minutes = String(withinDay % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${day}T${hours}${minutes}00`;
}

function utcStamp(now: Date): string {
  return now.toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, '').replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z');
}

export function buildIcs(blocks: Block[], date: LocalDate, alarmMinutes: number, now = new Date()): string {
  const lines: string[] = [
    'BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
    'VERSION:2.0',
    'PRODID:-//TEMPO//EN',
    'CALSCALE:GREGORIAN',
    `X-WR-CALNAME:TEMPO — ${date}`,
  ];
  for (const block of [...blocks].sort((a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin)) {
    lines.push(
      'BEGIN:VEVENT',
      `UID:${safeUid(block.id)}@tempo.local`,
      `DTSTAMP:${utcStamp(now)}`,
      `DTSTART:${stamp(date, block.startMin)}`,
      `DTEND:${stamp(date, block.startMin + block.durationMin)}`,
      `SUMMARY:${escapeText(block.title)}`,
      `DESCRIPTION:${escapeText(`Type: ${KIND_LABEL[block.kind]} | Duration: ${block.durationMin} min | Created by TEMPO.`)}`,
      'BEGIN:VALARM',
      'ACTION:DISPLAY',
      'DESCRIPTION:Starting now',
      `TRIGGER:${alarmMinutes === 0 ? 'PT0M' : `-PT${alarmMinutes}M`}`,
      'END:VALARM',
      'END:VEVENT',
    );
  }
  lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');
  return lines.map(foldLine).join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
}
```

```ts
describe('minute-of-day', () => {
  it('accepts only integers in the day', () => {
    expect(isMinuteOfDay(0)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMinuteOfDay(1439)).toBe(true);
    expect(isMinuteOfDay(1440)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMinuteOfDay(-1)).toBe(false);
    expect(isMinuteOfDay(12.5)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('snaps and ceils to five minutes without leaving the day', () => {
    expect(snapTo5(0)).toBe(0);
    expect(snapTo5(7)).toBe(5);
    expect(snapTo5(8)).toBe(10);
    expect(ceilTo5(0)).toBe(0);
    expect(ceilTo5(1)).toBe(5);
    expect(ceilTo5(1439)).toBe(MINUTES_PER_DAY);
  });

  it('clamps a block to the day and a five-minute minimum', () => {
    expect(clampBlock(-10, 30)).toEqual({ startMin: 0, durationMin: 30 });
    expect(clampBlock(1430, 30)).toEqual({ startMin: 1430, durationMin: 10 });
    expect(clampBlock(600, 2)).toEqual({ startMin: 600, durationMin: 5 });
    expect(clampBlock(600, 10)).toEqual({ startMin: 600, durationMin: 10 });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run to confirm failure**

```powershell
npx vitest run src/domain/values/minute-of-day.test.ts
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
export type MinuteOfDay = number;

export const MINUTES_PER_DAY = 1440;
export const MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES = 5;

export function isMinuteOfDay(value: number): value is MinuteOfDay {
  return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value < MINUTES_PER_DAY;
}

export function snapTo5(value: number): number {
  return Math.min(MINUTES_PER_DAY, Math.max(0, Math.round(value / 5) * 5));
}

export function ceilTo5(value: number): number {
  return Math.min(MINUTES_PER_DAY, Math.max(0, Math.ceil(value / 5) * 5));
}

export function clampBlock(startMin: number, durationMin: number): { startMin: number; durationMin: number } {
  const start = Math.min(MINUTES_PER_DAY - MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES, Math.max(0, Math.round(startMin)));
  const duration = Math.max(MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES, Math.min(MINUTES_PER_DAY - start, Math.round(durationMin)));
  return { startMin: start, durationMin: duration };
}
```

Expected: all pass. `escapeText` strips control bytes, keeps newlines, and escapes them as `\n`, so a title can never start a new iCalendar line; `safeUid` strips everything outside `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`. A block ending at 1440 is written as the next day's `T000000`, never the invalid `T240000`.

```ts
import type { LocalDate } from '../values/local-date';

export type TaskStatus = 'inbox' | 'planned' | 'done' | 'archived';

export interface Task {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  status: TaskStatus;
  carriedFrom?: LocalDate;
  sortOrder: number;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
  completedAt?: number;
  archivedAt?: number;
}

export function createTask(input: { id: string; title: string; now: number; sortOrder?: number; carriedFrom?: LocalDate }): Task {
  return {
    id: input.id,
    title: input.title.trim(),
    status: 'inbox',
    carriedFrom: input.carriedFrom,
    sortOrder: input.sortOrder ?? 0,
    createdAt: input.now,
    updatedAt: input.now,

}
```

```ts
import type { LocalDate } from '../values/local-date';
import type { MinuteOfDay } from '../values/minute-of-day';

export type BlockKind = 'task' | 'focus' | 'routine' | 'break';
export type BlockStatus = 'planned' | 'done';

export interface Block {
  id: string;
  date: LocalDate;
  startMin: MinuteOfDay;
  durationMin: number;
  kind: BlockKind;
  status: BlockStatus;
  title: string;
  taskId?: string;
  routineId?: string;
  anchor: boolean;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
  completedAt?: number;
}

export function blockEnd(block: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'durationMin'>): number {
  return block.startMin + block.durationMin;
}

export function createBlock(input: {
  id: string;
  date: LocalDate;
  startMin: number;
  durationMin: number;
  kind: BlockKind;
  title: string;
  taskId?: string;
  routineId?: string;
  anchor?: boolean;
  now: number;
}): Block {
  return {
    id: input.id,
    date: input.date,
    startMin: input.startMin,
    durationMin: input.durationMin,
    kind: input.kind,
    status: 'planned',
    title: input.title.trim(),
    taskId: input.taskId,
    routineId: input.routineId,
    anchor: input.anchor ?? false,
    createdAt: input.now,
    updatedAt: input.now,
  };
}
```

### Task 1.4: Overlap and block ordering helpers

**Files:**
- Create: `src/domain/services/overlap.ts`
- Create: `src/domain/services/overlap.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

```ts
import { blocksOverlap, compareBlocks, findOverlappingBlock } from './overlap';

const block = (id: string, startMin: number, durationMin: number, extra = {}) => ({
  id, startMin, durationMin, status: 'planned' as const, anchor: false, createdAt: 0, ...extra,
});

describe('overlap', () => {
  it('treats touching edges as non-overlapping', () => {
    expect(blocksOverlap(block('a', 540, 30), block('b', 570, 30))).toBe(false);
    expect(blocksOverlap(block('a', 540, 31), block('b', 570, 30))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('finds the first overlapping block and can ignore one id', () => {
    const a = block('a', 540, 30);
    const b = block('b', 555, 30);
    expect(findOverlappingBlock([a, b], block('c', 550, 10))?.id).toBe('a');
    expect(findOverlappingBlock([a, b], block('c', 550, 10), 'a')?.id).toBe('b');
  });

  it('orders by start, then creation, then id', () => {
    const items = [block('b', 600, 30), block('a', 600, 30), block('c', 540, 60)];
    expect(items.sort(compareBlocks).map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['c', 'a', 'b']);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';

export function blocksOverlap(
  a: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'durationMin'>,
  b: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'durationMin'>,
): boolean {
  return a.startMin < b.startMin + b.durationMin && b.startMin < a.startMin + a.durationMin;
}

export function findOverlappingBlock<T extends Pick<Block, 'id' | 'startMin' | 'durationMin'>>(
  blocks: T[],
  candidate: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'durationMin'>,
  ignoreId?: string,
): T | undefined {
  return blocks.find((block) => block.id !== ignoreId && blocksOverlap(block, candidate));
}

export function compareBlocks(
  a: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'createdAt' | 'id'>,
  b: Pick<Block, 'startMin' | 'createdAt' | 'id'>,
): number {
  if (a.startMin !== b.startMin) return a.startMin - b.startMin;
  if (a.createdAt !== b.createdAt) return a.createdAt - b.createdAt;
  return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import { blockEnd, type Block } from '../entities/block';
import { ceilTo5, MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../values/minute-of-day';
import { blocksOverlap, compareBlocks } from './overlap';

export interface BlockShift {
  blockId: string;
  fromStartMin: number;
  fromDurationMin: number;
  toStartMin: number;
  toDurationMin: number;
}

export type ReflowResult =
  | { kind: 'noop'; reason: 'already-clear' | 'no-blocks-after' }
  | { kind: 'preview'; changes: BlockShift[] }
  | { kind: 'blocked'; reason: 'no-slot' | 'past-midnight'; blockId: string; changes: BlockShift[] };

export interface ReflowInput {
  blocks: Block[];
  nowMin: number;
  overrunBlockId: string;
}

type SlotResult = { ok: true; startMin: number } | { ok: false; reason: 'no-slot' | 'past-midnight' };

function findEarliestStart(obstacles: Block[], durationMin: number, minStart: number): SlotResult {
  const first = ceilTo5(minStart);
  if (first + durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY) return { ok: false, reason: 'past-midnight' };

  for (let start = first; start + durationMin <= MINUTES_PER_DAY; start += 5) {
    const candidate = { startMin: start, durationMin };
    if (!obstacles.some((obstacle) => blocksOverlap(obstacle, candidate))) {
      return { ok: true, startMin: start };
    }
  }
  return { ok: false, reason: 'no-slot' };
}

export function previewReflow(input: ReflowInput): ReflowResult {
  const sorted = [...input.blocks].sort(compareBlocks);
  const overrun = sorted.find((block) => block.id === input.overrunBlockId);
  if (!overrun || overrun.status === 'done') return { kind: 'noop', reason: 'already-clear' };

  // A block is only overrun once its scheduled end has passed. While the
  // current time is still inside it, the day does not need to move yet.
  if (input.nowMin <= blockEnd(overrun)) return { kind: 'noop', reason: 'already-clear' };

  const newEnd = ceilTo5(input.nowMin);

  const after = sorted.filter((block) => block.id !== overrun.id && compareBlocks(block, overrun) > 0);
  if (after.length === 0) return { kind: 'noop', reason: 'no-blocks-after' };

  const fixed = input.blocks.filter((block) => block.id !== overrun.id && (block.anchor || block.status === 'done'));
  const changes: BlockShift[] = [];
  let cursor = newEnd;

  for (const block of after) {
    if (block.anchor || block.status === 'done') {
      cursor = Math.max(cursor, blockEnd(block));
      continue;
    }

    const slot = findEarliestStart(fixed, block.durationMin, Math.max(block.startMin, cursor));
    if (!slot.ok) return { kind: 'blocked', reason: slot.reason, blockId: block.id, changes };

    if (slot.startMin !== block.startMin) {
      changes.push({
        blockId: block.id,
        fromStartMin: block.startMin,
        fromDurationMin: block.durationMin,
        toStartMin: slot.startMin,
        toDurationMin: block.durationMin,
      });
    }
    cursor = slot.startMin + block.durationMin;
  }

  if (changes.length === 0) return { kind: 'noop', reason: 'already-clear' };
  return { kind: 'preview', changes };
}
```

The anchored/completed test must show the movable block flowing to 680, after both fixed blocks. `already-clear` is returned whenever the overrun block has not actually overrun yet (the current time is at or before its scheduled end), so a block that is still running is never reflowed.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import { ceilTo5, MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../values/minute-of-day';
import { blocksOverlap, compareBlocks } from './overlap';
import type { BlockShift } from './reflow';

export type PlacementResult =
  | { ok: true; movingStartMin: number; changes: BlockShift[] }
  | { ok: false; reason: 'no-slot' | 'past-midnight' | 'block-not-found' | 'block-done'; changes: BlockShift[] };

export interface PlacementInput {
  blocks: Block[];
  movingBlockId: string;
  targetStartMin: number;
  minStartMin: number;
}

type StartResult = { ok: true; startMin: number } | { ok: false; reason: 'no-slot' | 'past-midnight' };

function findNearestStart(
  obstacles: Block[],
  durationMin: number,
  targetStartMin: number,
  minStartMin: number,
  preferLater: boolean,
): StartResult {
  const minStart = ceilTo5(minStartMin);
  const target = Math.max(ceilTo5(targetStartMin), minStart);
  const maxStart = MINUTES_PER_DAY - durationMin;

  const fits = (start: number): boolean => {
    if (start < minStart || start > maxStart) return false;
    const candidate = { startMin: start, durationMin };
    return !obstacles.some((obstacle) => blocksOverlap(obstacle, candidate));
  };

  if (fits(target)) return { ok: true, startMin: target };

  // Search the drag direction first, then the other direction. This keeps a
  // later drag from snapping backward when a later slot is available.
  const directions = preferLater ? [1, -1] : [-1, 1];
  for (const direction of directions) {
    for (let start = target + direction * 5; start >= minStart && start <= maxStart; start += direction * 5) {
      if (fits(start)) return { ok: true, startMin: start };
    }
  }

  return { ok: false, reason: durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY ? 'past-midnight' : 'no-slot' };
}

export function resolvePlacement(input: PlacementInput): PlacementResult {
  const moving = input.blocks.find((block) => block.id === input.movingBlockId);
  if (!moving) return { ok: false, reason: 'block-not-found', changes: [] };
  if (moving.status === 'done') return { ok: false, reason: 'block-done', changes: [] };

  const target = Math.max(ceilTo5(input.targetStartMin), ceilTo5(input.minStartMin));
  const duration = Math.max(5, Math.min(MINUTES_PER_DAY, moving.durationMin));
  const obstacles = input.blocks.filter(
    (block) =>
      block.id !== moving.id &&
      (block.anchor || block.status === 'done' || block.startMin < target),
  );

  const preferLater = target > moving.startMin;
  const slot = findNearestStart(obstacles, duration, target, input.minStartMin, preferLater);
  if (!slot.ok) return { ok: false, reason: slot.reason, changes: [] };

  const movingStart = slot.startMin;
  const after = input.blocks
    .filter((block) => block.id !== moving.id && block.startMin >= movingStart)
    .sort(compareBlocks);

  const fixed = input.blocks.filter(
    (block) => block.id !== moving.id && (block.anchor || block.status === 'done'),
  );
  const changes: BlockShift[] = [];
  let cursor = movingStart + duration;

  for (const block of after) {
    if (block.anchor || block.status === 'done') {
      cursor = Math.max(cursor, block.startMin + block.durationMin);
      continue;
    }

    const next = findNearestStart(fixed, block.durationMin, Math.max(block.startMin, cursor), cursor, true);
    if (!next.ok) return { ok: false, reason: next.reason, changes };

    if (next.startMin !== block.startMin) {
      changes.push({
        blockId: block.id,
        fromStartMin: block.startMin,
        fromDurationMin: block.durationMin,
        toStartMin: next.startMin,
        toDurationMin: block.durationMin,
      });
    }
    cursor = next.startMin + block.durationMin;
  }

  return { ok: true, movingStartMin: movingStart, changes };
}
```

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import { createTask } from '../entities/task';
import { taskStatusAfterBlockChange, taskStatusAfterBlockDone } from './task-status';

const task = createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', now: 0 });
const block = (status: Block['status']): Block => ({
  id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', status, title: 'Draft', taskId: 't1', anchor: false, createdAt: 0, updatedAt: 0,
});

describe('task status', () => {
  it('marks the task done when every block is done', () => {
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockDone(task, [block('done')])).toBe('done');
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockDone(task, [block('done'), { ...block('done'), id: 'b2' }])).toBe('done');
  });

  it('keeps the task planned when a planned block remains', () => {
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockDone(task, [block('done'), { ...block('planned'), id: 'b2' }])).toBe('planned');
  });

  it('returns to inbox when the last planned block is removed or reopened', () => {
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task, [block('planned')])).toBe('planned');
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task, [])).toBe('inbox');
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task, [block('done')])).toBe('inbox');
  });

  it('keeps a done task done when a historical block is reopened without a planned block', () => {
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockChange({ ...task, status: 'done' }, [block('done')])).toBe('done');
  });

  it('never changes an archived task', () => {
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockDone({ ...task, status: 'archived' }, [])).toBe('archived');
    expect(taskStatusAfterBlockChange({ ...task, status: 'archived' }, [])).toBe('archived');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import type { Task, TaskStatus } from '../entities/task';

export function taskStatusAfterBlockDone(task: Task, blocks: Block[]): TaskStatus {
  if (task.status === 'archived') return 'archived';
  const own = blocks.filter((block) => block.taskId === task.id);
  return own.length > 0 && own.every((block) => block.status === 'done') ? 'done' : 'planned';
}

export function taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task: Task, blocks: Block[]): TaskStatus {
  if (task.status === 'archived') return 'archived';
  const own = blocks.filter((block) => block.taskId === task.id);
  if (own.some((block) => block.status === 'planned')) return 'planned';
  if (task.status === 'done') return 'done';
  return 'inbox';
}
```

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import type { Routine, RoutineException } from '../entities/routine';
import { isRoutineDue, isRoutinePlaced } from './routine-occurrence';

const routine: Routine = {
  id: 'r1', title: 'Morning walk', kind: 'routine', daysOfWeek: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], preferredStartMin: 480, durationMin: 30, anchor: true, active: true, createdAt: 0, updatedAt: 0,
};

const exception: RoutineException = { id: 'e1', routineId: 'r1', date: '2026-10-26', type: 'skip', createdAt: 0 };
const block: Block = { id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', status: 'planned', title: 'Morning walk', routineId: 'r1', anchor: true, createdAt: 0, updatedAt: 0 };

describe('routine occurrence', () => {
  it('is due on a matching weekday and not on a skipped date', () => {
    expect(isRoutineDue(routine, '2026-10-26', [])).toBe(true);
    expect(isRoutineDue(routine, '2026-10-26', [exception])).toBe(false);
    expect(isRoutineDue(routine, '2026-10-25', [])).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is never due when inactive', () => {
    expect(isRoutineDue({ ...routine, active: false }, '2026-10-26', [])).toBe(false);
  });

  it('detects a placed occurrence by routine and date', () => {
    expect(isRoutinePlaced([block], 'r1', '2026-10-26')).toBe(true);
    expect(isRoutinePlaced([block], 'r1', '2026-10-27')).toBe(false);
    expect(isRoutinePlaced([block], 'r2', '2026-10-26')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import type { Routine, RoutineException } from '../entities/routine';
import { dayOfWeek } from '../values/local-date';

export function isRoutineDue(routine: Routine, date: string, exceptions: RoutineException[]): boolean {
  if (!routine.active) return false;
  if (!routine.daysOfWeek.includes(dayOfWeek(date))) return false;
  return !exceptions.some((exception) => exception.routineId === routine.id && exception.date === date && exception.type === 'skip');
}

export function isRoutinePlaced(blocks: Block[], routineId: string, date: string): boolean {
  return blocks.some((block) => block.routineId === routineId && block.date === date);
}
```

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import { createTask } from '../entities/task';
import { planCarryOver } from './carry-over';

const task = (id: string, extra = {}) => ({ ...createTask({ id, title: id, now: 0 }), ...extra });
const block = (id: string, date: string, taskId: string | undefined, extra: Partial<Block> = {}): Block => ({
  id, date, startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', status: 'planned', title: id, taskId, anchor: false, createdAt: 0, updatedAt: 0, ...extra,
});

describe('planCarryOver', () => {
  it('returns unfinished past work to the inbox with a carried date', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({ today: '2026-10-25', tasks: [task('t1')], blocks: [block('b1', '2026-10-24', 't1')], now: 100 });
    expect(plan.deleteBlockIds).toEqual(['b1']);
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual(['t1']);
    expect(plan.taskUpdates[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 't1', status: 'inbox', carriedFrom: '2026-10-24' });
  });

  it('uses the latest source date across multiple days', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({ today: '2026-10-25', tasks: [task('t1', { carriedFrom: '2026-10-23' })], blocks: [block('b1', '2026-10-24', 't1')], now: 100 });
    expect(plan.taskUpdates[0]?.carriedFrom).toBe('2026-10-24');
  });

  it('uses the latest source date when past blocks arrive out of order', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({
      today: '2026-10-25',
      tasks: [task('t1')],
      blocks: [block('late', '2026-10-24', 't1'), block('early', '2026-10-23', 't1')],
      now: 100,
    });
    expect(plan.taskUpdates[0]?.carriedFrom).toBe('2026-10-24');
  });

  it('does not duplicate a task that already has a future planned block', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({
      today: '2026-10-25',
      tasks: [task('t1')],
      blocks: [block('old', '2026-10-24', 't1'), block('future', '2026-10-26', 't1')],
      now: 100,
    });
    expect(plan.deleteBlockIds).toEqual(['old']);
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(plan.taskUpdates[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 't1', status: 'planned', carriedFrom: undefined });
  });

  it('does not duplicate a task that already has a planned block today', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({
      today: '2026-10-25',
      tasks: [task('t1')],
      blocks: [block('old', '2026-10-24', 't1'), block('today', '2026-10-25', 't1')],
      now: 100,
    });
    expect(plan.deleteBlockIds).toEqual(['old']);
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(plan.taskUpdates[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 't1', status: 'planned', carriedFrom: undefined });
  });

  it('never touches routines or breaks', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({
      today: '2026-10-25',
      tasks: [],
      blocks: [block('r', '2026-10-24', undefined, { kind: 'routine', routineId: 'r1' }), block('br', '2026-10-24', undefined, { kind: 'break' })],
      now: 100,
    });
    expect(plan.deleteBlockIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(plan.taskUpdates).toEqual([]);
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('leaves done blocks alone and skips archived tasks', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({
      today: '2026-10-25',
      tasks: [task('t1', { status: 'archived' })],
      blocks: [block('done', '2026-10-24', 't1', { status: 'done' }), block('open', '2026-10-24', 't1')],
      now: 100,
    });
    expect(plan.deleteBlockIds).toEqual(['open']);
    expect(plan.taskUpdates).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('is idempotent when there is nothing unfinished', () => {
    const plan = planCarryOver({ today: '2026-10-25', tasks: [task('t1')], blocks: [block('b1', '2026-10-24', 't1', { status: 'done' })], now: 100 });
    expect(plan).toEqual({ deleteBlockIds: [], taskUpdates: [], carriedTaskIds: [] });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../entities/task';

export interface CarryOverInput {
  today: string;
  tasks: Task[];
  blocks: Block[];
  now: number;
}

export interface CarryOverPlan {
  deleteBlockIds: string[];
  taskUpdates: Task[];
  carriedTaskIds: string[];
}

export function planCarryOver(input: CarryOverInput): CarryOverPlan {
  const pastUnfinished = input.blocks.filter(
    (block) =>
      block.date < input.today &&
      block.status === 'planned' &&
      (block.kind === 'task' || block.kind === 'focus') &&
      Boolean(block.taskId),
  );
  if (pastUnfinished.length === 0) return { deleteBlockIds: [], taskUpdates: [], carriedTaskIds: [] };

  const futureTaskIds = new Set(
    input.blocks
      .filter(
        (block) =>
          block.date >= input.today &&
          block.status === 'planned' &&
          (block.kind === 'task' || block.kind === 'focus') &&
          block.taskId,
      )
      .map((block) => block.taskId as string),
  );
  const latestSource = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const block of pastUnfinished) {
    if (!block.taskId) continue;
    const previous = latestSource.get(block.taskId);
    if (!previous || block.date > previous) latestSource.set(block.taskId, block.date);
  }

  const taskUpdates: Task[] = [];
  const carriedTaskIds: string[] = [];

  for (const task of input.tasks) {
    if (!latestSource.has(task.id) || task.status === 'archived') continue;
    if (futureTaskIds.has(task.id)) {
      taskUpdates.push({
        ...task,
        status: 'planned',
        carriedFrom: undefined,
        completedAt: undefined,
        updatedAt: input.now,
      });
      continue;
    }
    taskUpdates.push({
      ...task,
      status: 'inbox',
      carriedFrom: latestSource.get(task.id),
      updatedAt: input.now,
    });
    carriedTaskIds.push(task.id);
  }

  return { deleteBlockIds: pastUnfinished.map((block) => block.id), taskUpdates, carriedTaskIds };
}
```

`src/infrastructure/db/tempo-db.ts`:

```ts
import Dexie, { type Table } from 'dexie';
import type { Block, MetaRecord, Routine, RoutineException, Settings, Task } from '../../domain/entities';

export const DB_NAME = 'tempo';

export class TempoDb extends Dexie {
  tasks!: Table<Task, string>;
  blocks!: Table<Block, string>;
  routines!: Table<Routine, string>;
  routineExceptions!: Table<RoutineException, string>;
  settings!: Table<Settings, string>;
  meta!: Table<MetaRecord, string>;

  constructor(name = DB_NAME) {
    super(name);
    this.version(1).stores({
      tasks: 'id, status, carriedFrom, sortOrder, updatedAt',
      blocks: 'id, date, [date+startMin], taskId, routineId, status, updatedAt',
      routines: 'id, active, updatedAt',
      routineExceptions: 'id, [routineId+date], routineId, date',
      settings: 'id',
      meta: 'key',
    });
  }
}

export function createTempoDb(name?: string): TempoDb {
  return new TempoDb(name);
}
```

```ts
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../../application/ports/task-repository';
import type { TempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';

export class DexieTaskRepository implements TaskRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb) {}
  listAll() { return this.db.tasks.orderBy('sortOrder').toArray(); }
  get(id: string) { return this.db.tasks.get(id); }
  async put(task: Task) { await this.db.tasks.put(task); }
  async bulkPut(tasks: Task[]) { await this.db.tasks.bulkPut(tasks); }
  async delete(id: string) { await this.db.tasks.delete(id); }
}
```

`src/infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository.ts`:

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../../application/ports/block-repository';
import type { TempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';

export class DexieBlockRepository implements BlockRepository {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb) {}
  listByDate(date: LocalDate) { return this.db.blocks.where('date').equals(date).sortBy('startMin'); }
  get(id: string) { return this.db.blocks.get(id); }
  async put(block: Block) { await this.db.blocks.put(block); }
  async bulkPut(blocks: Block[]) { await this.db.blocks.bulkPut(blocks); }
  async delete(id: string) { await this.db.blocks.delete(id); }
  async bulkDelete(ids: string[]) { await this.db.blocks.bulkDelete(ids); }
  async deleteByDateAndTask(date: LocalDate, taskId: string) {
    const ids = await this.db.blocks.where('date').equals(date).and((block) => block.taskId === taskId).primaryKeys();
    await this.db.blocks.bulkDelete(ids);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the adapters**

`src/infrastructure/db/unit-of-work.ts`:

```ts
import type { StoreName, UnitOfWork } from '../../application/ports/unit-of-work';
import type { TempoDb } from './tempo-db';

export class DexieUnitOfWork implements UnitOfWork {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb) {}
  run<T>(stores: StoreName[], fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const tables = stores.map((store) => this.db.table(store));
    return this.db.transaction('rw', tables, fn);
  }
}
```

`src/infrastructure/time/system-clock.ts`:

`src/infrastructure/repositories/dexie-day-plan-reader.ts`:

```ts
import type { DayPlan, DayPlanReader } from '../../application/ports/day-plan-reader';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { TempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';

export class DexieDayPlanReader implements DayPlanReader {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb) {}

  async read(date: LocalDate): Promise<DayPlan> {
    const [blocks, tasks, routines, routineExceptions, settings, meta] = await Promise.all([
      this.db.blocks.where('date').equals(date).sortBy('startMin'),
      this.db.tasks.toArray(),
      this.db.routines.toArray(),
      this.db.routineExceptions.where('date').equals(date).toArray(),
      this.db.settings.get('app'),
      this.db.meta.toArray(),
    ]);
    return { date, blocks, tasks, routines, routineExceptions, settings: settings ?? defaultSettings(0), meta };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Test the reader and transaction rollback**

```ts
import { createTempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieDayPlanReader } from './dexie-day-plan-reader';
import { createTask } from '../../domain/entities/task';

it('reads a day plan and rolls back a failed transaction', async () => {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-day-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const reader = new DexieDayPlanReader(db);

  await uow.run(['tasks'], async () => {
    await db.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', now: 100 }));
    throw new Error('rollback');
  }).catch(() => undefined);

  expect(await db.tasks.count()).toBe(0);
  expect((await reader.read('2026-10-25')).settings.theme).toBe('system');
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
});
```

```ts
import type { BackupFile } from '../../application/ports/backup-gateway';
import type { Block, MetaRecord, Routine, RoutineException, Settings, Task, TaskStatus } from '../../domain/entities';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import { isLocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import { isMinuteOfDay, MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES, MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';
import { BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION } from '../db/migrations';

const TASK_STATUSES: TaskStatus[] = ['inbox', 'planned', 'done', 'archived'];
const BLOCK_KINDS = ['task', 'focus', 'routine', 'break'] as const;
const BLOCK_STATUSES = ['planned', 'done'] as const;
const ROUTINE_KINDS = ['routine', 'break'] as const;
const THEMES = ['system', 'dark', 'light'] as const;
const TIME_FORMATS = ['system', '12h', '24h'] as const;
const SAFE_META_KEYS = new Set(['lastActiveDate', 'realPlanDates', 'lastBackupAt', 'backupReminderSnoozedUntil']);

export const MAX_IMPORT_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
export const MAX_ITEMS_PER_COLLECTION = 10_000;
export const MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 200;

const TASK_KEYS = new Set(['id', 'title', 'status', 'carriedFrom', 'sortOrder', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt', 'completedAt', 'archivedAt']);
const BLOCK_KEYS = new Set(['id', 'date', 'startMin', 'durationMin', 'kind', 'status', 'title', 'taskId', 'routineId', 'anchor', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt', 'completedAt']);
const ROUTINE_KEYS = new Set(['id', 'title', 'kind', 'daysOfWeek', 'preferredStartMin', 'durationMin', 'anchor', 'active', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt']);
const EXCEPTION_KEYS = new Set(['id', 'routineId', 'date', 'type', 'createdAt']);
const SETTINGS_KEYS = new Set(['id', 'theme', 'timeFormat', 'reflowAutoApply', 'inAppReminders', 'icsAlarmMinutes', 'installCardDismissed', 'updatedAt']);
const META_KEYS = new Set(['key', 'value']);
const FORBIDDEN_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype']);

export class BackupValidationError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'BackupValidationError';
  }
}

const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === 'object' && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
const isString = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0;
const isTimestamp = (value: unknown): value is number => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0;
const isOptionalTimestamp = (value: unknown): value is number | undefined => value === undefined || isTimestamp(value);
const isBoolean = (value: unknown): value is boolean => typeof value === 'boolean';

function fail(errors: string[]): never {
  throw new BackupValidationError(errors.slice(0, 8).join(' '));
}

function rejectUnsafeKeys(value: unknown, path: string, errors: string[]): void {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    value.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnsafeKeys(item, `${path}[${index}]`, errors));
    return;
  }
  if (!isRecord(value)) return;
  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
    if (FORBIDDEN_KEYS.has(key)) errors.push(`${path} contains a forbidden key.`);
    rejectUnsafeKeys(value[key], `${path}.${key}`, errors);
  }
}

function rejectUnknownKeys(value: unknown, allowed: Set<string>, path: string, errors: string[]): void {
  if (!isRecord(value)) return;
  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
    if (!allowed.has(key)) errors.push(`${path} has an unknown field.`);
  }
}

function taskFrom(raw: unknown, index: number, errors: string[]): Task | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(raw)) { errors.push(`Task ${index} is not an object.`); return undefined; }
  if (!isString(raw.id) || !isString(raw.title) || raw.title.length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH || !TASK_STATUSES.includes(raw.status as TaskStatus) || !isTimestamp(raw.createdAt) || !isTimestamp(raw.updatedAt)) {
    errors.push(`Task ${index} has an invalid shape.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  if (raw.carriedFrom !== undefined && !isLocalDate(raw.carriedFrom as string)) {
    errors.push(`Task ${index} has an invalid carriedFrom.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  if (!isOptionalTimestamp(raw.completedAt) || !isOptionalTimestamp(raw.archivedAt)) {
    errors.push(`Task ${index} has an invalid timestamp.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  return {
    id: raw.id,
    title: raw.title,
    status: raw.status as TaskStatus,
    carriedFrom: raw.carriedFrom as string | undefined,
    sortOrder: typeof raw.sortOrder === 'number' ? raw.sortOrder : 0,
    createdAt: raw.createdAt,
    updatedAt: raw.updatedAt,
    completedAt: raw.completedAt,
    archivedAt: raw.archivedAt,
  };
}

function blockFrom(raw: unknown, index: number, errors: string[]): Block | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(raw)) { errors.push(`Block ${index} is not an object.`); return undefined; }
  const start = raw.startMin;
  const duration = raw.durationMin;
  if (
    !isString(raw.id) || !isLocalDate(raw.date as string) ||
    typeof start !== 'number' || !isMinuteOfDay(start) ||
    typeof duration !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(duration) || duration < MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES ||
    !BLOCK_KINDS.includes(raw.kind as never) || !BLOCK_STATUSES.includes(raw.status as never) ||
    !isString(raw.title) || raw.title.length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH || !isBoolean(raw.anchor) || !isTimestamp(raw.createdAt) || !isTimestamp(raw.updatedAt) ||
    !isOptionalTimestamp(raw.completedAt)
  ) {
    errors.push(`Block ${index} has an invalid shape.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  if (raw.taskId !== undefined && !isString(raw.taskId)) { errors.push(`Block ${index} has an invalid taskId.`); return undefined; }
  if (raw.routineId !== undefined && !isString(raw.routineId)) { errors.push(`Block ${index} has an invalid routineId.`); return undefined; }
  return {
    id: raw.id, date: raw.date as string, startMin: start, durationMin: duration, kind: raw.kind as Block['kind'], status: raw.status as Block['status'],
    title: raw.title, taskId: raw.taskId, routineId: raw.routineId, anchor: raw.anchor,
    createdAt: raw.createdAt, updatedAt: raw.updatedAt, completedAt: raw.completedAt,
  };
}

function routineFrom(raw: unknown, index: number, errors: string[]): Routine | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(raw)) { errors.push(`Routine ${index} is not an object.`); return undefined; }
  const days = raw.daysOfWeek;
  if (
    !isString(raw.id) || !isString(raw.title) || raw.title.length > MAX_TITLE_LENGTH || !ROUTINE_KINDS.includes(raw.kind as never) ||
    !Array.isArray(days) || days.length === 0 || days.some((day) => !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 0 || day > 6) ||
    new Set(days).size !== days.length || !isMinuteOfDay(raw.preferredStartMin as number) ||
    typeof raw.durationMin !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(raw.durationMin) || raw.durationMin < MIN_BLOCK_MINUTES || raw.durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY ||
    !isBoolean(raw.anchor) || !isBoolean(raw.active) || !isTimestamp(raw.createdAt) || !isTimestamp(raw.updatedAt)
  ) {
    errors.push(`Routine ${index} has an invalid shape.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  return {
    id: raw.id, title: raw.title, kind: raw.kind as Routine['kind'], daysOfWeek: days as number[],
    preferredStartMin: raw.preferredStartMin as number, durationMin: raw.durationMin, anchor: raw.anchor, active: raw.active,
    createdAt: raw.createdAt, updatedAt: raw.updatedAt,
  };
}

function exceptionFrom(raw: unknown, index: number, errors: string[]): RoutineException | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(raw)) { errors.push(`Routine exception ${index} is not an object.`); return undefined; }
  if (!isString(raw.id) || !isString(raw.routineId) || !isLocalDate(raw.date as string) || raw.type !== 'skip' || !isTimestamp(raw.createdAt)) {
    errors.push(`Routine exception ${index} has an invalid shape.`);
    return undefined;
  }
  return { id: raw.id, routineId: raw.routineId, date: raw.date as string, type: 'skip', createdAt: raw.createdAt };
}

function settingsFrom(raw: unknown, errors: string[]): Settings | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(raw)) { errors.push('Settings are not an object.'); return undefined; }
  if (
    raw.id !== 'app' || !THEMES.includes(raw.theme as never) || !TIME_FORMATS.includes(raw.timeFormat as never) ||
    !isBoolean(raw.reflowAutoApply) || !isBoolean(raw.inAppReminders) || !isBoolean(raw.installCardDismissed) ||
    typeof raw.icsAlarmMinutes !== 'number' || ![0, 5, 10, 15, 30, 60].includes(raw.icsAlarmMinutes) ||
    !isTimestamp(raw.updatedAt)
  ) {
    errors.push('Settings have an invalid shape.');
    return undefined;
  }
  return {
    id: 'app', theme: raw.theme as Settings['theme'], timeFormat: raw.timeFormat as Settings['timeFormat'],
    reflowAutoApply: raw.reflowAutoApply, inAppReminders: raw.inAppReminders, icsAlarmMinutes: raw.icsAlarmMinutes,
    installCardDismissed: raw.installCardDismissed, updatedAt: raw.updatedAt,
  };
}

function metaFrom(raw: unknown, errors: string[]): MetaRecord[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) { errors.push('Meta is not an array.'); return []; }
  const records: MetaRecord[] = [];
  for (const item of raw) {
    if (!isRecord(item) || !isString(item.key)) { errors.push('A meta record is invalid.'); continue; }
    if (!SAFE_META_KEYS.has(item.key)) continue;
    const value = item.value;
    const validPrimitive = typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean';
    const validPlanDates = item.key === 'realPlanDates' && Array.isArray(value) && value.every((entry) => isLocalDate(entry as string));
    if (!validPrimitive && !validPlanDates) { errors.push(`Meta ${item.key} has an invalid value.`); continue; }
    records.push({ key: item.key, value });
  }
  return records;
}

export function validateBackupFile(raw: unknown): BackupFile {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  if (!isRecord(raw)) fail(['The backup is not an object.']);
  if (raw.app !== 'tempo') fail(['This is not a TEMPO backup.']);
  if (typeof raw.schemaVersion !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(raw.schemaVersion) || raw.schemaVersion < 1) {
    fail(['The backup schema version is invalid.']);
  }
  if (raw.schemaVersion > BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new BackupValidationError('This backup was made by a newer version of TEMPO.');
  }
  if (raw.schemaVersion !== BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION) fail(['The backup schema version is not supported.']);
  if (typeof raw.exportedAt !== 'string' || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(raw.exportedAt))) fail(['The backup export date is invalid.']);
  if (!isRecord(raw.data)) fail(['The backup data is invalid.']);

  rejectUnsafeKeys(raw, 'backup', errors);
  rejectUnknownKeys(raw, new Set(['app', 'schemaVersion', 'exportedAt', 'data']), 'Backup', errors);

  const data = raw.data;
  if (!Array.isArray(data.tasks) || !Array.isArray(data.blocks) || !Array.isArray(data.routines) || !Array.isArray(data.routineExceptions) || !Array.isArray(data.meta)) {
    fail(['The backup collections are invalid.']);
  }

  for (const [name, collection] of Object.entries({
    tasks: data.tasks,
    blocks: data.blocks,
    routines: data.routines,
    routineExceptions: data.routineExceptions,
    meta: data.meta,
  })) {
    if (collection.length > MAX_ITEMS_PER_COLLECTION) errors.push(`${name} exceeds the import limit.`);
  }
  data.tasks.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnknownKeys(item, TASK_KEYS, `Task ${index}`, errors));
  data.blocks.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnknownKeys(item, BLOCK_KEYS, `Block ${index}`, errors));
  data.routines.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnknownKeys(item, ROUTINE_KEYS, `Routine ${index}`, errors));
  data.routineExceptions.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnknownKeys(item, EXCEPTION_KEYS, `Routine exception ${index}`, errors));
  rejectUnknownKeys(data.settings, SETTINGS_KEYS, 'Settings', errors);
  data.meta.forEach((item, index) => rejectUnknownKeys(item, META_KEYS, `Meta ${index}`, errors));

  const tasks = data.tasks.map((item, index) => taskFrom(item, index, errors)).filter((item): item is Task => Boolean(item));
  const blocks = data.blocks.map((item, index) => blockFrom(item, index, errors)).filter((item): item is Block => Boolean(item));
  const routines = data.routines.map((item, index) => routineFrom(item, index, errors)).filter((item): item is Routine => Boolean(item));
  const routineExceptions = data.routineExceptions.map((item, index) => exceptionFrom(item, index, errors)).filter((item): item is RoutineException => Boolean(item));
  const settings = settingsFrom(data.settings, errors);
  const meta = metaFrom(data.meta, errors);

  if (errors.length) fail(errors);
  if (!settings) fail(['Settings are missing.']);

  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const item of [...tasks, ...blocks, ...routines, ...routineExceptions]) {
    if (ids.has(item.id)) errors.push(`Duplicate id ${item.id}.`);
    ids.add(item.id);
  }
  const taskIds = new Set(tasks.map((task) => task.id));
  const routineIds = new Set(routines.map((routine) => routine.id));
  for (const block of blocks) {
    if (block.taskId && !taskIds.has(block.taskId)) errors.push(`Block ${block.id} references a missing task.`);
    if (block.routineId && !routineIds.has(block.routineId)) errors.push(`Block ${block.id} references a missing routine.`);
    if (block.startMin + block.durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY) errors.push(`Block ${block.id} crosses midnight.`);
  }
  for (const exception of routineExceptions) {
    if (!routineIds.has(exception.routineId)) errors.push(`Routine exception ${exception.id} references a missing routine.`);
  }
  const byDate = new Map<string, Block[]>();
  for (const block of blocks) byDate.set(block.date, [...(byDate.get(block.date) ?? []), block]);
  for (const [date, dayBlocks] of byDate) {
    const sorted = [...dayBlocks].sort((a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin);
    for (let index = 1; index < sorted.length; index += 1) {
      const previous = sorted[index - 1];
      const current = sorted[index];
      if (previous && current && blocksOverlap(previous, current)) errors.push(`Blocks overlap on ${date}.`);
    }
  }
  if (errors.length) fail(errors);

  return { app: 'tempo', schemaVersion: 1, exportedAt: raw.exportedAt, data: { tasks, blocks, routines, routineExceptions, settings, meta } };
}

export function parseBackupText(text: string): unknown {
  if (new TextEncoder().encode(text).byteLength > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) {
    throw new BackupValidationError('That backup is too large. The limit is 10 MB.');
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    throw new BackupValidationError('That file is not valid JSON.');
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2b: Confirm the security limits and text parser**

`backup-file.ts` already includes `MAX_IMPORT_BYTES`, `MAX_ITEMS_PER_COLLECTION`, `MAX_TITLE_LENGTH`, `rejectUnsafeKeys`, `rejectUnknownKeys`, and `parseBackupText`. Confirm each is present before moving on.

`parseBackupText` is the only path from a picked file to JSON. The codec branches on the encrypted envelope and validates plain files with `validateBackupFile`; do not call `JSON.parse` in the component.

- [ ] **Step 3: Test the validator**

```ts
import { validateBackupFile, BackupValidationError } from './backup-file';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';

const valid = {
  app: 'tempo',
  schemaVersion: 1,
  exportedAt: '2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z',
  data: {
    tasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', status: 'inbox', sortOrder: 0, createdAt: 1, updatedAt: 1 }],
    blocks: [{ id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', status: 'planned', title: 'Draft', taskId: 't1', anchor: false, createdAt: 1, updatedAt: 1 }],
    routines: [],
    routineExceptions: [],
    settings: defaultSettings(1),
    meta: [{ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: '2026-10-25' }, { key: 'unknown', value: { nested: true } }],
  },
};

it('accepts a valid backup and ignores unknown meta', () => {
  const file = validateBackupFile(valid);
  expect(file.data.meta).toEqual([{ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: '2026-10-25' }]);
});

it('rejects a newer schema with a plain message', () => {
  expect(() => validateBackupFile({ ...valid, schemaVersion: 2 })).toThrow('newer version of TEMPO');
});

it('rejects overlapping blocks', () => {
  const overlap = {
    ...valid,
    data: {
      ...valid.data,
      blocks: [
        valid.data.blocks[0],
        { ...valid.data.blocks[0], id: 'b2', startMin: 555 },
      ],
    },
  };
  expect(() => validateBackupFile(overlap)).toThrow(BackupValidationError);
});

it('rejects a broken reference and a midnight crossing', () => {
  expect(() => validateBackupFile({ ...valid, data: { ...valid.data, tasks: [] } })).toThrow('references a missing task');
  expect(() => validateBackupFile({ ...valid, data: { ...valid.data, blocks: [{ ...valid.data.blocks[0], startMin: 1430, durationMin: 30 }] } })).toThrow('crosses midnight');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3b: Test the security cases**

Add to `backup-file.security.test.ts`:

```ts
import { BackupValidationError, MAX_IMPORT_BYTES, parseBackupText, validateBackupFile } from './backup-file';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';

function validBackup() {
  return {
    app: 'tempo',
    schemaVersion: 1,
    exportedAt: '2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z',
    data: {
      tasks: [], blocks: [], routines: [], routineExceptions: [], settings: defaultSettings(1), meta: [],
    },
  };
}

it('rejects a huge file before parsing', () => {
  expect(() => parseBackupText('x'.repeat(MAX_IMPORT_BYTES + 1))).toThrow('too large');
});

it('rejects prototype-pollution keys at any depth', () => {
  const file = validBackup();
  (file.data.tasks as unknown[]).push({ id: 't1', __proto__: { polluted: true } });
  expect(() => validateBackupFile(file)).toThrow(BackupValidationError);
});

it('rejects unknown top-level and collection fields', () => {
  expect(() => validateBackupFile({ ...validBackup(), extra: true })).toThrow('unknown field');
  const file = validBackup();
  (file.data.tasks as unknown[]).push({ id: 't1', title: 'x', status: 'inbox', sortOrder: 0, createdAt: 1, updatedAt: 1, evil: true });
  expect(() => validateBackupFile(file)).toThrow('unknown field');
});

it('rejects oversized arrays and titles before a write', () => {
  const huge = validBackup();
  huge.data.tasks = Array.from({ length: 10_001 }, (_, index) => ({
    id: `t${index}`, title: 'x', status: 'inbox', sortOrder: 0, createdAt: 1, updatedAt: 1,
  })) as typeof huge.data.tasks;
  expect(() => validateBackupFile(huge)).toThrow('import limit');

  const longTitle = validBackup();
  longTitle.data.tasks.push({
    id: 't1', title: 'x'.repeat(201), status: 'inbox', sortOrder: 0, createdAt: 1, updatedAt: 1,
  } as never);
  expect(() => validateBackupFile(longTitle)).toThrow(BackupValidationError);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the gateway**

`src/infrastructure/export/dexie-backup-gateway.ts`:

```ts
import type { BackupFile, BackupGateway, ImportSummary } from '../../application/ports/backup-gateway';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../../application/ports/unit-of-work';
import type { MetaRecord } from '../../domain/entities';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import type { TempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';
import { validateBackupFile } from './backup-file';

const SAFE_META_KEYS = new Set(['lastActiveDate', 'realPlanDates', 'lastBackupAt', 'backupReminderSnoozedUntil']);

function safeMeta(meta: MetaRecord[]): MetaRecord[] {
  return meta.filter((record) => SAFE_META_KEYS.has(record.key));
}

async function putNewer<T extends { id: string; updatedAt: number }>(
  table: { get: (id: string) => Promise<T | undefined>; put: (value: T) => Promise<unknown> },
  incoming: T,
  summary: ImportSummary,
): Promise<void> {
  const local = await table.get(incoming.id);
  if (local && local.updatedAt >= incoming.updatedAt) {
    summary.skipped += 1;
    return;
  }
  await table.put(incoming);
  if (local) summary.updated += 1;
  else summary.added += 1;
}

export class DexieBackupGateway implements BackupGateway {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb, private readonly uow: UnitOfWork) {}

  async exportAll(): Promise<BackupFile> {
    const [tasks, blocks, routines, routineExceptions, settings, meta] = await Promise.all([
      this.db.tasks.toArray(), this.db.blocks.toArray(), this.db.routines.toArray(),
      this.db.routineExceptions.toArray(), this.db.settings.get('app'), this.db.meta.toArray(),
    ]);

    const settingsRecord = settings ?? defaultSettings(Date.now());
    if (!settings) await this.db.settings.put(settingsRecord);

    return {
      app: 'tempo', schemaVersion: 1, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      data: { tasks, blocks, routines, routineExceptions, settings: settingsRecord, meta: safeMeta(meta) },
    };
  }

  async importAll(raw: BackupFile, mode: 'replace' | 'merge'): Promise<ImportSummary> {
    const file = validateBackupFile(raw);
    if (mode === 'replace') {
      return this.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks', 'routines', 'routineExceptions', 'settings', 'meta'], async () => {
        await Promise.all([this.db.tasks.clear(), this.db.blocks.clear(), this.db.routines.clear(), this.db.routineExceptions.clear(), this.db.settings.clear(), this.db.meta.clear()]);
        await this.db.tasks.bulkPut(file.data.tasks);
        await this.db.blocks.bulkPut(file.data.blocks);
        await this.db.routines.bulkPut(file.data.routines);
        await this.db.routineExceptions.bulkPut(file.data.routineExceptions);
        await this.db.settings.put(file.data.settings);
        await this.db.meta.bulkPut(safeMeta(file.data.meta));
        return { added: file.data.tasks.length + file.data.blocks.length + file.data.routines.length + file.data.routineExceptions.length, updated: 0, skipped: 0 };
      });
    }

    return this.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks', 'routines', 'routineExceptions'], async () => {
      const localTasks = await this.db.tasks.toArray();
      const localBlocks = await this.db.blocks.toArray();
      const localRoutines = await this.db.routines.toArray();
      const localExceptions = await this.db.routineExceptions.toArray();
      const tasks = new Map(localTasks.map((task) => [task.id, task]));
      const routines = new Map(localRoutines.map((routine) => [routine.id, routine]));
      const blocks = new Map(localBlocks.map((block) => [block.id, block]));

      for (const task of file.data.tasks) tasks.set(task.id, task);
      for (const routine of file.data.routines) routines.set(routine.id, routine);
      for (const block of file.data.blocks) blocks.set(block.id, block);
      for (const block of file.data.blocks) {
        if (block.taskId && !tasks.has(block.taskId)) throw new Error(`Block ${block.id} references a missing task.`);
        if (block.routineId && !routines.has(block.routineId)) throw new Error(`Block ${block.id} references a missing routine.`);
        for (const local of localBlocks.filter((item) => item.date === block.date && item.id !== block.id)) {
          if (blocksOverlap(local, block)) throw new Error(`Blocks overlap on ${block.date}.`);
        }
      }

      const summary: ImportSummary = { added: 0, updated: 0, skipped: 0 };
      for (const task of file.data.tasks) await putNewer(this.db.tasks, task, summary);
      for (const block of file.data.blocks) await putNewer(this.db.blocks, block, summary);
      for (const routine of file.data.routines) await putNewer(this.db.routines, routine, summary);
      for (const exception of file.data.routineExceptions) {
        const local = localExceptions.find((item) => item.id === exception.id);
        if (local && local.createdAt >= exception.createdAt) { summary.skipped += 1; continue; }
        await this.db.routineExceptions.put(exception);
        if (local) summary.updated += 1; else summary.added += 1;
      }
      return summary;
    });
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Test replace/merge and rollback**

```ts
import { createTempoDb } from '../db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieBackupGateway } from './dexie-backup-gateway';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import { createTask } from '../../domain/entities/task';

const file = {
  app: 'tempo' as const,
  schemaVersion: 1 as const,
  exportedAt: '2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z',
  data: {
    tasks: [createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Imported', now: 10 })],
    blocks: [], routines: [], routineExceptions: [], settings: defaultSettings(10), meta: [],
  },
};

it('replaces and merges without overwriting newer local data', async () => {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-backup-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const gateway = new DexieBackupGateway(db, new DexieUnitOfWork(db));
  await db.tasks.put({ ...createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Local', now: 20 }), updatedAt: 20 });
  const summary = await gateway.importAll(file, 'merge');
  expect(summary.skipped).toBe(1);
  expect((await db.tasks.get('t1'))?.title).toBe('Local');
  await gateway.importAll(file, 'replace');
  expect((await db.tasks.get('t1'))?.title).toBe('Imported');
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
});

it('leaves the previous data intact when a replace fails validation', async () => {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-backup-fail-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const gateway = new DexieBackupGateway(db, new DexieUnitOfWork(db));
  await db.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 'keep', title: 'Keep me', now: 10 }));
  const bad = { ...file, data: { ...file.data, blocks: [{ id: 'b1' }] } };
  await expect(gateway.importAll(bad as never, 'replace')).rejects.toThrow();
  expect((await db.tasks.get('keep'))?.title).toBe('Keep me');
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
});
```

```ts
import { buildIcs } from './ics-writer';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';

const block = (extra: Partial<Block> = {}): Block => ({
  id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', status: 'planned', title: 'Deep work', anchor: false, createdAt: 0, updatedAt: 0, ...extra,
});

it('writes a floating local event with an alarm', () => {
  const ics = buildIcs([block()], '2026-10-25', 10, new Date('2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z'));
  expect(ics).toContain('BEGIN:VCALENDAR');
  expect(ics).toContain('DTSTART:20261025T090000');
  expect(ics).toContain('DTEND:20261025T093000');
  expect(ics).toContain('TRIGGER:-PT10M');
  expect(ics).toContain('UID:b1@tempo.local');
  expect(ics).toContain('DTSTAMP:20261025T120000Z');
});

it('escapes text and folds long lines', () => {
  const ics = buildIcs([block({ title: 'A, B; C\\D\nE' })], '2026-10-25', 0, new Date('2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z'));
  expect(ics).toContain('SUMMARY:A\\, B\\; C\\\\D\\nE');
  expect(ics).toContain('TRIGGER:PT0M');
  const long = buildIcs([block({ title: 'x'.repeat(120) })], '2026-10-25', 0, new Date('2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z'));
  for (const line of long.split('\r\n')) expect(new TextEncoder().encode(line).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(75);
});

it('rolls a block ending at midnight to the next day', () => {
  const ics = buildIcs([block({ startMin: 1410, durationMin: 30 })], '2026-10-25', 0, new Date('2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z'));
  expect(ics).toContain('DTSTART:20261025T233000');
  expect(ics).toContain('DTEND:20261026T000000');
  expect(ics).not.toContain('T240000');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';

const KIND_LABEL: Record<Block['kind'], string> = { task: 'Task', focus: 'Focus', routine: 'Routine', break: 'Break' };

function escapeText(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, '')
    .replace(/\\/g, '\\\\')
    .replace(/;/g, '\\;')
    .replace(/,/g, '\\,')
    .replace(/\r?\n/g, '\\n');
}

function safeUid(id: string): string {
  return id.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, '') || 'block';
}

function foldLine(line: string): string {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const parts: string[] = [];
  let current = '';
  for (const char of line) {
    const limit = parts.length === 0 ? 75 : 74;
    if (encoder.encode(current + char).length > limit) {
      parts.push(current);
      current = char;
    } else {
      current += char;
    }
  }
  parts.push(current);
  return parts.join('\r\n ');
}

function stamp(date: LocalDate, minuteOfDay: number): string {
  const day = date.replaceAll('-', '');
  const hours = String(Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
  const minutes = String(minuteOfDay % 60).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${day}T${hours}${minutes}00`;
}

function utcStamp(now: Date): string {
  return now.toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, '').replace(/\.\d{3}Z$/, 'Z');
}

export function buildIcs(blocks: Block[], date: LocalDate, alarmMinutes: number, now = new Date()): string {
  const lines: string[] = [
    'BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
    'VERSION:2.0',
    'PRODID:-//TEMPO//EN',
    'CALSCALE:GREGORIAN',
    `X-WR-CALNAME:TEMPO — ${date}`,
  ];
  for (const block of [...blocks].sort((a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin)) {
    lines.push(
      'BEGIN:VEVENT',
      `UID:${safeUid(block.id)}@tempo.local`,
      `DTSTAMP:${utcStamp(now)}`,
      `DTSTART:${stamp(date, block.startMin)}`,
      `DTEND:${stamp(date, block.startMin + block.durationMin)}`,
      `SUMMARY:${escapeText(block.title)}`,
      `DESCRIPTION:${escapeText(`Type: ${KIND_LABEL[block.kind]} | Duration: ${block.durationMin} min | Created by TEMPO.`)}`,
      'BEGIN:VALARM',
      'ACTION:DISPLAY',
      'DESCRIPTION:Starting now',
      `TRIGGER:${alarmMinutes === 0 ? 'PT0M' : `-PT${alarmMinutes}M`}`,
      'END:VALARM',
      'END:VEVENT',
    );
  }
  lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');
  return lines.map(foldLine).join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
}
```

```ts
  requestPersistence(): Promise<boolean>;
  persisted(): Promise<boolean>;
  estimate(): Promise<{ usage: number; quota: number } | undefined>;
```
