- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

`browser-storage-gateway.ts`:

```ts
import type { StorageGateway } from '../../application/ports/storage-gateway';

export class BrowserStorageGateway implements StorageGateway {
  async requestPersistence() {
    return (await navigator.storage?.persist?.()) ?? false;
  }

  async persisted() {
    return (await navigator.storage?.persisted?.()) ?? false;
  }

  async estimate() {
    const result = await navigator.storage?.estimate?.();
    return result ? { usage: result.usage ?? 0, quota: result.quota ?? 0 } : undefined;
  }
}
```

`browser-share-gateway.ts`:

```ts
import type { ShareGateway } from '../../application/ports/share-gateway';

function download(file: File): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
  const anchor = document.createElement('a');
  anchor.href = url;
  anchor.download = file.name;
  anchor.rel = 'noopener';
  document.body.append(anchor);
  anchor.click();
  anchor.remove();
  // Safari can abort a download if the object URL is revoked in the same task.
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 0);
}

export class BrowserShareGateway implements ShareGateway {
  canShareFile(file: File) {
    return (
      typeof navigator.share === 'function' &&
      typeof navigator.canShare === 'function' &&
      navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })
    );
  }

  async shareFile(file: File, title: string) {
    if (!this.canShareFile(file)) {
      download(file);
      return 'downloaded' as const;
    }
    await navigator.share({ files: [file], title });
    return 'shared' as const;
  }
}
```

`standalone.ts`:

```ts
export function isStandalone(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  const displayMode = window.matchMedia?.('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ?? false;
  const navigatorStandalone =
    typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && (navigator as Navigator & { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true;
  return displayMode || navigatorStandalone;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Test the fallbacks**

```ts
import { BrowserShareGateway } from './browser-share-gateway';
import { BrowserStorageGateway } from './browser-storage-gateway';
import { isStandalone } from './standalone';

function override(target: object, key: string, value: unknown): () => void {
  const record = target as Record<string, unknown>;
  const original = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(record, key);
  Object.defineProperty(record, key, { configurable: true, value });
  return () => {
    if (original) Object.defineProperty(record, key, original);
    else Reflect.deleteProperty(record, key);
  };
}

describe('isStandalone', () => {
  it('detects standalone display mode', () => {
    const restoreMatchMedia = override(window, 'matchMedia', () => ({ matches: true }));
    try {
      expect(isStandalone()).toBe(true);
    } finally {
      restoreMatchMedia();
    }
  });

  it('detects the iOS navigator flag and defaults to false', () => {
    const restoreMatchMedia = override(window, 'matchMedia', () => ({ matches: false }));
    const restoreStandalone = override(navigator, 'standalone', true);
    try {
      expect(isStandalone()).toBe(true);
    } finally {
      restoreStandalone();
      restoreMatchMedia();
    }
    expect(isStandalone()).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('BrowserStorageGateway', () => {
  it('fails closed when the Storage API is unavailable', async () => {
    const restore = override(navigator, 'storage', undefined);
    try {
      const gateway = new BrowserStorageGateway();
      await expect(gateway.requestPersistence()).resolves.toBe(false);
      await expect(gateway.persisted()).resolves.toBe(false);
      await expect(gateway.estimate()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });

  it('reports persistence and a zero-filled estimate', async () => {
    const restore = override(navigator, 'storage', {
      persist: () => Promise.resolve(true),
      persisted: () => Promise.resolve(true),
      estimate: () => Promise.resolve({}),
    });
    try {
      const gateway = new BrowserStorageGateway();
      await expect(gateway.requestPersistence()).resolves.toBe(true);
      await expect(gateway.persisted()).resolves.toBe(true);
      await expect(gateway.estimate()).resolves.toEqual({ usage: 0, quota: 0 });
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });
});

describe('BrowserShareGateway', () => {
  it('shares a file when the platform supports it', async () => {
    const share = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    const restoreShare = override(navigator, 'share', share);
    const restoreCanShare = override(navigator, 'canShare', () => true);
    try {
      const gateway = new BrowserShareGateway();
      const file = new File(['{}'], 'tempo.json', { type: 'application/json' });
      expect(gateway.canShareFile(file)).toBe(true);
      await expect(gateway.shareFile(file, 'TEMPO backup')).resolves.toBe('shared');
      expect(share).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ files: [file], title: 'TEMPO backup' });
    } finally {
      restoreCanShare();
      restoreShare();
    }
  });

  it('downloads the file when sharing is unavailable', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const restoreShare = override(navigator, 'share', undefined);
    const restoreCanShare = override(navigator, 'canShare', undefined);
    const click = vi.spyOn(HTMLAnchorElement.prototype, 'click').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const createObjectURL = vi.fn(() => 'blob:tempo');
    const revokeObjectURL = vi.fn();
    const restoreCreate = override(URL, 'createObjectURL', createObjectURL);
    const restoreRevoke = override(URL, 'revokeObjectURL', revokeObjectURL);
    try {
      const gateway = new BrowserShareGateway();
      const file = new File(['{}'], 'tempo.json', { type: 'application/json' });
      expect(gateway.canShareFile(file)).toBe(false);
      await expect(gateway.shareFile(file, 'TEMPO backup')).resolves.toBe('downloaded');
      expect(createObjectURL).toHaveBeenCalledWith(file);
      expect(click).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(revokeObjectURL).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      vi.runAllTimers();
      expect(revokeObjectURL).toHaveBeenCalledWith('blob:tempo');
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
      vi.restoreAllMocks();
      restoreRevoke();
      restoreCreate();
      restoreCanShare();
      restoreShare();
    }
  });
});
```

Expected: all pass. Each test restores the browser globals it overrides. The share fallback appends the anchor, clicks it, and revokes the object URL on the next task so iOS Safari can start the download.

### Task 2.7: Optional passphrase-encrypted backup

**Files:**
- Create: `src/infrastructure/export/encrypted-backup.ts`
- Create: `src/infrastructure/export/encrypted-backup.test.ts`
- Create: `src/infrastructure/export/backup-codec.ts`
- Create: `src/infrastructure/export/backup-codec.test.ts`

- Modify: `src/infrastructure/export/backup-file.ts` (export `MAX_IMPORT_BYTES`)

This task implements D-035 and SEC-17/SEC-19. It uses Web Crypto only. Do not add a crypto library.

- [ ] **Step 1: Define the envelope and helpers**

`encrypted-backup.ts`:

```ts
import type { BackupFile } from '../../application/ports/backup-gateway';
import { MAX_IMPORT_BYTES, BackupValidationError, validateBackupFile } from './backup-file';

export const ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_FORMAT = 'tempo-encrypted-backup' as const;
export const ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_VERSION = 1 as const;
export const PBKDF2_ITERATIONS = 600_000;
const SALT_BYTES = 16;
const IV_BYTES = 12;
const TAG_BITS = 128;

export interface EncryptedBackupEnvelope {
  format: typeof ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_FORMAT;
  version: typeof ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_VERSION;
  kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA-256';
  iterations: typeof PBKDF2_ITERATIONS;
  cipher: 'AES-256-GCM';
  salt: string;
  iv: string;
  aad: string;
  ciphertext: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class EncryptedBackupError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('That file could not be opened. Check the passphrase and try again.');
    this.name = 'EncryptedBackupError';
  }
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const decoder = new TextDecoder();

function bytesToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = '';
  for (const byte of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte);
  return btoa(binary);
}

function base64ToBytes(value: string): Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> {
  const binary = atob(value);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < binary.length; index += 1) bytes[index] = binary.charCodeAt(index);
  return bytes;
}

function headerOf(envelope: Omit<EncryptedBackupEnvelope, 'aad' | 'ciphertext'>): string {
  return JSON.stringify({
    format: envelope.format,
    version: envelope.version,
    kdf: envelope.kdf,
    iterations: envelope.iterations,
    cipher: envelope.cipher,
    salt: envelope.salt,
    iv: envelope.iv,
    createdAt: envelope.createdAt,
  });
}

async function deriveKey(passphrase: string, salt: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<CryptoKey> {
  const material = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', encoder.encode(passphrase), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey']);
  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    { name: 'PBKDF2', salt, iterations: PBKDF2_ITERATIONS, hash: 'SHA-256' },
    material,
    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    false,
    ['encrypt', 'decrypt'],
  );
}

export function isEncryptedBackup(raw: unknown): raw is EncryptedBackupEnvelope {
  return typeof raw === 'object' && raw !== null && !Array.isArray(raw) && (raw as { format?: unknown }).format === ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_FORMAT;
}

export async function encryptBackup(file: BackupFile, passphrase: string): Promise<EncryptedBackupEnvelope> {
  if (passphrase.length < 12) throw new BackupValidationError('Use a passphrase of at least 12 characters.');
  const plaintext = encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(file));
  if (plaintext.byteLength > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) throw new BackupValidationError('The backup is too large to encrypt.');

  const salt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(SALT_BYTES));
  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(IV_BYTES));
  const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const header = headerOf({
    format: ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_FORMAT,
    version: ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_VERSION,
    kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA-256',
    iterations: PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
    cipher: 'AES-256-GCM',
    salt: bytesToBase64(salt),
    iv: bytesToBase64(iv),
    createdAt,
  });
  const key = await deriveKey(passphrase, salt);
  const ciphertext = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: encoder.encode(header), tagLength: TAG_BITS },
    key,
    plaintext,
  );

  return {
    format: ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_FORMAT,
    version: ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_VERSION,
    kdf: 'PBKDF2-SHA-256',
    iterations: PBKDF2_ITERATIONS,
    cipher: 'AES-256-GCM',
    salt: bytesToBase64(salt),
    iv: bytesToBase64(iv),
    aad: bytesToBase64(encoder.encode(header)),
    ciphertext: bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array(ciphertext)),
    createdAt,
  };
}

export async function decryptBackup(raw: unknown, passphrase: string): Promise<BackupFile> {
  if (!isEncryptedBackup(raw)) throw new EncryptedBackupError();
  const envelope = raw;
  if (
    envelope.version !== ENCRYPTED_BACKUP_VERSION ||
    envelope.kdf !== 'PBKDF2-SHA-256' ||
    envelope.iterations !== PBKDF2_ITERATIONS ||
    envelope.cipher !== 'AES-256-GCM' ||
    typeof envelope.salt !== 'string' ||
    typeof envelope.iv !== 'string' ||
    typeof envelope.aad !== 'string' ||
    typeof envelope.ciphertext !== 'string' ||
    typeof envelope.createdAt !== 'string'
  ) {
    throw new EncryptedBackupError();
  }

  try {
    const salt = base64ToBytes(envelope.salt);
    const iv = base64ToBytes(envelope.iv);
    if (salt.byteLength !== SALT_BYTES || iv.byteLength !== IV_BYTES) throw new Error('bad envelope');
    const header = headerOf(envelope);
    if (envelope.aad !== bytesToBase64(encoder.encode(header))) throw new Error('bad aad');
    const key = await deriveKey(passphrase, salt);
    const plaintext = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv, additionalData: encoder.encode(header), tagLength: TAG_BITS },
      key,
      base64ToBytes(envelope.ciphertext),
    );
    if (plaintext.byteLength > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) throw new Error('too large');
    return validateBackupFile(JSON.parse(decoder.decode(plaintext)));
  } catch {
    throw new EncryptedBackupError();
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Test the failure modes**

`encrypted-backup.test.ts`:

```ts
import { decryptBackup, encryptBackup, EncryptedBackupError } from './encrypted-backup';
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import { createTask } from '../../domain/entities/task';

const file = {
  app: 'tempo' as const,
  schemaVersion: 1 as const,
  exportedAt: '2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z',
  data: { tasks: [], blocks: [], routines: [], routineExceptions: [], settings: defaultSettings(1), meta: [] },
};

it('round-trips a backup', async () => {
  const envelope = await encryptBackup(file, 'correct horse battery staple');
  await expect(decryptBackup(envelope, 'correct horse battery staple')).resolves.toEqual(file);
});

it('fails a wrong passphrase and a tampered file with the same message', async () => {
  const envelope = await encryptBackup(file, 'correct horse battery staple');
  await expect(decryptBackup(envelope, 'wrong passphrase')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
  const tampered = { ...envelope, ciphertext: `${envelope.ciphertext.slice(0, -1)}A` };
  await expect(decryptBackup(tampered, 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
  const reheadered = { ...envelope, iterations: 1 as never };
  await expect(decryptBackup(reheadered, 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
  const resalted = { ...envelope, salt: `${envelope.salt.slice(0, -4)}AAAA` };
  await expect(decryptBackup(resalted, 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
});

it('rejects a short passphrase', async () => {
  await expect(encryptBackup(file, 'short')).rejects.toThrow('at least 12');
});

it('never stores the passphrase or plaintext in the envelope', async () => {
  const titled = {
    ...file,
    data: { ...file.data, tasks: [createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Private plan detail', now: 1 })] },
  };
  const envelope = await encryptBackup(titled, 'correct horse battery staple');
  const serialised = JSON.stringify(envelope);
  expect(serialised).not.toContain('Private plan detail');
  expect(serialised).not.toContain('correct horse battery staple');
});

it('rejects a plain file and a malformed envelope', async () => {
  await expect(decryptBackup(file, 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
  await expect(decryptBackup({ format: 'tempo-encrypted-backup' }, 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(
    EncryptedBackupError,
  );
  await expect(decryptBackup([], 'correct horse battery staple')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the infrastructure codec**

`src/infrastructure/export/backup-codec.ts`:

```ts
import type { BackupCodec, BackupFile } from '../../application/ports/backup-gateway';
import { parseBackupText, validateBackupFile } from './backup-file';
import { decryptBackup, encryptBackup, isEncryptedBackup } from './encrypted-backup';

export const backupCodec: BackupCodec = {
  async parseText(text: string, passphrase?: string): Promise<BackupFile> {
    const raw = parseBackupText(text);
    return isEncryptedBackup(raw) ? decryptBackup(raw, passphrase ?? '') : validateBackupFile(raw);
  },
  encrypt(file: BackupFile, passphrase: string): Promise<unknown> {
    return encryptBackup(file, passphrase);
  },
};
```

`backup-codec.test.ts`:

```ts
import { defaultSettings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import { MAX_IMPORT_BYTES } from './backup-file';
import { backupCodec } from './backup-codec';
import { EncryptedBackupError } from './encrypted-backup';

const file = {
  app: 'tempo' as const,
  schemaVersion: 1 as const,
  exportedAt: '2026-10-25T12:00:00.000Z',
  data: { tasks: [], blocks: [], routines: [], routineExceptions: [], settings: defaultSettings(1), meta: [] },
};

it('parses a plain backup without a passphrase', async () => {
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText(JSON.stringify(file))).resolves.toEqual(file);
});

it('parses an encrypted backup with the passphrase', async () => {
  const envelope = await backupCodec.encrypt(file, 'correct horse battery staple');
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText(JSON.stringify(envelope), 'correct horse battery staple')).resolves.toEqual(file);
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText(JSON.stringify(envelope), 'wrong passphrase')).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText(JSON.stringify(envelope))).rejects.toThrow(EncryptedBackupError);
});

it('rejects oversized input before parsing', async () => {
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText('x'.repeat(MAX_IMPORT_BYTES + 1))).rejects.toThrow('too large');
});

it('rejects malformed JSON and an invalid plain backup', async () => {
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText('{')).rejects.toThrow('not valid JSON');
  await expect(backupCodec.parseText('{"app":"other"}')).rejects.toThrow('not a TEMPO backup');
});
```

Expected: all pass. The application layer depends only on the `BackupCodec` port; it must not import `backup-file.ts` or `encrypted-backup.ts`. The wrong-passphrase, tampered-ciphertext, reheadered, resalted, and malformed-envelope cases all fail with the same `EncryptedBackupError`, so the UI cannot become an oracle. The envelope contains no passphrase and no plaintext.

`src/domain/errors.ts`:

```ts
export type DomainErrorCode =
  | 'EMPTY_TITLE'
  | 'TITLE_TOO_LONG'
  | 'TASK_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'TASK_ARCHIVED'
  | 'BLOCK_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'BLOCK_DONE'
  | 'BLOCK_OVERLAP'
  | 'NO_SLOT'
  | 'PAST_DATE'
  | 'ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND'
  | 'ROUTINE_NOT_DUE'
  | 'ROUTINE_ALREADY_PLACED'
  | 'INVALID_INPUT';

export class DomainError extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly code: DomainErrorCode, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DomainError';
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement add and update**

`add-task.ts`:

```ts
import { createTask, type Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';

export interface AddTaskInput { title: string; carriedFrom?: LocalDate; }
export interface AddTaskDeps { tasks: TaskRepository; clock: Clock; createId: () => string; }

export function makeAddTask(deps: AddTaskDeps) {
  return async (input: AddTaskInput): Promise<Task> => {
    const title = input.title.trim();
    if (!title) throw new DomainError('EMPTY_TITLE', 'Give the task a name.');
    if (title.length > 200) throw new DomainError('TITLE_TOO_LONG', 'Keep the title under 200 characters.');
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const task = createTask({ id: deps.createId(), title, now, carriedFrom: input.carriedFrom });
    await deps.tasks.put(task);
    return task;
  };
}
```

`update-task.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';

export interface UpdateTaskInput { id: string; title?: string; archive?: boolean; }
export function makeUpdateTask(deps: { tasks: TaskRepository; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: UpdateTaskInput): Promise<Task> => {
    const task = await deps.tasks.get(input.id);
    if (!task) throw new DomainError('TASK_NOT_FOUND', 'That task no longer exists.');
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    if (input.archive) {
      const archived: Task = { ...task, status: 'archived', archivedAt: now, updatedAt: now };
      await deps.tasks.put(archived);
      return archived;
    }
    const title = input.title?.trim() ?? task.title;
    if (!title) throw new DomainError('EMPTY_TITLE', 'Give the task a name.');
    if (title.length > 200) throw new DomainError('TITLE_TOO_LONG', 'Keep the title under 200 characters.');
    const updated: Task = { ...task, title, updatedAt: now };
    await deps.tasks.put(updated);
    return updated;
  };
}
```

`add-task.test.ts` uses the shared fake:

```ts
import { makeAddTask } from './add-task';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';

const clock = { now: () => new Date(1000) };
const repo = (): TaskRepository & { values: Map<string, Task> } => {
  const values = new Map<string, Task>();
  return {
    values,
    listAll: () => Promise.resolve([...values.values()]),
    get: (id) => Promise.resolve(values.get(id)),
    put: (task) => {
      values.set(task.id, task);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    bulkPut: (tasks) => {
      tasks.forEach((task) => values.set(task.id, task));
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    delete: (id) => {
      values.delete(id);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
  };
};

it('adds a trimmed task and rejects an empty title', async () => {
  const tasks = repo();
  const add = makeAddTask({ tasks, clock, createId: () => 't1' });
  const task = await add({ title: '  Draft  ' });
  expect(task).toMatchObject({ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', status: 'inbox', createdAt: 1000, updatedAt: 1000 });
  await expect(add({ title: '   ' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'EMPTY_TITLE' });
  await expect(add({ title: 'x'.repeat(201) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TITLE_TOO_LONG' });
  expect(tasks.values.size).toBe(1);
});

it('records where a carried task came from', async () => {
  const tasks = repo();
  const add = makeAddTask({ tasks, clock, createId: () => 't1' });
  const task = await add({ title: 'Carried', carriedFrom: '2026-10-24' });
  expect(task.carriedFrom).toBe('2026-10-24');
});
```

const clock = { now: () => new Date(1000) };

  const values = new Map<string, Task>();
  return {
    values,
    listAll: () => Promise.resolve([...values.values()]),
    get: (id) => Promise.resolve(values.get(id)),
    put: (task) => {
      values.set(task.id, task);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    bulkPut: (tasks) => {
      tasks.forEach((task) => values.set(task.id, task));
      return Promise.resolve();
    },

      values.delete(id);
      return Promise.resolve();

  await tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', now: 0 }));

  expect((await update({ id: 't1', title: '  Final  ' })).title).toBe('Final');

  await expect(update({ id: 'missing' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TASK_NOT_FOUND' });

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `place-task.ts`**

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { resolvePlacement } from '../../domain/services/placement';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export interface PlaceTaskInput {
  taskId: string;
  date: LocalDate;
  startMin: number;
  durationMin?: number;
  minStartMin?: number;
}

export function makePlaceTask(deps: {
  tasks: TaskRepository;
  blocks: BlockRepository;
  uow: UnitOfWork;
  clock: Clock;
  createId: () => string;
}) {
  return async (input: PlaceTaskInput) => {
    const task = await deps.tasks.get(input.taskId);
    if (!task) throw new DomainError('TASK_NOT_FOUND', 'That task no longer exists.');
    if (task.status === 'archived') throw new DomainError('TASK_ARCHIVED', 'That task is archived.');
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const existing = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    const current = existing.find((block) => block.taskId === task.id);
    const durationMin = input.durationMin ?? current?.durationMin ?? 30;
    if (!Number.isInteger(durationMin) || durationMin < 5) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a duration of at least 5 minutes.');
    }
    const draft = current
      ? { ...current, durationMin }
      : createBlock({ id: deps.createId(), date: input.date, startMin: input.startMin, durationMin, kind: 'task', title: task.title, taskId: task.id, now });
    // The draft carries the new duration, so a resize-and-place computes its
    // shifts against the block's real footprint instead of its old one.
    const movable = existing.filter((block) => block.id !== draft.id);
    const result = resolvePlacement({
      blocks: [...movable, draft],
      movingBlockId: draft.id,
      targetStartMin: input.startMin,
      minStartMin: input.minStartMin ?? 0,
    });
    if (!result.ok) throw new DomainError('NO_SLOT', 'There is no room for that block.');

    await deps.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks'], async () => {
      const byId = new Map(existing.map((block) => [block.id, block]));
      byId.set(draft.id, { ...draft, startMin: result.movingStartMin, durationMin, updatedAt: now });
      for (const change of result.changes) {
        const block = byId.get(change.blockId);
        if (block) byId.set(block.id, { ...block, startMin: change.toStartMin, durationMin: change.toDurationMin, updatedAt: now });
      }
      await deps.blocks.bulkPut([...byId.values()]);
      await deps.tasks.put({ ...task, status: 'planned', completedAt: undefined, updatedAt: now });
    });

    return { blockId: draft.id, startMin: result.movingStartMin, changes: result.changes };
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `move-block.ts`**

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { resolvePlacement } from '../../domain/services/placement';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makeMoveBlock(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; uow: UnitOfWork; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { blockId: string; targetStartMin: number; minStartMin?: number }) => {
    const block = await deps.blocks.get(input.blockId);
    if (!block) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'That block no longer exists.');
    if (block.status === 'done') throw new DomainError('BLOCK_DONE', 'Reopen the block before moving it.');
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(block.date);
    const result = resolvePlacement({ blocks, movingBlockId: block.id, targetStartMin: input.targetStartMin, minStartMin: input.minStartMin ?? 0 });
    if (!result.ok) throw new DomainError('NO_SLOT', 'There is no room for that block.');

    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    await deps.uow.run(['blocks'], async () => {
      const byId = new Map(blocks.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
      byId.set(block.id, { ...block, startMin: result.movingStartMin, updatedAt: now });
      for (const change of result.changes) {
        const item = byId.get(change.blockId);
        if (item) byId.set(item.id, { ...item, startMin: change.toStartMin, durationMin: change.toDurationMin, updatedAt: now });
      }
      await deps.blocks.bulkPut([...byId.values()]);
    });
    return result;
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement resize, complete, delete, break, and focus**

`resize-block.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import { MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';

export function makeResizeBlock(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { blockId: string; durationMin: number }) => {
    const block = await deps.blocks.get(input.blockId);
    if (!block) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'That block no longer exists.');
    if (block.status === 'done') throw new DomainError('BLOCK_DONE', 'Reopen the block before resizing it.');
    if (!Number.isInteger(input.durationMin) || input.durationMin < 5) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a duration of at least 5 minutes.');
    }
    const duration = input.durationMin;
    if (block.startMin + duration > MINUTES_PER_DAY) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'A block cannot cross midnight.');
    const updated = { ...block, durationMin: duration, updatedAt: deps.clock.now().getTime() };
    const others = (await deps.blocks.listByDate(block.date)).filter((item) => item.id !== block.id);
    if (others.some((item) => blocksOverlap(item, updated))) {
      throw new DomainError('BLOCK_OVERLAP', 'That would overlap another block. Choose a shorter length or move the other block.');
    }
    await deps.blocks.put(updated);
    return updated;
  };
}
```

`complete-block.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { taskStatusAfterBlockChange, taskStatusAfterBlockDone } from '../../domain/services/task-status';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makeCompleteBlock(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; tasks: TaskRepository; uow: UnitOfWork; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { blockId: string; done: boolean }) => {
    const block = await deps.blocks.get(input.blockId);
    if (!block) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'That block no longer exists.');
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const updated = {
      ...block,
      status: input.done ? ('done' as const) : ('planned' as const),
      completedAt: input.done ? now : undefined,
      updatedAt: now,
    };
    await deps.uow.run(['blocks', 'tasks'], async () => {
      await deps.blocks.put(updated);
      if (!block.taskId) return;
      const task = await deps.tasks.get(block.taskId);
      if (!task) return;
      const all = await deps.blocks.listByTask(block.taskId);
      const nextBlocks = all.map((item) => (item.id === block.id ? updated : item));
      const status = input.done ? taskStatusAfterBlockDone(task, nextBlocks) : taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task, nextBlocks);
      await deps.tasks.put({ ...task, status, completedAt: status === 'done' ? now : undefined, updatedAt: now });
    });
    return updated;
  };
}
```

`delete-block.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { taskStatusAfterBlockChange } from '../../domain/services/task-status';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makeDeleteBlock(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; tasks: TaskRepository; uow: UnitOfWork; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (blockId: string) => {
    const block = await deps.blocks.get(blockId);
    if (!block) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'That block no longer exists.');
    await deps.uow.run(['blocks', 'tasks'], async () => {
      await deps.blocks.delete(blockId);
      if (!block.taskId) return;
      const task = await deps.tasks.get(block.taskId);
      if (!task) return;
      const remaining = await deps.blocks.listByTask(block.taskId);
      await deps.tasks.put({ ...task, status: taskStatusAfterBlockChange(task, remaining), updatedAt: deps.clock.now().getTime() });
    });
  };
}
```

`add-break.ts`:

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import { MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';

export function makeAddBreak(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; clock: Clock; createId: () => string }) {
  return async (input: { date: LocalDate; startMin: number; durationMin: number; title?: string }) => {
    if (!Number.isInteger(input.startMin) || input.startMin < 0 || input.startMin >= MINUTES_PER_DAY) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a valid start time.');
    }
    if (!Number.isInteger(input.durationMin) || input.durationMin < 5) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a duration of at least 5 minutes.');
    }
    const title = input.title?.trim() || 'Break';
    if (title.length > 200) throw new DomainError('TITLE_TOO_LONG', 'Keep the title under 200 characters.');
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    const block = createBlock({
      id: deps.createId(), date: input.date, startMin: input.startMin, durationMin: input.durationMin,
      kind: 'break', title, anchor: true, now: deps.clock.now().getTime(),
    });
    if (block.startMin + block.durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'A block cannot cross midnight.');
    if (blocks.some((item) => blocksOverlap(item, block))) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_OVERLAP', 'That would overlap another block.');
    await deps.blocks.put(block);
    return block;
  };
}
```

import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import { MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';

export function makeAddFocus(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; clock: Clock; createId: () => string }) {
  return async (input: { date: LocalDate; startMin: number; durationMin: number; title: string }) => {
    const title = input.title.trim();
    if (!title) throw new DomainError('EMPTY_TITLE', 'Give the focus block a name.');
    if (title.length > 200) throw new DomainError('TITLE_TOO_LONG', 'Keep the title under 200 characters.');
    if (!Number.isInteger(input.startMin) || input.startMin < 0 || input.startMin >= MINUTES_PER_DAY) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a valid start time.');
    }
    if (!Number.isInteger(input.durationMin) || input.durationMin < 5) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a duration of at least 5 minutes.');
    }
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    const block = createBlock({
      id: deps.createId(), date: input.date, startMin: input.startMin, durationMin: input.durationMin,
      kind: 'focus', title, now: deps.clock.now().getTime(),
    });
    if (block.startMin + block.durationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'A block cannot cross midnight.');
    if (blocks.some((item) => blocksOverlap(item, block))) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_OVERLAP', 'That would overlap another block.');
    await deps.blocks.put(block);
    return block;
  };
}

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { createTempoDb } from '../../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieBlockRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository';
import { DexieTaskRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-task-repository';
import { makeAddBreak } from './add-break';
import { makeAddFocus } from './add-focus';
import { makeAddTask } from './add-task';
import { makeCompleteBlock } from './complete-block';
import { makeDeleteBlock } from './delete-block';
import { makeMoveBlock } from './move-block';
import { makePlaceTask } from './place-task';
import { makeResizeBlock } from './resize-block';

function harness() {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-blocks-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const tasks = new DexieTaskRepository(db);
  const blocks = new DexieBlockRepository(db);
  const clock = { now: () => new Date(1000) };
  const createId = () => crypto.randomUUID();
  return {
    db,
    tasks,
    blocks,
    addTask: makeAddTask({ tasks, clock, createId }),
    placeTask: makePlaceTask({ tasks, blocks, uow, clock, createId }),
    moveBlock: makeMoveBlock({ blocks, uow, clock }),
    resize: makeResizeBlock({ blocks, clock }),
    complete: makeCompleteBlock({ blocks, tasks, uow, clock }),
    remove: makeDeleteBlock({ blocks, tasks, uow, clock }),
    addBreak: makeAddBreak({ blocks, clock, createId }),
    addFocus: makeAddFocus({ blocks, clock, createId }),
  };
}

async function close(db: ReturnType<typeof createTempoDb>) {
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
}

it('places, moves, resizes, completes, reopens, and deletes a task block', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const task = await h.addTask({ title: 'Draft' });
    const placed = await h.placeTask({ taskId: task.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30 });
    expect((await h.tasks.get(task.id))?.status).toBe('planned');

    await h.moveBlock({ blockId: placed.blockId, targetStartMin: 600 });
    expect((await h.blocks.get(placed.blockId))?.startMin).toBe(600);

    await h.resize({ blockId: placed.blockId, durationMin: 45 });
    expect((await h.blocks.get(placed.blockId))?.durationMin).toBe(45);

    await h.complete({ blockId: placed.blockId, done: true });
    expect((await h.tasks.get(task.id))?.status).toBe('done');
    await h.complete({ blockId: placed.blockId, done: false });
    expect((await h.tasks.get(task.id))?.status).toBe('planned');

    await h.remove(placed.blockId);
    expect(await h.blocks.listByDate('2026-10-25')).toHaveLength(0);
    expect((await h.tasks.get(task.id))?.status).toBe('inbox');
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('uses the new duration when re-placing a task and pushes the next block', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const first = await h.addTask({ title: 'First' });
    const second = await h.addTask({ title: 'Second' });
    await h.placeTask({ taskId: first.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30 });
    const other = await h.placeTask({ taskId: second.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30 });

    const replaced = await h.placeTask({ taskId: first.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 90 });
    expect((await h.blocks.get(replaced.blockId))?.durationMin).toBe(90);
    expect((await h.blocks.get(other.blockId))?.startMin).toBe(630);

    const day = await h.blocks.listByDate('2026-10-25');
    expect(day.some((block) => block.startMin === 600)).toBe(false);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('moves a block earlier and pushes the blocks in between', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const first = await h.addTask({ title: 'First' });
    const second = await h.addTask({ title: 'Second' });
    const late = await h.placeTask({ taskId: first.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 660, durationMin: 30 });
    const middle = await h.placeTask({ taskId: second.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 630, durationMin: 30 });

    await h.moveBlock({ blockId: late.blockId, targetStartMin: 600 });
    expect((await h.blocks.get(late.blockId))?.startMin).toBe(600);
    expect((await h.blocks.get(middle.blockId))?.startMin).toBe(630);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('rejects placement for unknown and archived tasks', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await expect(
      h.placeTask({ taskId: 'missing', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540 }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TASK_NOT_FOUND' });
    const task = await h.addTask({ title: 'Old' });
    await h.tasks.put({ ...task, status: 'archived' });
    await expect(
      h.placeTask({ taskId: task.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540 }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TASK_ARCHIVED' });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('rejects a resize that overlaps, crosses midnight, or is too short', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'a', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'A', now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'b', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'B', now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'late', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 1400, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Late', now: 1 }),
    ]);
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: 'a', durationMin: 90 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_OVERLAP' });
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: 'late', durationMin: 60 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: 'a', durationMin: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: 'a', durationMin: 30.5 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: 'missing', durationMin: 30 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_NOT_FOUND' });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('rejects moving, resizing, or deleting a completed block', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const task = await h.addTask({ title: 'Done' });
    const placed = await h.placeTask({ taskId: task.id, date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540 });
    await h.complete({ blockId: placed.blockId, done: true });
    await expect(h.moveBlock({ blockId: placed.blockId, targetStartMin: 600 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_DONE' });
    await expect(h.resize({ blockId: placed.blockId, durationMin: 60 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_DONE' });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('adds anchored breaks and focus blocks and rejects overlap or midnight', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const rest = await h.addBreak({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 60 });
    expect(rest).toMatchObject({ kind: 'break', anchor: true, title: 'Break' });
    await expect(h.addFocus({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 560, durationMin: 30, title: 'Deep work' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BLOCK_OVERLAP',
    });
    const focus = await h.addFocus({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 660, durationMin: 90, title: '  Deep work  ' });
    expect(focus).toMatchObject({ kind: 'focus', title: 'Deep work', anchor: false });
    await expect(h.addFocus({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 660, durationMin: 90, title: ' ' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'EMPTY_TITLE',
    });
    await expect(h.addBreak({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 1400, durationMin: 60 })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.addBreak({ date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 900, durationMin: -30 })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});
```

Expected: all pass. `placeTask` feeds the draft (with its new duration) into `resolvePlacement`, so re-placing a task with a longer duration pushes overlapping blocks instead of persisting an overlap. `resizeBlock`, `addBreak`, and `addFocus` reject non-integer or sub-5-minute durations, invalid start times, and over-long titles with `INVALID_INPUT`/`TITLE_TOO_LONG`. `placeTask` clears `completedAt` when it plans a previously done task. The suite counts blocks with `listByDate` because the repository port exposes no `count`.

`add-routine.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { Routine } from '../../domain/entities/routine';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { RoutineRepository } from '../ports/routine-repository';

export interface RoutineInput {
  title: string;
  kind: 'routine' | 'break';
  daysOfWeek: number[];
  preferredStartMin: number;
  durationMin: number;
  anchor: boolean;
}

export function validateRoutineInput(input: RoutineInput): RoutineInput {
  const title = input.title.trim();
  if (!title) throw new DomainError('EMPTY_TITLE', 'Give the routine a name.');
  if (title.length > 200) throw new DomainError('TITLE_TOO_LONG', 'Keep the title under 200 characters.');
  if (input.daysOfWeek.length === 0 || input.daysOfWeek.some((day) => !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 0 || day > 6)) {
    throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose at least one valid day.');
  }
  if (new Set(input.daysOfWeek).size !== input.daysOfWeek.length) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose each day once.');
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.preferredStartMin) || input.preferredStartMin < 0 || input.preferredStartMin >= 1440) {
    throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a valid start time.');
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.durationMin) || input.durationMin < 5 || input.durationMin > 1440 - input.preferredStartMin) {
    throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a valid duration.');
  }
  return { ...input, title, daysOfWeek: [...input.daysOfWeek].sort((a, b) => a - b) };
}

export function makeAddRoutine(deps: { routines: RoutineRepository; clock: Clock; createId: () => string }) {
  return async (input: RoutineInput): Promise<Routine> => {
    const clean = validateRoutineInput(input);
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const routine: Routine = { id: deps.createId(), ...clean, active: true, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now };
    await deps.routines.put(routine);
    return routine;
  };
}
```

`update-routine.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { Routine } from '../../domain/entities/routine';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { RoutineRepository } from '../ports/routine-repository';
import { validateRoutineInput, type RoutineInput } from './add-routine';

export function makeUpdateRoutine(deps: { routines: RoutineRepository; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { id: string; patch: Partial<RoutineInput> & { active?: boolean } }): Promise<Routine> => {
    const routine = await deps.routines.get(input.id);
    if (!routine) throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND', 'That routine no longer exists.');
    const clean = validateRoutineInput({ ...routine, ...input.patch });
    const updated: Routine = { ...routine, ...clean, active: input.patch.active ?? routine.active, updatedAt: deps.clock.now().getTime() };
    await deps.routines.put(updated);
    return updated;
  };
}
```

`delete-routine.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { toLocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { RoutineExceptionRepository } from '../ports/routine-exception-repository';
import type { RoutineRepository } from '../ports/routine-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

function withoutRoutineLink(block: Block): Block {
  const copy = { ...block };
  delete copy.routineId;
  return copy;
}

export function makeDeleteRoutine(deps: {
  routines: RoutineRepository;
  blocks: BlockRepository;
  exceptions: RoutineExceptionRepository;
  uow: UnitOfWork;
  clock: Clock;
}) {
  return async (routineId: string) => {
    const routine = await deps.routines.get(routineId);
    if (!routine) throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND', 'That routine no longer exists.');
    const today = toLocalDate(deps.clock.now());

    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByRoutine(routineId);
    // Today's and future uncompleted blocks go. Completed blocks and past days
    // stay as history, with the routine link removed so no backup carries a
    // dangling reference.
    const removed = blocks.filter((block) => block.status === 'planned' && block.date >= today);
    const removedIds = new Set(removed.map((block) => block.id));
    const retained = blocks.filter((block) => !removedIds.has(block.id)).map(withoutRoutineLink);
    const exceptions = await deps.exceptions.listByRoutine(routineId);

    await deps.uow.run(['routines', 'blocks', 'routineExceptions'], async () => {
      if (retained.length > 0) await deps.blocks.bulkPut(retained);
      if (removed.length > 0) await deps.blocks.bulkDelete([...removedIds]);
      if (exceptions.length > 0) await deps.exceptions.bulkDelete(exceptions.map((exception) => exception.id));
      await deps.routines.delete(routineId);
    });

    return { deletedBlocks: removed.length, retainedBlocks: retained.length, deletedExceptions: exceptions.length };
  };
}
```

`place-routine.ts`:

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { resolvePlacement } from '../../domain/services/placement';
import { isRoutineDue, isRoutinePlaced } from '../../domain/services/routine-occurrence';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { RoutineExceptionRepository } from '../ports/routine-exception-repository';
import type { RoutineRepository } from '../ports/routine-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makePlaceRoutine(deps: {
  routines: RoutineRepository;
  blocks: BlockRepository;
  exceptions: RoutineExceptionRepository;
  uow: UnitOfWork;
  clock: Clock;
  createId: () => string;
}) {
  return async (input: { routineId: string; date: LocalDate; startMin?: number; minStartMin?: number }) => {
    const routine = await deps.routines.get(input.routineId);
    if (!routine) throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND', 'That routine no longer exists.');
    const exceptions = await deps.exceptions.listByDate(input.date);
    if (!isRoutineDue(routine, input.date, exceptions)) throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_NOT_DUE', 'That routine is not due today.');
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    if (isRoutinePlaced(blocks, routine.id, input.date)) {
      throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_ALREADY_PLACED', 'That routine is already on the timeline.');
    }

    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const draft = createBlock({
      id: deps.createId(),
      date: input.date,
      startMin: input.startMin ?? routine.preferredStartMin,
      durationMin: routine.durationMin,
      kind: routine.kind,
      title: routine.title,
      routineId: routine.id,
      anchor: routine.anchor,
      now,
    });
    const result = resolvePlacement({
      blocks: [...blocks, draft],
      movingBlockId: draft.id,
      targetStartMin: draft.startMin,
      minStartMin: input.minStartMin ?? 0,
    });
    if (!result.ok) throw new DomainError('NO_SLOT', 'No open slot today.');

    await deps.uow.run(['blocks'], async () => {
      const byId = new Map(blocks.map((block) => [block.id, block]));
      byId.set(draft.id, { ...draft, startMin: result.movingStartMin, updatedAt: now });
      for (const change of result.changes) {
        const block = byId.get(change.blockId);
        if (block) {
          byId.set(block.id, { ...block, startMin: change.toStartMin, durationMin: change.toDurationMin, updatedAt: now });
        }
      }
      await deps.blocks.bulkPut([...byId.values()]);
    });

    return { blockId: draft.id, startMin: result.movingStartMin, changes: result.changes };
  };
}
```

`skip-routine.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { RoutineExceptionRepository } from '../ports/routine-exception-repository';
import type { RoutineRepository } from '../ports/routine-repository';

export function makeSkipRoutine(deps: {
  routines: RoutineRepository;
  exceptions: RoutineExceptionRepository;
  clock: Clock;
  createId: () => string;
}) {
  return async (input: { routineId: string; date: LocalDate }) => {
    const routine = await deps.routines.get(input.routineId);
    if (!routine) throw new DomainError('ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND', 'That routine no longer exists.');
    const existing = await deps.exceptions.listByDate(input.date);
    const alreadySkipped = existing.find((exception) => exception.routineId === routine.id);
    if (alreadySkipped) return alreadySkipped;

    const exception = {
      id: deps.createId(),
      routineId: routine.id,
      date: input.date,
      type: 'skip' as const,
      createdAt: deps.clock.now().getTime(),
    };
    await deps.exceptions.put(exception);
    return exception;
  };
}
```

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { validateBackupFile } from '../../infrastructure/export/backup-file';
import { DexieBackupGateway } from '../../infrastructure/export/dexie-backup-gateway';
import { createTempoDb } from '../../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieBlockRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository';
import { DexieRoutineExceptionRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-routine-exception-repository';
import { DexieRoutineRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-routine-repository';
import { makeAddRoutine } from './add-routine';
import { makeDeleteRoutine } from './delete-routine';
import { makePlaceRoutine } from './place-routine';
import { makeSkipRoutine } from './skip-routine';
import { makeUpdateRoutine } from './update-routine';

function harness(now = new Date('2026-10-26T08:00:00')) {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-routines-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const routines = new DexieRoutineRepository(db);
  const exceptions = new DexieRoutineExceptionRepository(db);
  const blocks = new DexieBlockRepository(db);
  const clock = { now: () => now };
  const createId = () => crypto.randomUUID();
  return {
    db,
    uow,
    routines,
    exceptions,
    blocks,
    add: makeAddRoutine({ routines, clock, createId }),
    update: makeUpdateRoutine({ routines, clock }),
    place: makePlaceRoutine({ routines, blocks, exceptions, uow, clock, createId }),
    skip: makeSkipRoutine({ routines, exceptions, clock, createId }),
    remove: makeDeleteRoutine({ routines, blocks, exceptions, uow, clock }),
  };
}

async function close(db: ReturnType<typeof createTempoDb>) {
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
}

it('adds a routine, sorts its days, and rejects invalid input', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const routine = await h.add({
      title: '  Morning walk  ',
      kind: 'routine',
      daysOfWeek: [5, 1, 3],
      preferredStartMin: 480,
      durationMin: 30,
      anchor: true,
    });
    expect(routine).toMatchObject({
      title: 'Morning walk',
      daysOfWeek: [1, 3, 5],
      active: true,
    });

    const base = { title: 'Walk', kind: 'routine' as const, daysOfWeek: [1], preferredStartMin: 480, durationMin: 30, anchor: true };
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, title: '  ' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'EMPTY_TITLE' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, title: 'x'.repeat(201) })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'TITLE_TOO_LONG' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, daysOfWeek: [] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, daysOfWeek: [0, 7] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, daysOfWeek: [1, 1] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, preferredStartMin: 1440 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, durationMin: 4 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, durationMin: 30.5 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.add({ ...base, preferredStartMin: 1430, durationMin: 30 })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('places a routine once, respects due days and skips, and updates it', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    const routine = await h.add({
      title: 'Morning walk',
      kind: 'routine',
      daysOfWeek: [1],
      preferredStartMin: 480,
      durationMin: 30,
      anchor: true,
    });

    const placed = await h.place({ routineId: routine.id, date: '2026-10-26' });
    expect(await h.blocks.get(placed.blockId)).toMatchObject({

    await expect(h.place({ routineId: 'missing', date: '2026-10-26' })).rejects.toMatchObject({

    expect(await h.skip({ routineId: routine.id, date: '2026-10-26' })).toEqual(skipped);
    expect(await h.exceptions.listByDate('2026-10-26')).toHaveLength(1);

    expect(updated).toMatchObject({ title: 'Evening walk', preferredStartMin: 1020, active: true });
    await expect(h.update({ id: 'missing', patch: {} })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND' });
  } finally {

  }
});

it('removes today and future uncompleted blocks on delete and keeps clean history', async () => {
  const h = harness();

      kind: 'routine',
      daysOfWeek: [1],
      preferredStartMin: 420,
      durationMin: 30,
      anchor: true,
    });
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'past-planned', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Past', routineId: routine.id, anchor: true, now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'today-planned', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 420, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Today', routineId: routine.id, anchor: true, now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'future-planned', date: '2026-10-27', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Future', routineId: routine.id, anchor: true, now: 1 }),
      {
        ...createBlock({ id: 'today-done', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Done', routineId: routine.id, anchor: true, now: 1 }),
        status: 'done' as const,
        completedAt: 2,
      },
    ]);
    await h.exceptions.put({ id: 'skip-1', routineId: routine.id, date: '2026-10-25', type: 'skip', createdAt: 1 });
    await h.exceptions.put({ id: 'skip-2', routineId: routine.id, date: '2026-10-27', type: 'skip', createdAt: 1 });

    const result = await h.remove(routine.id);
    expect(result).toEqual({ deletedBlocks: 2, retainedBlocks: 2, deletedExceptions: 2 });
    expect(await h.routines.get(routine.id)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('today-planned')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('future-planned')).toBeUndefined();
    expect((await h.blocks.get('past-planned'))?.routineId).toBeUndefined();
    expect((await h.blocks.get('today-done'))?.status).toBe('done');
    expect((await h.blocks.get('today-done'))?.routineId).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.exceptions.listByRoutine(routine.id)).toHaveLength(0);

    const backup = await new DexieBackupGateway(h.db, h.uow).exportAll();
    expect(() => validateBackupFile(backup)).not.toThrow();
    await expect(h.remove('missing')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ROUTINE_NOT_FOUND' });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('pushes movable blocks when an anchored routine takes the preferred slot', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.put(
      createBlock({ id: 'movable', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', title: 'Focus', now: 1 }),
    );
    const routine = await h.add({
      title: 'Standup',
      kind: 'routine',
      daysOfWeek: [1],
      preferredStartMin: 480,
      durationMin: 30,
      anchor: true,
    });
    const placed = await h.place({ routineId: routine.id, date: '2026-10-26' });
    expect(placed.startMin).toBe(480);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('movable'))?.startMin).toBe(510);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});
```

Expected: all pass. Deleting a routine removes its `RoutineException` rows and today/future uncompleted blocks, and strips `routineId` from retained history so a backup can never carry a dangling reference; the test proves the exported file still validates. Skipping twice returns the same exception. An anchored routine pushes movable blocks out of its preferred slot.

import { previewReflow } from '../../domain/services/reflow';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';

export function makePreviewReflow(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository }) {
  return async (input: { date: LocalDate; overrunBlockId: string; nowMin: number }) => {
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    return previewReflow({ blocks, nowMin: input.nowMin, overrunBlockId: input.overrunBlockId });

`apply-reflow.ts`:

```ts
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities';
import { blocksOverlap } from '../../domain/services/overlap';
import type { BlockShift } from '../../domain/services/reflow';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import { MINUTES_PER_DAY } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

const STALE_MESSAGE = 'The day changed. Preview the reflow again.';

export function makeApplyReflow(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; uow: UnitOfWork; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { date: LocalDate; changes: BlockShift[] }) => {
    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    const byId = new Map(blocks.map((block) => [block.id, block]));
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const updatedBlocks: Block[] = [];
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();

    for (const change of input.changes) {
      if (seen.has(change.blockId)) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'The reflow lists the same block twice.');
      seen.add(change.blockId);

      const block = byId.get(change.blockId);
      if (!block) throw new DomainError('BLOCK_NOT_FOUND', 'That block no longer exists.');
      if (block.status === 'done') throw new DomainError('BLOCK_DONE', 'A completed block cannot be reflowed.');
      if (block.anchor) throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'An anchored block cannot be reflowed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(change.toStartMin) || change.toStartMin < 0) {
        throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a valid start time.');
      }
      if (!Number.isInteger(change.toDurationMin) || change.toDurationMin < 5) {
        throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'Choose a duration of at least 5 minutes.');
      }
      // A change written against an older version of the day must not be
      // applied on top of newer data.
      if (block.startMin !== change.fromStartMin || block.durationMin !== change.fromDurationMin) {
        throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', STALE_MESSAGE);
      }
      if (change.toStartMin + change.toDurationMin > MINUTES_PER_DAY) {
        throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'A block cannot cross midnight.');
      }

      const updated = { ...block, startMin: change.toStartMin, durationMin: change.toDurationMin, updatedAt: now };
      byId.set(block.id, updated);
      updatedBlocks.push(updated);
    }

    // Validation runs once, on the finished day. Checking each change in
    // isolation would miss two shifted blocks landing on each other.
    const next = [...byId.values()].sort((a, b) => a.startMin - b.startMin);
    for (let index = 1; index < next.length; index += 1) {
      const previous = next[index - 1];
      const current = next[index];
      if (previous && current && blocksOverlap(previous, current)) {
        throw new DomainError('BLOCK_OVERLAP', STALE_MESSAGE);
      }
    }

    await deps.uow.run(['blocks'], async () => {
      await deps.blocks.bulkPut(updatedBlocks);
    });
    return updatedBlocks.length;
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Test preview and apply**

```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { createTempoDb } from '../../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieBlockRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository';
import { makeApplyReflow } from './apply-reflow';
import { makePreviewReflow } from './preview-reflow';

function harness() {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-reflow-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const blocks = new DexieBlockRepository(db);
  const clock = { now: () => new Date(1000) };
  return {
    db,
    blocks,
    preview: makePreviewReflow({ blocks }),
    apply: makeApplyReflow({ blocks, uow, clock }),
  };
}

async function close(db: ReturnType<typeof createTempoDb>) {
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
}

const day = '2026-10-25';

it('previews and applies a reflow in one transaction', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'a', date: day, startMin: 540, durationMin: 60, kind: 'task', title: 'A', now: 100 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'b', date: day, startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'B', now: 100 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'c', date: day, startMin: 630, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'C', now: 100 }),
    ]);

    const result = await h.preview({ date: day, overrunBlockId: 'a', nowMin: 615 });
    expect(result.kind).toBe('preview');
    if (result.kind !== 'preview') return;

    expect(await h.apply({ date: day, changes: result.changes })).toBe(2);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('b'))?.startMin).toBe(615);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('c'))?.startMin).toBe(645);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('c'))?.updatedAt).toBe(1000);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('a'))?.updatedAt).toBe(100);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('reports no work when the day is already clear or nothing follows', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'a', date: day, startMin: 540, durationMin: 60, kind: 'task', title: 'A', now: 100 }),
    ]);
    await expect(h.preview({ date: day, overrunBlockId: 'a', nowMin: 540 })).resolves.toEqual({
      kind: 'noop',
      reason: 'already-clear',
    });
    await expect(h.preview({ date: day, overrunBlockId: 'a', nowMin: 615 })).resolves.toEqual({
      kind: 'noop',
      reason: 'no-blocks-after',
    });
    await expect(h.preview({ date: day, overrunBlockId: 'missing', nowMin: 615 })).resolves.toEqual({
      kind: 'noop',
      reason: 'already-clear',
    });
    expect(await h.apply({ date: day, changes: [] })).toBe(0);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('protects anchored, completed, and stale blocks', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'anchor', date: day, startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'break', title: 'Break', anchor: true, now: 100 }),
      {
        ...createBlock({ id: 'done', date: day, startMin: 660, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Done', now: 100 }),
        status: 'done' as const,
        completedAt: 101,
      },
      createBlock({ id: 'b', date: day, startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'B', now: 100 }),
    ]);
    const move = (blockId: string, fromStartMin: number, toStartMin: number) => ({
      blockId,
      fromStartMin,
      fromDurationMin: 30,
      toStartMin,
      toDurationMin: 30,
    });

    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [move('anchor', 600, 570)] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [move('done', 660, 630)] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_DONE' });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [move('b', 545, 570)] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [move('missing', 540, 570)] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BLOCK_NOT_FOUND',
    });
    expect((await h.blocks.get('b'))?.updatedAt).toBe(100);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('rejects duplicate ids and invalid times', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'b', date: day, startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'B', now: 100 }),
    ]);
    const base = { blockId: 'b', fromStartMin: 540, fromDurationMin: 30, toStartMin: 570, toDurationMin: 30 };

    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [base, base] })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INPUT' });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [{ ...base, toStartMin: -5 }] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [{ ...base, toStartMin: 540.5 }] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [{ ...base, toDurationMin: 4 }] })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes: [{ ...base, toStartMin: 1420, toDurationMin: 30 }] })).rejects.toMatchObject(
      { code: 'INVALID_INPUT' },
    );
    expect(await h.apply({ date: day, changes: [base] })).toBe(1);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('rejects a plan where two shifted blocks land on each other', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'b', date: day, startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'B', now: 100 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'c', date: day, startMin: 660, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'C', now: 100 }),
    ]);
    const changes = [
      { blockId: 'b', fromStartMin: 600, fromDurationMin: 30, toStartMin: 660, toDurationMin: 30 },
      { blockId: 'c', fromStartMin: 660, fromDurationMin: 30, toStartMin: 660, toDurationMin: 30 },
    ];
    await expect(h.apply({ date: day, changes })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BLOCK_OVERLAP' });
    // A rejected apply writes nothing.
    expect((await h.blocks.get('b'))?.startMin).toBe(600);
    expect((await h.blocks.get('c'))?.startMin).toBe(660);
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});
```

Expected: all pass. `applyReflow` rejects duplicate block ids, a stale `fromStartMin`/`fromDurationMin`, anchored or completed blocks, invalid times, and a block crossing midnight before any write. It validates overlap once against the finished day, so two shifted blocks landing on each other are caught. Only the shifted blocks are written, so untouched blocks keep their `updatedAt`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Implement close day**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { DomainError } from '../../domain/errors';
import { toLocalDate, type LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export interface CloseDayUndo {
  task: Task;
  blocks: Block[];
}

export interface CloseDayResult {
  deletedBlocks: number;
  futureDeletedBlocks: number;
  archived: boolean;
  returnedToInbox: boolean;
  undo: CloseDayUndo | null;
}

function emptyResult(): CloseDayResult {
  return { deletedBlocks: 0, futureDeletedBlocks: 0, archived: false, returnedToInbox: false, undo: null };
}

function isMovableWork(block: Block): boolean {
  return block.kind === 'task' || block.kind === 'focus';
}

export function makeCloseDay(deps: { tasks: TaskRepository; blocks: BlockRepository; uow: UnitOfWork; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (input: { date: LocalDate; taskId: string; action: 'keep' | 'let-go' }): Promise<CloseDayResult> => {
    if (input.date > toLocalDate(deps.clock.now())) {
      throw new DomainError('INVALID_INPUT', 'You can close only today or a past day.');
    }

    const task = await deps.tasks.get(input.taskId);
    if (!task) throw new DomainError('TASK_NOT_FOUND', 'That task no longer exists.');
    if (task.status === 'archived') return emptyResult();

    const blocks = await deps.blocks.listByDate(input.date);
    const unfinished = blocks.filter(
      (block) => block.taskId === task.id && isMovableWork(block) && block.status === 'planned',
    );
    if (unfinished.length === 0) return emptyResult();

    let deletedBlocks = 0;
    let futureDeletedBlocks = 0;
    let archived = false;
    let returnedToInbox = false;
    let futureBlocks: Block[] = [];
    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();

    await deps.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks'], async () => {
      await deps.blocks.bulkDelete(unfinished.map((block) => block.id));
      deletedBlocks = unfinished.length;

      if (input.action === 'let-go') {
        const remaining = await deps.blocks.listByTask(task.id);
        futureBlocks = remaining.filter(
          (block) => block.date > input.date && block.status === 'planned' && isMovableWork(block),
        );
        if (futureBlocks.length) await deps.blocks.bulkDelete(futureBlocks.map((block) => block.id));
        futureDeletedBlocks = futureBlocks.length;
        await deps.tasks.put({ ...task, status: 'archived', completedAt: undefined, updatedAt: now });
        archived = true;
        return;
      }

      const remaining = await deps.blocks.listByTask(task.id);
      const hasFuture = remaining.some(
        (block) => block.date > input.date && block.status === 'planned' && isMovableWork(block),
      );
      await deps.tasks.put({
        ...task,
        status: hasFuture ? 'planned' : 'inbox',
        carriedFrom: hasFuture ? undefined : input.date,
        completedAt: undefined,
        updatedAt: now,
      });
      returnedToInbox = !hasFuture;
    });

    return {
      deletedBlocks,
      futureDeletedBlocks,
      archived,
      returnedToInbox,
      undo: { task, blocks: [...unfinished, ...futureBlocks] },
    };
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement carry-over**

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { planCarryOver, type CarryOverPlan } from '../../domain/services/carry-over';
import { isLocalDate, toLocalDate, type LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { MetaRepository } from '../ports/meta-repository';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export interface CarryOverUndo {
  blocks: Block[];
  tasks: Task[];
}

export interface CarryOverResult extends CarryOverPlan {
  undo: CarryOverUndo;
}

function emptyResult(): CarryOverResult {
  return {
    deleteBlockIds: [],
    taskUpdates: [],
    carriedTaskIds: [],
    undo: { blocks: [], tasks: [] },
  };
}

export function makeCarryOver(deps: {
  tasks: TaskRepository;
  blocks: BlockRepository;
  meta: MetaRepository;
  uow: UnitOfWork;
  clock: Clock;
}) {
  return async (today: LocalDate = toLocalDate(deps.clock.now())): Promise<CarryOverResult> => {
    const [tasks, blocks, activeMeta, planMeta] = await Promise.all([
      deps.tasks.listAll(),
      deps.blocks.listAll(),
      deps.meta.get('lastActiveDate'),
      deps.meta.get('realPlanDates'),
    ]);

    const lastActiveDate =
      typeof activeMeta?.value === 'string' && isLocalDate(activeMeta.value) ? activeMeta.value : null;
    const realPlanDates = Array.isArray(planMeta?.value)
      ? planMeta.value.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && isLocalDate(value))
      : [];
    const hasPlanToday = blocks.filter((block) => block.date === today).length >= 3;
    const nextRealPlanDates =
      hasPlanToday && !realPlanDates.includes(today) ? [...realPlanDates, today].sort() : realPlanDates;

    if (!lastActiveDate) {
      await deps.uow.run(['meta'], async () => {
        await deps.meta.put({ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: today });
        await deps.meta.put({ key: 'realPlanDates', value: nextRealPlanDates });
      });
      return emptyResult();
    }

    // The rollover runs once per calendar day. A backwards clock or a repeated
    // call must never carry the same blocks twice.
    if (today < lastActiveDate) return emptyResult();
    if (today === lastActiveDate) {
      if (hasPlanToday && !realPlanDates.includes(today)) {
        await deps.uow.run(['meta'], async () => {
          await deps.meta.put({ key: 'realPlanDates', value: nextRealPlanDates });
        });
      }
      return emptyResult();
    }

    const now = deps.clock.now().getTime();
    const plan = planCarryOver({ today, tasks, blocks, now });
    const changedTaskIds = new Set(plan.taskUpdates.map((task) => task.id));
    const deletedIds = new Set(plan.deleteBlockIds);
    const undo = {
      blocks: blocks.filter((block) => deletedIds.has(block.id)),
      tasks: tasks.filter((task) => changedTaskIds.has(task.id)),
    };

    await deps.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks', 'meta'], async () => {
      if (plan.deleteBlockIds.length) await deps.blocks.bulkDelete(plan.deleteBlockIds);
      if (plan.taskUpdates.length) await deps.tasks.bulkPut(plan.taskUpdates);
      await deps.meta.put({ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: today });
      await deps.meta.put({ key: 'realPlanDates', value: nextRealPlanDates });
    });
    return { ...plan, undo };
  };
}
```

`restore-close-day.ts`:

```ts
import type { CloseDayUndo } from './close-day';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makeRestoreCloseDay(deps: { tasks: TaskRepository; blocks: BlockRepository; uow: UnitOfWork }) {
  return async (snapshot: CloseDayUndo) => {
    await deps.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks'], async () => {
      await deps.tasks.put(snapshot.task);
      if (snapshot.blocks.length) await deps.blocks.bulkPut(snapshot.blocks);
    });
  };
}
```

`restore-carry-over.ts`:

```ts
import type { CarryOverUndo } from './carry-over';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export function makeRestoreCarryOver(deps: { tasks: TaskRepository; blocks: BlockRepository; uow: UnitOfWork }) {
  return async (snapshot: CarryOverUndo) => {
    await deps.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks'], async () => {
      if (snapshot.blocks.length) await deps.blocks.bulkPut(snapshot.blocks);
      if (snapshot.tasks.length) await deps.tasks.bulkPut(snapshot.tasks);
    });
  };
}
```
