```ts
import { createBlock } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { createTask } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { createTempoDb } from '../../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieBlockRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository';
import { DexieMetaRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-meta-repository';
import { DexieTaskRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-task-repository';
import { makeCarryOver } from './carry-over';
import { makeCloseDay } from './close-day';
import { makeRestoreCarryOver } from './restore-carry-over';
import { makeRestoreCloseDay } from './restore-close-day';

function harness(now = new Date('2026-10-25T00:05:00')) {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-carry-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const tasks = new DexieTaskRepository(db);
  const blocks = new DexieBlockRepository(db);
  const meta = new DexieMetaRepository(db);
  const clock = { now: () => now };
  return {
    db,
    tasks,
    blocks,
    meta,
    carry: makeCarryOver({ tasks, blocks, meta, uow, clock }),
    close: makeCloseDay({ tasks, blocks, uow, clock }),
    restoreCarry: makeRestoreCarryOver({ tasks, blocks, uow }),
    restoreClose: makeRestoreCloseDay({ tasks, blocks, uow }),
  };
}

async function close(db: ReturnType<typeof createTempoDb>) {
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
}

it('carries unfinished work, closes per task, and undoes both', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.meta.put({ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: '2026-10-24' });
    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Draft', now: 1 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-24', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Draft', taskId: 't1', now: 1 }));

    const plan = await h.carry('2026-10-25');
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual(['t1']);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t1')).toMatchObject({ status: 'inbox', carriedFrom: '2026-10-24' });
    expect(await h.meta.get('lastActiveDate')).toMatchObject({ value: '2026-10-25' });
    expect(plan.undo.blocks.map((block) => block.id)).toEqual(['b1']);
    expect(plan.undo.tasks.map((task) => task.id)).toEqual(['t1']);

    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't2', title: 'Keep me', now: 2 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b2', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', title: 'Focus', taskId: 't2', now: 2 }));
    const kept = await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 't2', action: 'keep' });
    expect(kept).toMatchObject({ deletedBlocks: 1, futureDeletedBlocks: 0, archived: false, returnedToInbox: true });
    expect(kept.undo?.blocks.map((block) => block.id)).toEqual(['b2']);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b2')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t2')).toMatchObject({ status: 'inbox', carriedFrom: '2026-10-25' });

    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't3', title: 'Future', now: 3 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b3', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 660, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Future', taskId: 't3', now: 3 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b4', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 660, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Future', taskId: 't3', now: 3 }));
    expect(await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 't3', action: 'keep' })).toMatchObject({
      deletedBlocks: 1,
      futureDeletedBlocks: 0,
      returnedToInbox: false,
    });
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t3')).toMatchObject({ status: 'planned' });

    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't4', title: 'Let go', now: 4 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b5', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 720, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Let go', taskId: 't4', now: 4 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b6', date: '2026-10-27', startMin: 720, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Let go', taskId: 't4', now: 4 }));
    const letGo = await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 't4', action: 'let-go' });
    expect(letGo).toMatchObject({ deletedBlocks: 1, futureDeletedBlocks: 1, archived: true, returnedToInbox: false });
    expect(letGo.undo?.blocks.map((block) => block.id).sort()).toEqual(['b5', 'b6']);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b5')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b6')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t4')).toMatchObject({ status: 'archived' });

    await h.restoreClose(letGo.undo!);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b5')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b6')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t4')).toMatchObject({ status: 'inbox' });

    await h.meta.put({ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: '2026-10-26' });
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b7', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 780, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Draft', taskId: 't1', now: 5 }));
    expect(await h.carry('2026-10-24')).toEqual({
      deleteBlockIds: [],
      taskUpdates: [],
      carriedTaskIds: [],
      undo: { blocks: [], tasks: [] },
    });
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b7')).toBeDefined();

    await h.restoreCarry(plan.undo);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b1')).toBeDefined();

    expect((await h.carry('2026-10-25')).carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('b1')).toBeDefined();
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('guards close day and touches only task and focus blocks', async () => {
  const h = harness(new Date('2026-10-25T10:00:00'));
  try {
    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'One', now: 1 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'future', date: '2026-10-26', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'One', taskId: 't1', now: 1 }));
    await expect(h.close({ date: '2026-10-26', taskId: 't1', action: 'keep' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INPUT',
    });
    await expect(h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 'missing', action: 'keep' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TASK_NOT_FOUND',
    });

    await h.tasks.put({ ...createTask({ id: 'archived', title: 'Old', now: 2 }), status: 'archived' });
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'old', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Old', taskId: 'archived', now: 2 }));
    expect(await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 'archived', action: 'keep' })).toMatchObject({
      deletedBlocks: 0,
      undo: null,
    });
    expect(await h.blocks.get('old')).toBeDefined();

    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 'empty', title: 'Empty', now: 3 }));
    expect(await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 'empty', action: 'keep' })).toMatchObject({
      deletedBlocks: 0,
      undo: null,
    });

    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 'mixed', title: 'Mixed', now: 4 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'work', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 660, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', title: 'Mixed', taskId: 'mixed', now: 4 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'rest', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 690, durationMin: 30, kind: 'break', title: 'Break', taskId: 'mixed', anchor: true, now: 4 }));
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'recurring', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 720, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Walk', taskId: 'mixed', anchor: true, now: 4 }));
    const result = await h.close({ date: '2026-10-25', taskId: 'mixed', action: 'keep' });
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ deletedBlocks: 1, returnedToInbox: true });
    expect(await h.blocks.get('work')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('rest')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('recurring')).toBeDefined();
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('carries only unfinished task and focus blocks, with the latest source date winning', async () => {
  const h = harness();
  try {
    await h.meta.put({ key: 'lastActiveDate', value: '2026-10-24' });
    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't1', title: 'Carry', now: 1 }));
    await h.tasks.put(createTask({ id: 't2', title: 'Continues', now: 2 }));
    await h.tasks.put({ ...createTask({ id: 't3', title: 'Archived', now: 3 }), status: 'archived' });
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'old-1', date: '2026-10-22', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Carry', taskId: 't1', now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'old-2', date: '2026-10-24', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', title: 'Carry', taskId: 't1', now: 1 }),
      {
        ...createBlock({ id: 'done-1', date: '2026-10-24', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Carry', taskId: 't1', now: 1 }),
        status: 'done' as const,
        completedAt: 2,
      },
      createBlock({ id: 'future-1', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Continues', taskId: 't2', now: 2 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'old-3', date: '2026-10-24', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Continues', taskId: 't2', now: 2 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'archived-old', date: '2026-10-23', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'Archived', taskId: 't3', now: 3 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'break-old', date: '2026-10-23', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'break', title: 'Tea', taskId: 't1', anchor: true, now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'routine-old', date: '2026-10-23', startMin: 600, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'Walk', now: 1 }),
    ]);

    const plan = await h.carry('2026-10-25');
    expect([...plan.deleteBlockIds].sort()).toEqual(['archived-old', 'old-1', 'old-2', 'old-3']);
    expect(plan.carriedTaskIds).toEqual(['t1']);
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t1')).toMatchObject({ status: 'inbox', carriedFrom: '2026-10-24' });
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t2')).toMatchObject({ status: 'planned' });
    expect((await h.tasks.get('t2'))?.carriedFrom).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t3')).toMatchObject({ status: 'archived' });

    expect(await h.blocks.get('done-1')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('break-old')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('routine-old')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('future-1')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('old-1')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('old-2')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('old-3')).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('archived-old')).toBeUndefined();

    await h.restoreCarry(plan.undo);
    expect(await h.blocks.get('old-1')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.blocks.get('archived-old')).toBeDefined();
    expect(await h.tasks.get('t1')).toMatchObject({ status: 'inbox' });
    expect((await h.tasks.get('t1'))?.carriedFrom).toBeUndefined();
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});

it('records the first real plan once and advances the active date only', async () => {
  const h = harness(new Date('2026-10-25T08:00:00'));
  try {
    await h.blocks.bulkPut([
      createBlock({ id: 'b1', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 480, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'A', now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'b2', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 510, durationMin: 30, kind: 'focus', title: 'B', now: 1 }),
      createBlock({ id: 'b3', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 540, durationMin: 30, kind: 'routine', title: 'C', now: 1 }),
    ]);
    expect((await h.carry('2026-10-25')).carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(await h.meta.get('lastActiveDate')).toMatchObject({ value: '2026-10-25' });
    expect(await h.meta.get('realPlanDates')).toMatchObject({ value: ['2026-10-25'] });

    await h.meta.put({ key: 'realPlanDates', value: ['2026-10-20'] });
    await h.blocks.put(createBlock({ id: 'b4', date: '2026-10-25', startMin: 570, durationMin: 30, kind: 'task', title: 'D', now: 1 }));
    expect((await h.carry('2026-10-25')).carriedTaskIds).toEqual([]);
    expect(await h.meta.get('realPlanDates')).toMatchObject({ value: ['2026-10-20', '2026-10-25'] });
  } finally {
    await close(h.db);
  }
});
```

Expected: all pass. Close day ignores routine and break blocks, refuses a future date, and returns an empty result for archived or already-clear tasks. `keep` deletes today's unfinished block and either keeps the task `planned` (future blocks remain) or returns it to `inbox` with `carriedFrom`. `let-go` also deletes later uncompleted blocks and archives the task. Carry-over deletes only past planned task/focus blocks with a `taskId`, never routines, breaks, or completed blocks, uses the latest source date, leaves future-planned tasks `planned`, skips archived tasks, and runs once per date. Both snapshots restore the deleted blocks and the prior task records.

- [ ] **Step 1: Implement settings**

```ts
import { defaultSettings, type Settings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { SettingsRepository } from '../ports/settings-repository';

export function makeUpdateSettings(deps: { settings: SettingsRepository; clock: Clock }) {
  return async (patch: Partial<Omit<Settings, 'id' | 'updatedAt'>>): Promise<Settings> => {
    const current = (await deps.settings.get()) ?? defaultSettings(deps.clock.now().getTime());
    const next: Settings = { ...current, ...patch, id: 'app', updatedAt: deps.clock.now().getTime() };
    if (!['system', 'dark', 'light'].includes(next.theme)) throw new Error('Invalid theme.');
    if (!['system', '12h', '24h'].includes(next.timeFormat)) throw new Error('Invalid time format.');
    if (![0, 5, 10, 15, 30, 60].includes(next.icsAlarmMinutes)) throw new Error('Invalid alarm.');
    await deps.settings.put(next);
    return next;
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement backup export**

```ts
import type { BackupCodec, BackupGateway } from '../ports/backup-gateway';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { MetaRepository } from '../ports/meta-repository';
import type { ShareGateway } from '../ports/share-gateway';

export function makeExportBackup(deps: {
  backup: BackupGateway;
  codec: BackupCodec;
  share: ShareGateway;
  meta: MetaRepository;
  clock: Clock;
}) {
  return async (options?: { passphrase?: string }) => {
    const backup = await deps.backup.exportAll();
    const encrypted = Boolean(options?.passphrase);
    const payload = encrypted ? await deps.codec.encrypt(backup, options!.passphrase!) : backup;
    const fileName = encrypted
      ? `tempo-backup-${backup.exportedAt.slice(0, 10)}.enc.json`
      : `tempo-backup-${backup.exportedAt.slice(0, 10)}.json`;
    const file = new File([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], fileName, { type: 'application/json' });
    const method = await deps.share.shareFile(file, encrypted ? 'TEMPO encrypted backup' : 'TEMPO backup');
    await deps.meta.put({ key: 'lastBackupAt', value: deps.clock.now().getTime() });
    return { fileName, method, encrypted };
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement backup import**

```ts
import type { BackupCodec, BackupGateway } from '../ports/backup-gateway';

export function makeImportBackup(deps: { backup: BackupGateway; codec: BackupCodec }) {
  return async (input: { text: string; mode: 'replace' | 'merge'; passphrase?: string }) => {
    const file = await deps.codec.parseText(input.text, input.passphrase);
    return deps.backup.importAll(file, input.mode);
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `.ics` export**

```ts
import { toLocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { CalendarExportGateway } from '../ports/calendar-export-gateway';
import type { Clock } from '../ports/clock';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { SettingsRepository } from '../ports/settings-repository';
import type { ShareGateway } from '../ports/share-gateway';

export function makeExportIcs(deps: {
  blocks: BlockRepository;
  settings: SettingsRepository;
  calendar: CalendarExportGateway;
  share: ShareGateway;
  clock: Clock;
}) {
  return async (date?: string) => {
    const target = date ?? toLocalDate(deps.clock.now());
    const [blocks, settings] = await Promise.all([deps.blocks.listByDate(target), deps.settings.get()]);
    const file = await deps.calendar.exportDay(target, blocks, settings?.icsAlarmMinutes ?? 10);
    const method = await deps.share.shareFile(file, `TEMPO ${target}`);
    return { fileName: file.name, method };
  };
}
```

```ts
import { createTempoDb } from '../../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieSettingsRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-settings-repository';
import { DexieMetaRepository } from '../../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-meta-repository';
import { DexieBackupGateway } from '../../infrastructure/export/dexie-backup-gateway';
import { backupCodec } from '../../infrastructure/export/backup-codec';
import { makeUpdateSettings } from './update-settings';
import { makeExportBackup } from './export-backup';

it('updates settings and records the last backup date', async () => {
  const db = createTempoDb(`tempo-settings-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
  const settings = new DexieSettingsRepository(db);
  const meta = new DexieMetaRepository(db);
  const clock = { now: () => new Date('2026-10-25T12:00:00Z') };
  const update = makeUpdateSettings({ settings, clock });
  expect((await update({ theme: 'dark', icsAlarmMinutes: 15 })).theme).toBe('dark');

  const backup = new DexieBackupGateway(db, new DexieUnitOfWork(db));
  const exportBackup = makeExportBackup({ backup, codec: backupCodec, meta, clock, share: { canShareFile: () => false, shareFile: async () => 'downloaded' } });
  const result = await exportBackup();
  expect(result.fileName).toBe('tempo-backup-2026-10-25.json');
  expect(await meta.get('lastBackupAt')).toMatchObject({ key: 'lastBackupAt' });
  db.close();
  await db.delete();
});
```

```tsx
import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { AppServices } from '../../application/services';

const ServicesContext = createContext<AppServices | null>(null);

export function ServicesProvider({ services, children }: { services: AppServices; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <ServicesContext.Provider value={services}>{children}</ServicesContext.Provider>;
}

export function useServices(): AppServices {
  const value = useContext(ServicesContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('ServicesProvider is missing');
  return value;
}
```

```ts
import type { AppServices } from '../application/services';
import { makeAddTask } from '../application/use-cases/add-task';
import { makeUpdateTask } from '../application/use-cases/update-task';
import { makePlaceTask } from '../application/use-cases/place-task';
import { makeMoveBlock } from '../application/use-cases/move-block';
import { makeResizeBlock } from '../application/use-cases/resize-block';
import { makeCompleteBlock } from '../application/use-cases/complete-block';
import { makeDeleteBlock } from '../application/use-cases/delete-block';
import { makeAddBreak } from '../application/use-cases/add-break';
import { makeAddFocus } from '../application/use-cases/add-focus';
import { makeAddRoutine } from '../application/use-cases/add-routine';
import { makeUpdateRoutine } from '../application/use-cases/update-routine';
import { makeDeleteRoutine } from '../application/use-cases/delete-routine';
import { makePlaceRoutine } from '../application/use-cases/place-routine';
import { makeSkipRoutine } from '../application/use-cases/skip-routine';
import { makePreviewReflow } from '../application/use-cases/preview-reflow';
import { makeApplyReflow } from '../application/use-cases/apply-reflow';
import { makeCloseDay } from '../application/use-cases/close-day';
import { makeCarryOver } from '../application/use-cases/carry-over';
import { makeRestoreCloseDay } from '../application/use-cases/restore-close-day';
import { makeRestoreCarryOver } from '../application/use-cases/restore-carry-over';
import { makeExportBackup } from '../application/use-cases/export-backup';
import { makeImportBackup } from '../application/use-cases/import-backup';
import { makeExportIcs } from '../application/use-cases/export-ics';
import { makeUpdateSettings } from '../application/use-cases/update-settings';
import { createTempoDb } from '../infrastructure/db/tempo-db';
import { DexieUnitOfWork } from '../infrastructure/db/unit-of-work';
import { DexieTaskRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-task-repository';
import { DexieBlockRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-block-repository';
import { DexieRoutineRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-routine-repository';
import { DexieRoutineExceptionRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-routine-exception-repository';
import { DexieSettingsRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-settings-repository';
import { DexieMetaRepository } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-meta-repository';
import { DexieDayPlanReader } from '../infrastructure/repositories/dexie-day-plan-reader';
import { SystemClock } from '../infrastructure/time/system-clock';
import { DexieBackupGateway } from '../infrastructure/export/dexie-backup-gateway';
import { backupCodec } from '../infrastructure/export/backup-codec';
import { BrowserCalendarExportGateway } from '../infrastructure/export/browser-calendar-export-gateway';
import { BrowserShareGateway } from '../infrastructure/platform/browser-share-gateway';
import { BrowserStorageGateway } from '../infrastructure/platform/browser-storage-gateway';
import { isStandalone } from '../infrastructure/platform/standalone';

export function createContainer(): AppServices {
  const db = createTempoDb();
  const uow = new DexieUnitOfWork(db);
  const tasks = new DexieTaskRepository(db);
  const blocks = new DexieBlockRepository(db);
  const routines = new DexieRoutineRepository(db);
  const exceptions = new DexieRoutineExceptionRepository(db);
  const settings = new DexieSettingsRepository(db);
  const meta = new DexieMetaRepository(db);
  const clock = new SystemClock();
  const createId = () => crypto.randomUUID();
  const backup = new DexieBackupGateway(db, uow);
  const calendar = new BrowserCalendarExportGateway();
  const share = new BrowserShareGateway();
  const storage = new BrowserStorageGateway();

  return {
    clock, dayPlan: new DexieDayPlanReader(db), storage, isStandalone,
    addTask: makeAddTask({ tasks, clock, createId }),
    updateTask: makeUpdateTask({ tasks, clock }),
    placeTask: makePlaceTask({ tasks, blocks, uow, clock, createId }),
    moveBlock: makeMoveBlock({ blocks, uow, clock }),
    resizeBlock: makeResizeBlock({ blocks, clock }),
    completeBlock: makeCompleteBlock({ blocks, tasks, uow, clock }),
    deleteBlock: makeDeleteBlock({ blocks, tasks, uow, clock }),
    addBreak: makeAddBreak({ blocks, clock, createId }),
    addFocus: makeAddFocus({ blocks, clock, createId }),
    addRoutine: makeAddRoutine({ routines, clock, createId }),
    updateRoutine: makeUpdateRoutine({ routines, clock }),
    deleteRoutine: makeDeleteRoutine({ routines, blocks, uow, clock }),
    placeRoutine: makePlaceRoutine({ routines, blocks, exceptions, uow, clock, createId }),
    skipRoutine: makeSkipRoutine({ routines, exceptions, clock, createId }),
    previewReflow: makePreviewReflow({ blocks }),
    applyReflow: makeApplyReflow({ blocks, uow, clock }),
    closeDay: makeCloseDay({ tasks, blocks, uow, clock }),
    carryOver: makeCarryOver({ tasks, blocks, meta, uow, clock }),
    restoreCloseDay: makeRestoreCloseDay({ tasks, blocks, uow }),
    restoreCarryOver: makeRestoreCarryOver({ tasks, blocks, uow }),
    exportBackup: makeExportBackup({ backup, codec: backupCodec, share, meta, clock }),
    importBackup: makeImportBackup({ backup, codec: backupCodec }),
    exportIcs: makeExportIcs({ blocks, settings, calendar, share, clock }),
    updateSettings: makeUpdateSettings({ settings, clock }),
  };
}
```

`use-now.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useNow(intervalMs = 30_000) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    let timer: number | undefined;
    const tick = () => setNow(new Date());
    const start = () => {
      tick();
      if (timer === undefined) timer = window.setInterval(tick, intervalMs);
    };
    const stop = () => {
      if (timer !== undefined) window.clearInterval(timer);
      timer = undefined;
    };
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') start();
      else stop();
    };
    start();
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    return () => {
      stop();
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    };
  }, [intervalMs]);
  return now;
}
```

`use-theme.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { Settings } from '../../domain/entities/settings';

export function useTheme(settings: Settings | undefined) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!settings) return;
    const media = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
    const apply = () => {
      const dark = settings.theme === 'dark' || (settings.theme === 'system' && media.matches);
      document.documentElement.dataset.theme = dark ? 'dark' : 'light';
      document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.setAttribute('content', dark ? '#05070c' : '#f8f7f4');
    };
    apply();
    if (settings.theme !== 'system') return;
    media.addEventListener('change', apply);
    return () => media.removeEventListener('change', apply);
  }, [settings?.theme]);
}
```

`use-undo.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';

export interface UndoAction { label: string; undo: () => Promise<void>; }

export function useUndo() {
  const stack = useRef<UndoAction[]>([]);
  const [, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const push = useCallback((action: UndoAction) => {
    stack.current = [...stack.current.slice(-9), action];
    setVersion((value) => value + 1);
  }, []);
  const undo = useCallback(async () => {
    const action = stack.current.at(-1);
    if (!action) return;
    stack.current = stack.current.slice(0, -1);
    setVersion((value) => value + 1);
    await action.undo();
  }, []);
  return { canUndo: stack.current.length > 0, label: stack.current.at(-1)?.label, push, undo };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the drag hook**

`use-drag.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { resolvePlacement, type PlacementResult } from '../../domain/services/placement';

export interface DragState {
  blockId: string;
  originStartMin: number;
  originDurationMin: number;
  startY: number;
  currentStartMin: number;
}

export function useDrag(input: {
  blocks: Block[];
  hourHeight: number;
  minStartMin: number;
  onCommit: (blockId: string, result: PlacementResult) => void;
}) {
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<DragState | null>(null);
  const dragRef = useRef<DragState | null>(null);

  const start = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent, block: Block) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    (event.currentTarget as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
    const next = { blockId: block.id, originStartMin: block.startMin, originDurationMin: block.durationMin, startY: event.clientY, currentStartMin: block.startMin };
    dragRef.current = next;
    setDrag(next);
  }, []);

  const move = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent) => {
    const current = dragRef.current;
    if (!current) return;
    const deltaMin = Math.round(((event.clientY - current.startY) / input.hourHeight) * 60 / 5) * 5;
    const result = resolvePlacement({
      blocks: input.blocks,
      movingBlockId: current.blockId,
      targetStartMin: current.originStartMin + deltaMin,
      minStartMin: input.minStartMin,
    });
    if (!result.ok) return;
    const next = { ...current, currentStartMin: result.movingStartMin };
    dragRef.current = next;
    setDrag(next);
  }, [input.blocks, input.hourHeight, input.minStartMin]);

  const end = useCallback(() => {
    const current = dragRef.current;
    if (!current) return;
    dragRef.current = null;
    setDrag(null);
    if (current.currentStartMin === current.originStartMin) return;
    const result = resolvePlacement({
      blocks: input.blocks,
      movingBlockId: current.blockId,
      targetStartMin: current.currentStartMin,
      minStartMin: input.minStartMin,
    });
    input.onCommit(current.blockId, result);
  }, [input.blocks, input.minStartMin, input.onCommit]);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    dragRef.current = null;
    setDrag(null);
  }, []);

  const ghost = useMemo(() => {
    if (!drag) return null;
    return resolvePlacement({
      blocks: input.blocks,
      movingBlockId: drag.blockId,
      targetStartMin: drag.currentStartMin,
      minStartMin: input.minStartMin,
    });
  }, [drag, input.blocks, input.minStartMin]);

  return { drag, ghost, start, move, end, cancel };
}
```

```tsx
import { renderHook, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useNow } from './use-now';
import { useUndo } from './use-undo';

it('ticks the clock and stores an undo action', async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useNow(1000));
  const first = result.current.getTime();
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000));
  expect(result.current.getTime()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(first);
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

it('undoes the last action', async () => {
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useUndo());
  const undone = vi.fn();
  act(() => result.current.push({ label: 'Move', undo: async () => { undone(); } }));
  await act(async () => { await result.current.undo(); });
  expect(undone).toHaveBeenCalled();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add time formatting**

`format-time.ts`:

```ts
export function formatTime(minuteOfDay: number, format: 'system' | '12h' | '24h' = 'system', locale = navigator.language): string {
  const date = new Date(2026, 0, 1, Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60), minuteOfDay % 60);
  if (format === '24h') return `${String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  if (format === '12h') return date.toLocaleTimeString(locale, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', hour12: true });
  return date.toLocaleTimeString(locale, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
}

export function formatDuration(minutes: number): string {
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const rest = minutes % 60;
  if (hours === 0) return `${rest} min`;
  if (rest === 0) return `${hours}h`;
  return `${hours}h ${rest}m`;
}
```

```tsx
import type { Block as BlockEntity } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { formatDuration, formatTime } from '../utils/format-time';
import styles from './Block.module.css';

export function Block({
  block,
  timeFormat,
  dragging,
  onOpen,
  onPointerDown,
  onPointerMove,
  onPointerUp,
}: {
  block: BlockEntity;
  timeFormat: 'system' | '12h' | '24h';
  dragging: boolean;
  onOpen: (block: BlockEntity) => void;
  onPointerDown: (event: React.PointerEvent, block: BlockEntity) => void;
  onPointerMove: (event: React.PointerEvent) => void;
  onPointerUp: (event: React.PointerEvent) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <article
      className={`${styles.block} ${styles[block.kind]} ${block.status === 'done' ? styles.done : ''} ${dragging ? styles.dragging : ''}`}
      style={{ top: (block.startMin / 60) * 88, height: Math.max(28, (block.durationMin / 60) * 88 - 2) }}
      onPointerDown={(event) => onPointerDown(event, block)}
      onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
      onPointerUp={onPointerUp}
      onPointerCancel={onPointerUp}
      onClick={() => onOpen(block)}
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') onOpen(block); }}
      aria-label={`${block.title}, ${formatTime(block.startMin, timeFormat)}, ${formatDuration(block.durationMin)}`}
    >
      <span className={styles.title}>{block.title}</span>
      <span className={styles.meta}>{formatTime(block.startMin, timeFormat)} · {formatDuration(block.durationMin)}</span>
      <span className={styles.handle} aria-hidden="true" />
    </article>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the timeline**

`Timeline.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import { Block as TimelineBlock } from './Block';
import { HourGrid, HOUR_HEIGHT } from './HourGrid';
import { NowLine } from './NowLine';
import styles from './Timeline.module.css';

export function Timeline({
  viewportRef,
  blocks,
  nowMin,
  timeFormat,
  dragBlockId,
  onOpen,
  onPointerDown,
  onPointerMove,
  onPointerUp,
}: {
  viewportRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
  blocks: Block[];
  nowMin: number;
  timeFormat: 'system' | '12h' | '24h';
  dragBlockId?: string;
  onOpen: (block: Block) => void;
  onPointerDown: (event: React.PointerEvent, block: Block) => void;
  onPointerMove: (event: React.PointerEvent) => void;
  onPointerUp: (event: React.PointerEvent) => void;
}) {
  const localViewport = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const viewport = viewportRef ?? localViewport;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (viewport.current) viewport.current.scrollTop = Math.max(0, (nowMin / 60) * HOUR_HEIGHT - 160);
  }, []);
  return (
    <div ref={viewport} className={styles.viewport}>
      <div className={styles.canvas} style={{ height: 24 * HOUR_HEIGHT }}>
        <HourGrid />
        <NowLine nowMin={nowMin} />
        {blocks.map((block) => (
          <TimelineBlock
            key={block.id}
            block={block}
            timeFormat={timeFormat}
            dragging={dragBlockId === block.id}
            onOpen={onOpen}
            onPointerDown={onPointerDown}
            onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
            onPointerUp={onPointerUp}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Implement the tray**

`Tray.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { Routine, RoutineException } from '../../domain/entities/routine';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { isRoutineDue, isRoutinePlaced } from '../../domain/services/routine-occurrence';
import { formatTime } from '../utils/format-time';
import styles from './Tray.module.css';

export function Tray({
  date,
  tasks,
  blocks,
  routines,
  exceptions,
  timeFormat,
  onAddTask,
  onOpenTask,
  onPlaceTask,
  onPlaceRoutine,
  onSkipRoutine,
  onOpenRoutine,
  onStartDrag,
  onOpenPlan,
}: {
  date: string;
  tasks: Task[];
  blocks: Block[];
  routines: Routine[];
  exceptions: RoutineException[];
  timeFormat: 'system' | '12h' | '24h';
  onAddTask: (title: string) => Promise<void>;
  onOpenTask: (task: Task) => void;
  onPlaceTask: (task: Task) => void;
  onPlaceRoutine: (routine: Routine) => void;
  onSkipRoutine: (routine: Routine) => void;
  onOpenRoutine: (routine: Routine) => void;
  onStartDrag: (event: React.PointerEvent, task: Task) => void;
  onOpenPlan: () => void;
}) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const inbox = tasks.filter((task) => task.status === 'inbox');
  const due = useMemo(
    () => routines.filter((routine) => isRoutineDue(routine, date, exceptions) && !isRoutinePlaced(blocks, routine.id, date)),
    [routines, date, exceptions, blocks],
  );

  const submit = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await onAddTask(title);
      setTitle('');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className={`${styles.tray} ${expanded ? styles.expanded : styles.collapsed}`} aria-label="Task tray">
      <div className={styles.topRow}>
        <button type="button" className={styles.handle} onClick={() => setExpanded((value) => !value)} aria-expanded={expanded}>
          {expanded ? 'Collapse' : 'Plan'}
        </button>
        <span className={styles.count}>{inbox.length} to place</span>
        <button type="button" className={styles.planButton} onClick={onOpenPlan}>Add block</button>
      </div>

      <form className={styles.brainDump} onSubmit={submit}>
        <label className={styles.srOnly} htmlFor="brain-dump">Brain dump</label>
        <input
          id="brain-dump"
          value={title}
          onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="What needs doing?"
          autoComplete="off"
          enterKeyHint="done"
          maxLength={200}
        />
        <button type="submit" disabled={!title.trim() || submitting}>Add</button>
      </form>

      <div className={styles.list} role="list" aria-label="Tasks to place">
        {inbox.length === 0 ? <p className={styles.empty}>Nothing waiting. Add a thought above.</p> : null}
        {inbox.map((task) => (
          <div key={task.id} className={styles.item} role="listitem">
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.chip}
              onPointerDown={(event) => onStartDrag(event, task)}
              onClick={() => onOpenTask(task)}
            >
              <span className={styles.chipTitle}>{task.title}</span>
              {task.carriedFrom ? <span className={styles.badge}>Carried over</span> : null}
            </button>
            <button type="button" className={styles.place} onClick={() => onPlaceTask(task)} aria-label={`Place ${task.title}`}>
              Place
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {expanded ? (
        <div className={styles.routines} aria-label="Routines">
          <h2>Routines</h2>
          {due.length === 0 ? <p className={styles.empty}>No routines due today.</p> : null}
          {due.map((routine) => (
            <div key={routine.id} className={styles.item}>
              <button type="button" className={styles.chip} onClick={() => onOpenRoutine(routine)}>
                <span className={styles.chipTitle}>{routine.title}</span>
                <span className={styles.meta}>{formatTime(routine.preferredStartMin, timeFormat)} · {routine.durationMin} min</span>
              </button>
              <button type="button" className={styles.place} onClick={() => onPlaceRoutine(routine)}>Place</button>
              <button type="button" className={styles.skip} onClick={() => onSkipRoutine(routine)}>Skip</button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the tray drag hook**

`use-tray-drag.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { snapTo5 } from '../../domain/values/minute-of-day';

export interface TrayDragState { taskId: string; startMin: number; }

export function useTrayDrag(input: {
  yToMinute: (clientY: number) => number;
  onCommit: (taskId: string, startMin: number) => void;
}) {
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<TrayDragState | null>(null);
  const dragRef = useRef<TrayDragState | null>(null);
  const cleanup = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => cleanup.current?.(), []);

  const start = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent, taskId: string) => {
    const startY = event.clientY;
    let moved = false;
    const startMin = snapTo5(input.yToMinute(event.clientY));
    const next = { taskId, startMin };
    dragRef.current = next;
    setDrag(next);
    const move = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (Math.abs(event.clientY - startY) > 8) moved = true;
      const next = { taskId, startMin: snapTo5(input.yToMinute(event.clientY)) };
      dragRef.current = next;
      setDrag(next);
    };
    const end = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      cleanup.current?.();
      dragRef.current = null;
      setDrag(null);
      if (!moved) return;
      const final = { taskId, startMin: snapTo5(input.yToMinute(event.clientY)) };
      input.onCommit(final.taskId, final.startMin);
    };
    const cancel = () => {
      cleanup.current?.();
      dragRef.current = null;
      setDrag(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', end, { once: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', cancel, { once: true });
    cleanup.current = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', end);
      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', cancel);
      cleanup.current = null;
    };
  }, [input.yToMinute, input.onCommit]);

  return { drag, start };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add snapshot restore for undo**

`restore-day.ts`:

```ts
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';
import type { BlockRepository } from '../ports/block-repository';
import type { TaskRepository } from '../ports/task-repository';
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../ports/unit-of-work';

export interface DaySnapshot { date: LocalDate; blocks: Block[]; tasks: Task[]; }

export function makeRestoreDay(deps: { blocks: BlockRepository; tasks: TaskRepository; uow: UnitOfWork }) {
  return async (snapshot: DaySnapshot) => {
    const current = await deps.blocks.listByDate(snapshot.date);
    const remove = current.filter((block) => !snapshot.blocks.some((item) => item.id === block.id)).map((block) => block.id);
    await deps.uow.run(['blocks', 'tasks'], async () => {
      if (remove.length) await deps.blocks.bulkDelete(remove);
      await deps.blocks.bulkPut(snapshot.blocks);
      await deps.tasks.bulkPut(snapshot.tasks);
    });
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Implement the scoped sheet**

`Sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import styles from './Sheet.module.css';

export function Sheet({ open, title, onClose, children, footer }: {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const panel = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    panel.current?.focus();
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        event.preventDefault();
        onClose();
      }
      if (event.key !== 'Tab' || !panel.current) return;
      const focusable = panel.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])');
      if (focusable.length === 0) return;
      const first = focusable[0];
      const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) { event.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
      if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) { event.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      previous?.focus();
    };
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return createPortal(
    <div className={styles.backdrop} onPointerDown={onClose}>
      <section
        ref={panel}
        className={styles.panel}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={title}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onPointerDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <header className={styles.header}>
          <h2>{title}</h2>
          <button type="button" className={styles.close} onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">Close</button>
        </header>
        <div className={styles.body}>{children}</div>
        {footer ? <footer className={styles.footer}>{footer}</footer> : null}
      </section>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../../domain/entities/task';
import { formatDuration, formatTime, parseTimeInput, toTimeInput } from '../utils/format-time';
import { Sheet } from './Sheet';
import styles from './TaskDetail.module.css';

export function TaskDetail({
  open, block, task, timeFormat, onClose, onComplete, onReopen, onResize, onMove, onDelete, onArchive,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  block: Block | null;
  task: Task | null;
  timeFormat: 'system' | '12h' | '24h';
  onClose: () => void;
  onComplete: () => void;
  onReopen: () => void;
  onResize: (durationMin: number) => void;
  onMove: (startMin: number) => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onArchive: () => void;
}) {
  if (!block) return null;
  return (
    <Sheet open={open} title="Block" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <h3>{block.title}</h3>
        <p>{formatTime(block.startMin, timeFormat)} · {formatDuration(block.durationMin)}</p>
        {task?.carriedFrom ? <span className={styles.badge}>Carried over</span> : null}
      </div>
      <div className={styles.actions}>
        {block.status === 'planned'
          ? <button type="button" className={styles.primary} onClick={onComplete}>Complete</button>
          : <button type="button" className={styles.primary} onClick={onReopen}>Reopen</button>}
        <button type="button" onClick={onDelete}>Delete block</button>
      </div>
      <section className={styles.section} aria-label="Duration">
        <h4>Duration</h4>
        <div className={styles.chips}>
          {[15, 30, 45, 60, 90].map((duration) => (
            <button key={duration} type="button" className={block.durationMin === duration ? styles.selected : ''} onClick={() => onResize(duration)}>
              {formatDuration(duration)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className={styles.section} aria-label="Move">
        <h4>Move</h4>
        <div className={styles.moveRow}>
          <label>
            Start time
            <input
              type="time"
              defaultValue={toTimeInput(block.startMin)}
              key={block.startMin}
              onChange={(event) => { const value = parseTimeInput(event.target.value); if (value !== undefined) onMove(value); }}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </section>
      {task ? (
        <section className={styles.section}>
          <button type="button" className={styles.quiet} onClick={onArchive}>Archive task</button>
        </section>
      ) : null}
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

Add `toTimeInput` and `parseTimeInput` to `format-time.ts`:

```ts
export function toTimeInput(minuteOfDay: number): string {
  return `${String(Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(minuteOfDay % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function parseTimeInput(value: string): number | undefined {
  const match = /^(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(value);
  if (!match) return undefined;
  const hours = Number(match[1]);
  const minutes = Number(match[2]);
  if (hours < 0 || hours > 23 || minutes < 0 || minutes > 59) return undefined;
  return hours * 60 + minutes;
}
```

```css
.actions { display: flex; gap: var(--tempo-space-2); margin: var(--tempo-space-4) 0; }
.actions button, .chips button, .quiet { min-height: var(--tempo-hit-target); border: 1px solid var(--tempo-color-line); border-radius: var(--tempo-radius-md); background: transparent; padding: 0 var(--tempo-space-3); }
```

`TaskSheet.tsx` contract (for a tray task with no block yet):

- Props: `open`, `task`, `onClose`, `onPlace`, `onArchive`.
- Shows the title, “Carried over” when applicable, and the fact that it is not on the timeline yet.
- Actions: **Place** (44 pt primary) calls `onPlace(task)`; **Archive task** calls `onArchive(task)`; **Close**.
- No delete action for an inbox task; archiving is the quiet removal.
- Test: open a carried task, assert the badge, click Place, assert the callback.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the remaining screens to the stated contract**

`AddSheet.tsx` contract:

- Props: `open`, `date`, `task?`, `onClose`, `onAddTask`, `onAddFocus`, `onAddBreak`.
- Tabs: Task, Focus, Break.
- Task: title (labeled **Title**), date, start time (labeled **Start time**), duration (15/30/45/60/90, labeled **Duration**), Add. Calls `onAddTask({ title, date, startMin, durationMin })` then closes.
- When `task` is provided, the title is prefilled and read-only and submit places that existing task; it must not create a duplicate. This is the non-drag “Place” path from the tray and task sheet.
- Focus: title, date, start, duration. Calls `onAddFocus`.
- Break: title (default “Break”), date, start, duration. Calls `onAddBreak`.
- Empty title keeps the sheet open and shows “Give it a name.”
- All fields are labeled; Add is disabled while saving.
- Test: open, switch tabs, submit a focus block; assert the callback.

`RoutineEditor.tsx` contract:

- Fields: title, kind (Routine/Break), days (seven 44px toggles), preferred start (`type="time"`), duration (15/30/45/60/90), anchor toggle, active toggle.
- Save validates at least one day and a valid duration. Calls `onSave(input)`.
- Delete shows a confirmation Sheet: “Delete this routine? Future placed routine blocks will be removed. Past blocks stay.” Calls `onDelete`.
- Editing never moves existing blocks. Test: toggle Tuesday, save, assert `daysOfWeek` includes 2 and the placed block is untouched.

`CloseDay.tsx` contract:

- Shows date, planned count, done count, carried count, a local `moved` count (incremented after each successful keep), and today’s unfinished task/focus blocks grouped by task.
- Per-task actions: **Keep for tomorrow** and **Let go**.
- Keep calls `onKeep(taskId)`; the use case deletes that task’s unfinished blocks, keeps it planned if it has a future block, otherwise returns it to the inbox with `carriedFrom = date`.
- Let go calls `onLetGo(taskId)` only after a destructive confirmation: “Archive <title>? Today’s unfinished blocks and any future planned blocks for this task will be deleted. Past done blocks stay.”
- The sheet’s **Close** action performs no writes. It never auto-closes.
- Test: let go calls `onLetGo(taskId)` only after confirmation; keep calls `onKeep(taskId)` immediately.

`Settings.tsx` contract:

- Sections: Appearance (System/Dark/Light segmented), Time (System/12h/24h), Reflow (auto-apply toggle, default off), Reminders (in-app toggle, explanation), Calendar export alarm (0/5/10/15/30/60), Routines (list, add, edit), Data (storage status, persistent status, last backup, and a **Data and backup** button that opens the `ImportExport` sheet), Install (instructions if not standalone), About (local-only privacy statement, version).
- Theme changes call `updateSettings({ theme })` immediately; no Save button.
- Every toggle is a 44px target and has a visible label.
- Test: change theme and assert `document.documentElement.dataset.theme` changes; change alarm and assert the settings patch.

`ImportExport.tsx` contract:

- Export backup: offers **Plain JSON** and **Encrypted JSON**. Encrypted asks for a passphrase of at least 12 characters and a confirmation; calls `exportBackup({ passphrase })`, then shows “Encrypted backup ready” plus the share/download result. The passphrase is held only in component state and cleared on close; it is never logged, stored, or put in a URL.
- Import: the file input is labeled **Import backup** and accepts `.json`; read the file as text in the component and pass it to `makeImportBackup`, never `JSON.parse` in the component. Choose Replace or Merge; if the file is encrypted, ask for the passphrase and retry once on failure; Replace requires confirmation and offers “Export current data first”; on success show added/updated/skipped.
- A failed import shows one plain message and makes no writes. Do not reveal whether a passphrase or the file was wrong.
- Export `.ics`: calls `exportIcs(date)`.
- Storage: “Stored on this iPhone”, persistent storage status, usage/quota, last backup date.
- Reset all data: destructive confirmation; calls `resetAll`.
- Test: invalid JSON shows the plain error and makes no writes; merge shows counts; an encrypted export/import round-trip works with the correct passphrase and fails closed with the wrong one.

`InstallCard.tsx` contract:

- Show only when `!isStandalone()` and `!settings.installCardDismissed`.
- iOS Safari instructions: Share → Add to Home Screen → Add; “Open from the Home Screen for the full app.”
- “Not now” calls `updateSettings({ installCardDismissed: true })`.
- Test: renders the instructions and dismisses.

`Toast.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import styles from './Toast.module.css';

interface ToastState { id: number; message: string; actionLabel?: string; onAction?: () => void; }
interface ToastApi { show: (message: string, action?: { label: string; onAction: () => void }) => void; }
const ToastContext = createContext<ToastApi | null>(null);

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<ToastState | null>(null);
  const show = useCallback((message: string, action?: { label: string; onAction: () => void }) => {
    setToast({ id: Date.now(), message, actionLabel: action?.label, onAction: action?.onAction });
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!toast) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 5_000);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [toast]);
  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ show }}>
      {children}
      {toast ? (
        <div className={styles.toast} role="status" aria-live="polite">
          <span>{toast.message}</span>
          {toast.actionLabel && toast.onAction ? <button type="button" onClick={() => { toast.onAction?.(); setToast(null); }}>{toast.actionLabel}</button> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useToast(): ToastApi {
  const value = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('ToastProvider is missing');
  return value;
}
```

```ts
import type { UnitOfWork } from '../../application/ports/unit-of-work';
import type { ResetGateway } from '../../application/ports/reset-gateway';
import type { TempoDb } from './tempo-db';

export class DexieResetGateway implements ResetGateway {
  constructor(private readonly db: TempoDb, private readonly uow: UnitOfWork) {}
  async resetAll() {
    await this.uow.run(['tasks', 'blocks', 'routines', 'routineExceptions', 'settings', 'meta'], async () => {
      await Promise.all([this.db.tasks.clear(), this.db.blocks.clear(), this.db.routines.clear(), this.db.routineExceptions.clear(), this.db.settings.clear(), this.db.meta.clear()]);
    });
  }
}
```

`use-install.ts`:

```ts
import { useMemo } from 'react';

export function useInstall(input: { settingsDismissed: boolean; standalone: boolean }) {
  return useMemo(() => {
    const ua = navigator.userAgent;
    const ios = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(ua) || (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
    return { show: ios && !input.standalone && !input.settingsDismissed };
  }, [input.settingsDismissed, input.standalone]);
}
```

`use-reminder.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';

export function useReminder(input: {
  blocks: Block[];
  nowMin: number;
  enabled: boolean;
  onRemind: (block: Block) => void;
}) {
  const reminded = useRef(new Set<string>());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!input.enabled || document.visibilityState !== 'visible') return;
    const due = input.blocks.find((block) => block.status === 'planned' && (block.kind === 'task' || block.kind === 'focus') && block.startMin === input.nowMin);
    if (due && !reminded.current.has(due.id)) {
      reminded.current.add(due.id);
      input.onRemind(due);
    }
  }, [input.blocks, input.nowMin, input.enabled, input.onRemind]);
}
```

`use-overrun.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { blockEnd, type Block } from '../../domain/entities/block';

export function useOverrun(input: {
  blocks: Block[];
  nowMin: number;
  enabled: boolean;
  onOverrun: (blockId: string) => void;
}) {
  const seen = useRef(new Set<string>());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!input.enabled) return;
    const overrun = input.blocks.find((block) =>
      block.status === 'planned' &&
      blockEnd(block) <= input.nowMin &&
      !seen.current.has(block.id) &&
      input.blocks.some((other) => other.id !== block.id && other.status === 'planned' && other.startMin > block.startMin),
    );
    if (!overrun) return;
    seen.current.add(overrun.id);
    input.onOverrun(overrun.id);
  }, [input.blocks, input.nowMin, input.enabled, input.onOverrun]);
}
```

`use-midnight.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { LocalDate } from '../../domain/values/local-date';

export function useMidnight(input: {
  today: LocalDate;
  viewingDate: LocalDate;
  blocked: boolean;
  onRollover: (next: LocalDate) => void;
  onAdvance: (next: LocalDate) => void;
}) {
  const previousToday = useRef(input.today);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ from: LocalDate; to: LocalDate } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (input.today === previousToday.current) return;
    const from = previousToday.current;
    previousToday.current = input.today;
    input.onRollover(input.today);
    if (input.viewingDate === from) setPending({ from, to: input.today });
  }, [input.today, input.viewingDate, input.onRollover]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pending || input.blocked) return;
    if (input.viewingDate !== pending.from) {
      setPending(null);
      return;
    }
    input.onAdvance(pending.to);
    setPending(null);
  }, [pending, input.blocked, input.viewingDate, input.onAdvance]);
}
```

`use-midnight.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useMidnight } from './use-midnight';

it('rolls over once and waits for a sheet to close before advancing', () => {
  const onRollover = vi.fn();
  const onAdvance = vi.fn();
  const { rerender } = renderHook((props) => useMidnight(props), {
    initialProps: {
      today: '2026-10-25',
      viewingDate: '2026-10-25',
      blocked: true,
      onRollover,
      onAdvance,
    },
  });

  rerender({
    today: '2026-10-26',
    viewingDate: '2026-10-25',
    blocked: true,
    onRollover,
    onAdvance,
  });
  expect(onRollover).toHaveBeenCalledWith('2026-10-26');
  expect(onAdvance).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

  rerender({
    today: '2026-10-26',
    viewingDate: '2026-10-25',
    blocked: false,
    onRollover,
    onAdvance,
  });
  expect(onAdvance).toHaveBeenCalledWith('2026-10-26');
});
```

`use-visual-viewport.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export function useVisualViewport() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const viewport = window.visualViewport;
    if (!viewport) return;
    const apply = () => document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--tempo-visual-height', `${viewport.height}px`);
    apply();
    viewport.addEventListener('resize', apply);
    viewport.addEventListener('scroll', apply);
    return () => {
      viewport.removeEventListener('resize', apply);
      viewport.removeEventListener('scroll', apply);
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty('--tempo-visual-height');
    };
  }, []);
}
```

```tsx
}
```

`App.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { toLocalDate } from '../domain/values/local-date';
import type { Block } from '../domain/entities/block';
import type { Task } from '../domain/entities/task';
import type { Routine } from '../domain/entities/routine';
import type { BlockShift } from '../domain/services/reflow';
import { formatTime } from '../presentation/utils/format-time';
import { AppShell } from '../presentation/components/AppShell';
import { Header } from '../presentation/components/Header';
import { DaySummary } from '../presentation/components/DaySummary';
import { Timeline } from '../presentation/components/Timeline';
import { Tray } from '../presentation/components/Tray';
import { ReflowPreview } from '../presentation/components/ReflowPreview';
import { TaskDetail } from '../presentation/components/TaskDetail';
import { TaskSheet } from '../presentation/components/TaskSheet';
import { AddSheet } from '../presentation/components/AddSheet';
import { RoutineEditor } from '../presentation/components/RoutineEditor';
import { CloseDay } from '../presentation/components/CloseDay';
import { Settings } from '../presentation/components/Settings';
import { ImportExport } from '../presentation/components/ImportExport';
import { InstallCard } from '../presentation/components/InstallCard';
import { ToastProvider, useToast } from '../presentation/components/Toast';
import { useServices } from '../presentation/providers/services';
import { useDayPlan } from '../presentation/hooks/use-day-plan';
import { useNow } from '../presentation/hooks/use-now';
import { useTheme } from '../presentation/hooks/use-theme';
import { useDrag } from '../presentation/hooks/use-drag';
import { useTrayDrag } from '../presentation/hooks/use-tray-drag';
import { useUndo } from '../presentation/hooks/use-undo';
import { useInstall } from '../presentation/hooks/use-install';
import { useReminder } from '../presentation/hooks/use-reminder';
import { useVisualViewport } from '../presentation/hooks/use-visual-viewport';
import { useOverrun } from '../presentation/hooks/use-overrun';
import { useMidnight } from '../presentation/hooks/use-midnight';
import { createContainer } from './container';
import { Providers } from './providers';

const HOUR_HEIGHT = 88;

function Screen() {
  const services = useServices();
  const [date, setDate] = useState(() => toLocalDate(new Date()));
  const [selectedBlockId, setSelectedBlockId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedTaskId, setSelectedTaskId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<'add' | 'routine' | 'close' | 'settings' | 'data' | null>(null);
  const [editingRoutine, setEditingRoutine] = useState<Routine | null>(null);
  const [reflowChanges, setReflowChanges] = useState<BlockShift[] | null>(null);
  const timelineRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const plan = useDayPlan(date);
  const now = useNow();
  const undo = useUndo();
  const toast = useToast();
  useTheme(plan?.settings);
  useVisualViewport();

  const today = toLocalDate(now);
  useMidnight({
    today,
    viewingDate: date,
    blocked: sheet !== null || selectedBlockId !== null || selectedTaskId !== null || reflowChanges !== null,
    onRollover: (next) => {
      void services.carryOver(next).then((result) => {
        if (result.carriedTaskIds.length === 0) return;
        undo.push({ label: 'Carry over', undo: () => services.restoreCarryOver(result.undo) });
        const count = result.carriedTaskIds.length;
        toast.show(`${count} task${count === 1 ? '' : 's'} carried over from yesterday.`, {
          label: 'Undo',
          onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); },
        });
      });
    },
    onAdvance: setDate,
  });
  const nowMin = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  const timeFormat = plan?.settings.timeFormat ?? 'system';
  const minStartMin = date === today ? nowMin : 0;
  const canCloseDay = date === today && nowMin >= 18 * 60;
  const blocks = plan?.blocks ?? [];
  const selectedBlock = blocks.find((block) => block.id === selectedBlockId) ?? null;
  const blockTask = selectedBlock?.taskId ? plan?.tasks.find((task) => task.id === selectedBlock.taskId) ?? null : null;
  const trayTask = selectedTaskId ? plan?.tasks.find((task) => task.id === selectedTaskId) ?? null : null;
  const install = useInstall({ settingsDismissed: plan?.settings.installCardDismissed ?? false, standalone: services.isStandalone() });

  useEffect(() => {
    void services.carryOver();
    void services.storage.requestPersistence();
  }, [services]);

  const snapshot = useCallback(() => ({ date, blocks: plan?.blocks ?? [], tasks: plan?.tasks ?? [] }), [date, plan?.blocks, plan?.tasks]);

  const commitMove = useCallback(async (blockId: string, result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof services.moveBlock>>) => {
    if (!result.ok) { toast.show('No room for that move.'); return; }
    const before = snapshot();
    try {
      await services.moveBlock({ blockId, targetStartMin: result.movingStartMin, minStartMin });
      undo.push({ label: 'Move', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
      toast.show(`Moved to ${formatTime(result.movingStartMin, timeFormat)}`, { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
    } catch (error) {
      toast.show(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not move that block.');
    }
  }, [minStartMin, services, snapshot, timeFormat, toast, undo]);

  const drag = useDrag({ blocks, hourHeight: HOUR_HEIGHT, minStartMin, onCommit: commitMove });
  const trayDrag = useTrayDrag({
    yToMinute: (clientY) => {
      const viewport = timelineRef.current;
      if (!viewport) return 0;
      const rect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
      return ((clientY - rect.top + viewport.scrollTop) / HOUR_HEIGHT) * 60;
    },
    onCommit: async (taskId, startMin) => {
      const before = snapshot();
      try {
        await services.placeTask({ taskId, date, startMin, minStartMin });
        undo.push({ label: 'Place', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
        toast.show('Placed', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
      } catch (error) {
        toast.show(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not place that task.');
      }
    },
  });

  useReminder({ blocks, nowMin, enabled: plan?.settings.inAppReminders ?? false, onRemind: (block) => toast.show(`${block.title} starts now.`) });

  useOverrun({
    blocks,
    nowMin,
    enabled: date === today && Boolean(plan),
    onOverrun: async (blockId) => {
      const result = await services.previewReflow({ date, overrunBlockId: blockId, nowMin });
      if (result.kind !== 'preview' || result.changes.length === 0) return;
      if (plan?.settings.reflowAutoApply) {
        const before = snapshot();
        await services.applyReflow({ date, changes: result.changes });
        undo.push({ label: 'Reflow', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
        toast.show('Day adjusted.', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
        return;
      }
      setReflowChanges(result.changes);
    },
  });

  const openBlock = (block: Block) => setSelectedBlockId(block.id);
  const openTask = (task: Task) => setSelectedTaskId(task.id);
  const openSettings = () => setSheet('settings');
  const openClose = () => { if (canCloseDay) setSheet('close'); };
  const openAdd = () => { setSelectedTaskId(null); setSheet('add'); };

  const complete = async (done: boolean) => {
    if (!selectedBlock) return;
    const before = snapshot();
    await services.completeBlock({ blockId: selectedBlock.id, done });
    undo.push({ label: done ? 'Complete' : 'Reopen', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
    toast.show(done ? 'Done.' : 'Reopened.', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
    setSelectedBlockId(null);
  };

  const resize = async (durationMin: number) => {
    if (!selectedBlock) return;
    const before = snapshot();
    try {
      await services.resizeBlock({ blockId: selectedBlock.id, durationMin });
      undo.push({ label: 'Resize', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
    } catch (error) {
      toast.show(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not resize that block.');
    }
  };

  const moveTo = async (startMin: number) => {
    if (!selectedBlock) return;
    const before = snapshot();
    try {
      await services.moveBlock({ blockId: selectedBlock.id, targetStartMin: startMin, minStartMin });
      undo.push({ label: 'Move', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
      setSelectedBlockId(null);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.show(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not move that block.');
    }
  };

  const remove = async () => {
    if (!selectedBlock) return;
    const before = snapshot();
    await services.deleteBlock(selectedBlock.id);
    undo.push({ label: 'Delete', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
    toast.show('Block deleted.', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
    setSelectedBlockId(null);
  };

  const archive = async () => {
    if (!blockTask) return;
    await services.updateTask({ id: blockTask.id, archive: true });
    setSelectedBlockId(null);
  };

  const applyReflow = async () => {
    if (!reflowChanges) return;
    const before = snapshot();
    await services.applyReflow({ date, changes: reflowChanges });
    undo.push({ label: 'Reflow', undo: () => services.restoreDay(before) });
    setReflowChanges(null);
    toast.show('Day adjusted.', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
  };

  const placeRoutine = async (routine: Routine) => {
    try {
      const result = await services.placeRoutine({ routineId: routine.id, date, minStartMin });
      toast.show(`Placed at ${formatTime(result.startMin, timeFormat)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.show(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'No open slot today.');
    }
  };

  const skipRoutine = async (routine: Routine) => {
    await services.skipRoutine({ routineId: routine.id, date });
    toast.show('Skipped for today.');
  };

  const dismissInstall = async () => {
    await services.updateSettings({ installCardDismissed: true });
  };

  return (
    <AppShell
      header={<Header date={date} today={today} canCloseDay={canCloseDay} onDate={setDate} onSettings={openSettings} onCloseDay={openClose} />}
      tray={(
        <Tray
          date={date}
          tasks={plan?.tasks ?? []}
          blocks={blocks}
          routines={plan?.routines ?? []}
          exceptions={plan?.routineExceptions ?? []}
          timeFormat={timeFormat}
          onAddTask={async (title) => { await services.addTask({ title }); }}
          onOpenTask={openTask}
          onPlaceTask={(task) => { setSelectedTaskId(task.id); setSheet('add'); }}
          onPlaceRoutine={placeRoutine}
          onSkipRoutine={skipRoutine}
          onOpenRoutine={(routine) => { setEditingRoutine(routine); setSheet('routine'); }}
          onStartDrag={(event, task) => { trayDrag.start(event, task.id); }}
          onOpenPlan={openAdd}
        />
      )}
    >
      <DaySummary planned={blocks.filter((block) => block.status === 'planned').length} done={blocks.filter((block) => block.status === 'done').length} minutes={blocks.reduce((total, block) => total + block.durationMin, 0)} />
      <Timeline
        viewportRef={timelineRef}
        blocks={blocks}
        nowMin={date === today ? nowMin : 0}
        timeFormat={timeFormat}
        dragBlockId={drag.drag?.blockId}
        onOpen={openBlock}
        onPointerDown={drag.start}
        onPointerMove={drag.move}
        onPointerUp={drag.end}
      />
      {reflowChanges ? <ReflowPreview count={reflowChanges.length} onApply={applyReflow} onDismiss={() => setReflowChanges(null)} /> : null}
      {install.show ? <InstallCard onDismiss={dismissInstall} /> : null}
      <TaskDetail
        open={Boolean(selectedBlock)}
        block={selectedBlock}
        task={blockTask}
        timeFormat={timeFormat}
        onClose={() => setSelectedBlockId(null)}
        onComplete={() => { void complete(true); }}
        onReopen={() => { void complete(false); }}
        onResize={(duration) => { void resize(duration); }}
        onMove={(startMin) => { void moveTo(startMin); }}
        onDelete={() => { void remove(); }}
        onArchive={() => { void archive(); }}
      />
      <TaskSheet
        open={Boolean(trayTask) && sheet !== 'add'}
        task={trayTask}
        onClose={() => setSelectedTaskId(null)}
        onPlace={(task) => { setSelectedTaskId(task.id); setSheet('add'); }}
        onArchive={async (task) => { await services.updateTask({ id: task.id, archive: true }); setSelectedTaskId(null); toast.show('Task archived.'); }}
      />
      <AddSheet
        open={sheet === 'add'}
        date={date}
        task={trayTask}
        onClose={() => { setSheet(null); setSelectedTaskId(null); }}
        onAddTask={async (input) => {
          const task = trayTask ?? await services.addTask({ title: input.title });
          await services.placeTask({ taskId: task.id, date, startMin: input.startMin, durationMin: input.durationMin, minStartMin });
          setSheet(null);
          setSelectedTaskId(null);
        }}
        onAddFocus={async (input) => { await services.addFocus({ ...input, date }); }}
        onAddBreak={async (input) => { await services.addBreak({ ...input, date }); }}
      />
      <RoutineEditor open={sheet === 'routine'} routine={editingRoutine} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onSave={async (input) => { if (editingRoutine) await services.updateRoutine({ id: editingRoutine.id, patch: input }); else await services.addRoutine(input); setSheet(null); }} onDelete={async () => { if (editingRoutine) await services.deleteRoutine(editingRoutine.id); setSheet(null); }} />
      <CloseDay
        open={sheet === 'close'}
        date={date}
        blocks={blocks}
        onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
        onKeep={async (taskId) => {
          const result = await services.closeDay({ date, taskId, action: 'keep' });
          const before = result.undo;
          if (before) undo.push({ label: 'Keep for tomorrow', undo: () => services.restoreCloseDay(before) });
          const message = result.returnedToInbox ? 'Kept for tomorrow.' : 'Future block stays.';
          if (before) toast.show(message, { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
          else toast.show(message);
        }}
        onLetGo={async (taskId) => {
          const result = await services.closeDay({ date, taskId, action: 'let-go' });
          const before = result.undo;
          if (before) undo.push({ label: 'Let go', undo: () => services.restoreCloseDay(before) });
          if (before) toast.show('Task archived.', { label: 'Undo', onAction: () => { void undo.undo(); } });
          else toast.show('Nothing left to archive.');
        }}
      />
      <Settings open={sheet === 'settings'} settings={plan?.settings} routines={plan?.routines ?? []} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onChange={async (patch) => { await services.updateSettings(patch); }} onEditRoutine={(routine) => { setEditingRoutine(routine); setSheet('routine'); }} onAddRoutine={() => { setEditingRoutine(null); setSheet('routine'); }} onOpenData={() => setSheet('data')} />
      <ImportExport open={sheet === 'data'} settings={plan?.settings} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onExportBackup={services.exportBackup} onImportBackup={services.importBackup} onExportIcs={() => services.exportIcs(date)} onReset={services.resetAll} />
    </AppShell>
  );
}

export function App() {
  const services = useMemo(createContainer, []);
  return (
    <Providers services={services}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <Screen />
      </ToastProvider>
    </Providers>
  );
}
```

## Milestone 5 — Installable shell, accessibility, and performance

**Exit criteria:** the app installs to the iOS Home Screen, reloads offline from the cached shell, passes axe with zero serious or critical issues, meets the JS/CSS/font budgets, and has an end-to-end test for every core journey. No push, analytics, backend, or paid service is introduced.
