- [ ] **Step 1: Install the PWA plugin**

```powershell
npm install -D vite-plugin-pwa workbox-window
```

`vite-plugin-pwa` is a dev dependency. `workbox-window` is the runtime helper the generated registration uses. Neither adds a network service or a paid dependency. Record both in `docs/reference/stack.md` under the build and PWA sections.

- [ ] **Step 2: Generate the icon set from one deterministic source**

Icons are simple, calm, and reproducible. The dark field is the app background; the royal dot is the only brand mark. No text, no gradient, no fine detail that disappears at 60 px.

`scripts/generate-icons.ps1`:

```powershell
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

$out = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\public\icons'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $out | Out-Null

function New-TempoIcon {
  param(
    [int]$Size,
    [string]$Name,
    [double]$DotScale = 0.48
  )

  $bitmap = [System.Drawing.Bitmap]::new($Size, $Size)
  $graphics = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bitmap)
  $graphics.SmoothingMode = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode]::AntiAlias
  $graphics.Clear([System.Drawing.ColorTranslator]::FromHtml('#05070C'))

  $dot = [int]($Size * $DotScale)
  $offset = [int](($Size - $dot) / 2)
  $brush = [System.Drawing.SolidBrush]::new([System.Drawing.ColorTranslator]::FromHtml('#7B5CFF'))
  $graphics.FillEllipse($brush, $offset, $offset, $dot, $dot)

  $bitmap.Save((Join-Path $out $Name), [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
  $brush.Dispose()
  $graphics.Dispose()
  $bitmap.Dispose()
}

New-TempoIcon -Size 192 -Name 'icon-192.png'
New-TempoIcon -Size 512 -Name 'icon-512.png'
New-TempoIcon -Size 1024 -Name 'icon-1024.png'
New-TempoIcon -Size 180 -Name 'apple-touch-icon.png'
New-TempoIcon -Size 512 -Name 'icon-maskable-512.png' -DotScale 0.34
```

Run:

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts/generate-icons.ps1
Get-ChildItem public/icons
```

Expected: five PNG files. The maskable dot sits inside the 80% safe zone. Commit the generated files; do not regenerate them in CI.

- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `index.html` with the production document shell**

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
    <meta name="color-scheme" content="dark light" />
    <meta name="theme-color" media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)" content="#05070c" />
    <meta name="theme-color" media="(prefers-color-scheme: light)" content="#f4f1ea" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
    <meta name="mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="default" />
    <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title" content="TEMPO" />
    <meta name="format-detection" content="telephone=no" />
    <meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer" />
    <!-- TEMPO_CSP -->
    <link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="%BASE_URL%icons/apple-touch-icon.png" />
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="%BASE_URL%boot.css" />
    <title>TEMPO</title>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="root"></div>
    <noscript>TEMPO needs JavaScript. It never sends your day anywhere.</noscript>
    <script type="module" src="/src/main.tsx"></script>
  </body>
</html>
```

`public/boot.css` is the only pre-hydration style. It is self-hosted and same-origin, so the production CSP needs no `unsafe-inline`:

```css
html, body, #root { height: 100%; margin: 0; }
html { background: #05070c; }
body { background: #05070c; color: #f4f1ea; }
#root:empty::after {
  content: 'TEMPO';
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font: 600 13px/1 ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  letter-spacing: 0.24em;
  color: #a99bff;
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: light) {
  html, body { background: #f4f1ea; }
  body { color: #17141f; }
  #root:empty::after { color: #5c3fd1; }
}
html[data-theme='light'], html[data-theme='light'] body { background: #f4f1ea; }
html[data-theme='light'] body { color: #17141f; }
html[data-theme='light'] #root:empty::after { color: #5c3fd1; }
html[data-theme='dark'], html[data-theme='dark'] body { background: #05070c; }
html[data-theme='dark'] body { color: #f4f1ea; }
html[data-theme='dark'] #root:empty::after { color: #a99bff; }
```

The boot stylesheet is only the background and wordmark. All product CSS comes from the token registry. It prevents a white flash before the first paint. The status bar remains `default`; the app does not rely on a translucent status bar or content under it.

The production CSP has no `unsafe-inline` for styles. Do not add a `style` attribute to `index.html` or set styles with `setAttribute('style', ...)`; React's CSSOM updates (`element.style.setProperty`) are allowed and are how the timeline positions blocks. `frame-ancestors` cannot be set from a meta CSP and GitHub Pages cannot add a header; record that as the accepted residual risk in `docs/security/verification.md`, not as a silent gap.

- [ ] **Step 4: Configure Vite, the manifest, the service worker, and a build-only CSP**

```ts
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { defineConfig, type Plugin } from 'vite';
import { VitePWA } from 'vite-plugin-pwa';

const contentSecurityPolicy = [
  "default-src 'self'",
  "script-src 'self'",
  "style-src 'self'",
  "img-src 'self' data:",
  "font-src 'self'",
  "connect-src 'self'",
  "manifest-src 'self'",
  "worker-src 'self'",
  "object-src 'none'",
  "base-uri 'none'",
  "form-action 'none'",
].join('; ');

function cspPlugin(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'tempo-csp',
    apply: 'build',
    transformIndexHtml(html) {
      return html.replace(
        '<!-- TEMPO_CSP -->',
        `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="${contentSecurityPolicy}" />`,
      );
    },
  };
}

export default defineConfig({
  base: process.env.VITE_BASE ?? '/',
  plugins: [
    react(),
    cspPlugin(),
    VitePWA({
      registerType: 'prompt',
      injectRegister: false,
      strategies: 'generateSW',
      includeAssets: ['icons/*.png'],
      manifest: {
        id: './',
        name: 'TEMPO',
        short_name: 'TEMPO',
        description: 'A calm, offline-first daily planner. Your day, one timeline.',
        lang: 'en',
        dir: 'ltr',
        start_url: './',
        scope: './',
        display: 'standalone',
        orientation: 'portrait',
        background_color: '#05070c',
        theme_color: '#05070c',
        categories: ['productivity', 'lifestyle'],
        icons: [
          { src: 'icons/icon-192.png', sizes: '192x192', type: 'image/png' },
          { src: 'icons/icon-512.png', sizes: '512x512', type: 'image/png' },
          { src: 'icons/icon-1024.png', sizes: '1024x1024', type: 'image/png' },
          { src: 'icons/icon-maskable-512.png', sizes: '512x512', type: 'image/png', purpose: 'maskable' },
        ],
      },
      workbox: {
        globPatterns: ['**/*.{js,css,html,woff2,png,svg,webmanifest}'],
        navigateFallback: 'index.html',
        cleanupOutdatedCaches: true,
        clientsClaim: false,
        skipWaiting: false,
      },
      devOptions: { enabled: false },
    }),
  ],
  server: { port: 5173, strictPort: true },
  preview: { port: 4173, strictPort: true },
  build: { target: 'es2022', cssCodeSplit: false },
});
```

Rules this config enforces:

- `registerType: 'prompt'` and `skipWaiting: false`: a new service worker waits. The user sees an update toast and chooses when to reload. The app never auto-reloads under an edit.
- `injectRegister: false`: registration happens through `useRegisterSW` in Task 5.2, not a hidden inline script.
- `navigateFallback: 'index.html'`: an offline cold start serves the shell.
- No `runtimeCaching`: only precached app assets. Backups and `.ics` files are generated as blobs and are never cached.
- `base` is `/` locally and `/tempo/` in GitHub Actions via `VITE_BASE`. The manifest and icons use relative URLs, so the installed app does not assume a domain root.
- CSP is injected only for `build`. Vite's development server needs websocket and inline behavior that a production CSP would break. The production policy allows no third-party origin.

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the manifest and cache checker**

`scripts/check-pwa.mjs`:

```js
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const requiredFiles = [
  'dist/index.html',
  'dist/manifest.webmanifest',
  'dist/sw.js',
  'dist/icons/icon-192.png',
  'dist/icons/icon-512.png',
  'dist/icons/icon-1024.png',
  'dist/icons/icon-maskable-512.png',
  'dist/icons/apple-touch-icon.png',
];

const missing = requiredFiles.filter((file) => !existsSync(file));
if (missing.length > 0) {
  console.error(`Missing PWA files:\n${missing.join('\n')}`);
  process.exit(1);
}

const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync('dist/manifest.webmanifest', 'utf8'));
const requiredFields = [
  'name',
  'short_name',
  'start_url',
  'scope',
  'display',
  'theme_color',
  'background_color',
  'icons',
];

for (const field of requiredFields) {
  if (!manifest[field]) {
    console.error(`Manifest is missing ${field}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

if (manifest.display !== 'standalone') {
  console.error('Manifest display must be standalone');
  process.exit(1);
}

const sizes = new Set((manifest.icons ?? []).map((icon) => icon.sizes));
for (const size of ['192x192', '512x512']) {
  if (!sizes.has(size)) {
    console.error(`Manifest is missing a ${size} icon`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

if (!(manifest.icons ?? []).some((icon) => icon.purpose === 'maskable')) {
  console.error('Manifest is missing a maskable icon');
  process.exit(1);
}

const sw = readFileSync('dist/sw.js', 'utf8');
if (!sw.includes('workbox') && !sw.includes('precache')) {
  console.error('dist/sw.js does not look like a generated service worker');
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log('PWA artifacts are valid.');
```

Add to `package.json`:

```json
"check:pwa": "node scripts/check-pwa.mjs",
"verify:pwa": "npm run build && npm run check:pwa"
```


Manual check in WebKit responsive mode:

2. Set the viewport to iPhone 13. The layout is not wider than the viewport and the boot wordmark is replaced by the app.
3. Confirm the status bar remains the browser's default; nothing is positioned under it.
4. In iOS Safari on a real device, Share → Add to Home Screen. The icon is the royal dot, not a screenshot.

### Task 5.2: Register the service worker and add update and offline messaging

**Files:**
- Create: `src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.tsx`
- Create: `src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.module.css`
- Create: `src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.test.tsx`

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the update toast**

The update toast is the only global overlay that can appear without a user action. It must never steal focus, never auto-reload, and never appear while a field has focus.

`src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRegisterSW } from 'virtual:pwa-register/react';
import styles from './UpdateToast.module.css';

export function UpdateToast() {
  const {
    needRefresh: [needRefresh, setNeedRefresh],
    updateServiceWorker,
  } = useRegisterSW();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => {
      const active = document.activeElement;
      setEditing(Boolean(active?.closest('input, textarea, select, [contenteditable="true"]')));
    };
    document.addEventListener('focusin', update);
    document.addEventListener('focusout', update);
    update();
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('focusin', update);
      document.removeEventListener('focusout', update);
    };
  }, []);

  if (!needRefresh || editing) return null;

  return (
    <div className={styles.toast} role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <span className={styles.text}>New version ready.</span>
      <button type="button" className={styles.action} onClick={() => { void updateServiceWorker(true); }}>
        Reload
      </button>
      <button type="button" className={styles.dismiss} onClick={() => setNeedRefresh(false)}>
        Later
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.module.css`:

```css
.toast {
  position: fixed;
  left: var(--tempo-space-4);
  right: var(--tempo-space-4);
  bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 112px);
  z-index: var(--tempo-z-toast);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--tempo-space-2);
  padding: var(--tempo-space-3);
  border: 1px solid var(--tempo-color-line-strong);
  border-radius: var(--tempo-radius-card);
  background: var(--tempo-color-surface-raised);
  box-shadow: var(--tempo-shadow-float);
}

.text {
  flex: 1;
  font-size: var(--tempo-text-sm);
  color: var(--tempo-color-text);
}

.action,
.dismiss {
  min-height: var(--tempo-size-target);
  min-width: var(--tempo-size-target);
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--tempo-radius-pill);
  font: inherit;
}

.action {
  background: var(--tempo-color-accent-fill);
  color: var(--tempo-color-on-accent);
}

.dismiss {
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--tempo-color-text-muted);
}
```

The bottom offset clears the collapsed tray. If the tray is expanded, the toast sits above the tray's top edge because the tray has a higher stacking context; the toast is informational, not a blocking modal.

In `src/app/App.tsx`, import `UpdateToast` and render it inside the provider:

```tsx

  const services = useMemo(createContainer, []);
  return (
    <Providers services={services}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <Screen />

    </Providers>
  );
}
```

`src/presentation/components/UpdateToast.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { vi } from 'vitest';
import { UpdateToast } from './UpdateToast';

const updateServiceWorker = vi.fn();

vi.mock('virtual:pwa-register/react', () => ({
  useRegisterSW: () => ({
    needRefresh: [true, vi.fn()],
    updateServiceWorker,
  }),
}));

it('waits for the user before reloading', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  render(<UpdateToast />);
  expect(screen.getByText('New version ready.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reload' }));
  expect(updateServiceWorker).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
```

Add a second test where `needRefresh` is `[false, vi.fn()]` and assert nothing renders. Mock modules are hoisted; split into two test files or use `vi.mocked` with a mutable flag. Keep the test focused.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the offline reload test**

`tests/e2e/offline.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

test('the built shell reloads with no network', async ({ page, context }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.waitForFunction(() => 'serviceWorker' in navigator);

  // First load registers the worker. A second load lets it control the page.
  await page.reload();
  await page.waitForFunction(() => navigator.serviceWorker.controller !== null);

  await context.setOffline(true);
  await page.reload();

  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'TEMPO' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Task tray')).toBeVisible();

  await context.setOffline(false);
});
```

The test runs against `vite preview`, not the dev server. The service worker is intentionally disabled in development.

- [ ] **Step 5: Verify update and offline behavior manually**

1. `npm run build && npm run preview`.
2. Load the app once, then reload. DevTools → Application → Service Workers shows `sw.js` activated and controlling.
3. Stop the preview server. Reload. The shell and the empty timeline still render. IndexedDB data is unchanged.
4. Change a visible string, rebuild, and reload. The update toast appears. Do not touch it; the app stays on the old version. Focus the brain dump, reload, and confirm the toast does not appear until focus leaves the field. Click **Reload**; the new version loads.
5. Confirm the toast never appears over the sheet scrim and never traps focus.

### Task 5.3: Harden accessibility, focus, and touch targets

**Files:**

`src/presentation/components/Icon.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { SVGProps } from 'react';

export type IconName =
  | 'task'
  | 'focus'
  | 'routine'
  | 'break'
  | 'carried'
  | 'anchor'
  | 'warning'
  | 'storage'
  | 'share'
  | 'clock';

export const ICON_LABEL: Record<IconName, string> = {
  task: 'Task',
  focus: 'Focus',
  routine: 'Routine',
  break: 'Break',
  carried: 'Carried over',
  anchor: 'Anchored',
  warning: 'Warning',
  storage: 'On this iPhone',
  share: 'Share',
  clock: 'Time',
};

export function Icon({
  name,
  size = 16,
  title,
  ...props
}: { name: IconName; size?: number; title?: string } & Omit<SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>, 'name'>) {
  const decorative = !title;
  return (
    <svg
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={1.5}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      role={decorative ? undefined : 'img'}
      aria-hidden={decorative}
      focusable="false"
      {...props}
    >
      {title ? <title>{title}</title> : null}
      <use href={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}icons/sprite.svg#icon-${name}`} />
    </svg>
  );
}
```

Rules: a decorative icon is `aria-hidden`; a meaningful icon has a `<title>`. A type icon is always paired with visible text (`Task`, `Focus`, `Routine`, `Break`) so the type is never communicated by color alone.

```css
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Audit every target to 44 × 44 pt**

Add a Playwright helper and test. `tests/e2e/a11y.spec.ts`:

```ts
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

async function expectNoSeriousViolations(page: Page) {
  const results = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).analyze();
  const bad = results.violations.filter(
    (violation) => violation.impact === 'serious' || violation.impact === 'critical',
  );
  expect(bad, JSON.stringify(bad, null, 2)).toEqual([]);
}

test.describe('accessibility @a11y', () => {
  test('day and sheets have no serious or critical axe violations', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await expectNoSeriousViolations(page);

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add block' }).click();
    await expectNoSeriousViolations(page);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }).click();

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' }).click();
    await expectNoSeriousViolations(page);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Data and backup' }).click();
    await expectNoSeriousViolations(page);
  });

  test('visible targets are at least 44 by 44 CSS pixels', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    const targets = await page
      .locator('button:visible, [role="button"]:visible, a:visible, input:visible, select:visible')
      .evaluateAll((elements) =>
        elements.map((element) => {
          const box = element.getBoundingClientRect();
          return {
            label: element.getAttribute('aria-label') ?? element.textContent?.trim() ?? element.tagName,
            width: box.width,
            height: box.height,
          };
        }),
      );

    for (const target of targets) {
      expect(target.width, `${target.label} width`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
      expect(target.height, `${target.label} height`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(44);
    }
  });

  test('keyboard opens a block and Escape returns focus', async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add block' }).click();
    await page.getByLabel('Title').fill('Keyboard check');
    await page.getByLabel('Start time').fill('09:00');
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }).click();

    const block = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Keyboard check/ });
    await block.focus();
    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
    await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeVisible();
    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
    await expect(block).toBeFocused();
  });
});
```

If a control legitimately needs a smaller visual mark (the 16 px type icon), the icon is inside a 44 pt button or row; the test only measures interactive elements. A dense inline text link is not added in v1.

- [ ] **Step 7: Verify with the accessibility matrix**

```powershell
npm run build
npx playwright test tests/e2e/a11y.spec.ts
```

Expected: all three tests pass. Then check manually:

1. iOS VoiceOver: swipe through header, timeline, tray, and a sheet. Every icon-only control announces an action; every block announces type, title, time, duration, and state.
2. Increase Dynamic Type to the largest setting. No text is clipped in the header, tray, block, or sheet; the layout scrolls rather than truncating the primary action.
3. Turn on Reduce Motion. The reflow preview appears without FLIP; drag still tracks the finger; the now line still updates.
4. Keyboard only: Tab through the skip link, date controls, timeline blocks, tray actions, sheet fields, and close actions. Focus is always visible and never trapped behind a closed sheet.
5. Color check: carried state, anchored state, and warnings remain understandable with grayscale or a color-blind simulation.

### Task 5.4: Enforce performance budgets and isolate drag work

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/check-size.mjs`
- Create: `tests/e2e/performance.spec.ts`

- Modify: `src/presentation/hooks/use-drag.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the size checker**

`scripts/check-size.mjs`:

```js
import { existsSync, readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib';

const assetDir = 'dist/assets';
if (!existsSync(assetDir)) {
  console.error('dist/assets is missing. Run npm run build first.');
  process.exit(1);
}

const files = readdirSync(assetDir);
const gzipSize = (file) => gzipSync(readFileSync(join(assetDir, file))).length;
const rawSize = (file) => readFileSync(join(assetDir, file)).length;
const total = (extension, sizeOf) =>
  files.filter((file) => file.endsWith(extension)).reduce((sum, file) => sum + sizeOf(file), 0);

const checks = [
  ['JavaScript (gzip)', total('.js', gzipSize), 180 * 1024],
  ['CSS (gzip)', total('.css', gzipSize), 30 * 1024],
  ['UI font (raw WOFF2)', total('.woff2', rawSize), 40 * 1024],
];

let failed = false;
for (const [label, value, limit] of checks) {
  const ok = value <= limit;
  console.log(`${ok ? 'OK  ' : 'FAIL'} ${label}: ${(value / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB / ${(limit / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`);
  if (!ok) failed = true;
}

if (failed) process.exit(1);
```

Add to `package.json`:

```json
"size": "node scripts/check-size.mjs",
"verify": "npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm run test && npm run build && npm run size && npm run check:pwa"
```

The budget counts the app bundle, not `sw.js`; the service worker never runs on the main thread. If the app crosses a budget, fix the cause. Do not raise the budget without a new decision in `docs/design/decisions.md`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Make the drag path frame-bound and memo-friendly**

Current `useDrag` calls `setDrag` on every `pointermove`. That is correct but too expensive for the 60 fps and no-long-task gates. Apply this refactor:

1. Add a `viewportRef` input to `useDrag` and cache `getBoundingClientRect()` at pointer down.
2. Throttle pointermove with `requestAnimationFrame`: store the next minute in a ref, render at most one state update per frame.
3. Only the dragged `Block` and the single ghost read the drag state. Memoize every other block.
4. At pointer up, cancel the pending frame, resolve placement, and commit once. The undo entry is created only for a real change.

`Block.tsx`:

```tsx
import { memo } from 'react';

export const Block = memo(function Block(props: BlockProps) {
  // ...existing body
});
```

`App.tsx`:

```tsx
const openBlock = useCallback((block: Block) => setSelectedBlockId(block.id), []);

const drag = useDrag({
  blocks,
  hourHeight: HOUR_HEIGHT,
  minStartMin,
  viewportRef: timelineRef,
  onCommit: commitMove,
});
```

`useDrag` adds `viewportRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>` to its input and uses `viewportRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()` at `start` instead of reading the block's rect on every move.

`Timeline.tsx` passes stable callbacks to `Block`; the hook's `start`, `move`, and `end` are stable `useCallback` values. Do not pass an inline arrow to every block.

The behavior contract does not change: the ghost previews the landing and the shifts, and the drop commits one move plus shifts as one undoable action. Only the rendering cost changes.

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the frame and DOM budget test**

`tests/e2e/performance.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

test('drag stays under the long-frame and DOM budgets', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date('2026-10-25T08:55:00') });
  await page.goto('/');

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add block' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Focus' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Title').fill('Performance check');
  await page.getByLabel('Start time').fill('09:00');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '30 min' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }).click();

  const block = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Performance check/ });
  await expect(block).toBeVisible();
  const box = await block.boundingBox();
  if (!box) throw new Error('Block has no box');

  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const state = window as Window & { __frames?: number[]; __recording?: boolean };
    state.__frames = [];
    state.__recording = true;
    let previous = performance.now();
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      state.__frames?.push(now - previous);
      previous = now;
      if (state.__recording) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  });

  await page.mouse.move(box.x + 24, box.y + 22);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await page.mouse.move(box.x + 24, box.y + 22 + 88, { steps: 12 });
  await page.mouse.up();

  await page.waitForTimeout(100);
  const result = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const state = window as Window & { __frames?: number[]; __recording?: boolean };
    state.__recording = false;
    return {
      frames: state.__frames ?? [],
      nodes: document.querySelectorAll('*').length,
    };
  });

  expect(result.frames.filter((duration) => duration > 50)).toEqual([]);
  expect(result.nodes).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1500);
});
```

This test measures frame intervals in the same WebKit engine the app targets. The 50 ms threshold matches the product invariant. Lighthouse and INP p75 are measured in the operations plan on every pull request; they cannot be reproduced faithfully in a local unit test.

2. The drag ghost and reflow FLIP are skipped under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`; the drop still commits.
3. Run the performance test with `page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' })`; it must still pass.
4. Background the page for two minutes, return, and confirm the now line is correct and there is exactly one interval (no duplicate timers).

Add these as assertions to the performance test or as a focused hook test. Do not leave an interval running when the page is hidden.

- [ ] **Step 1: Create the Playwright configuration**

`playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'tests/e2e',
  timeout: 30_000,
  expect: { timeout: 5_000 },
  fullyParallel: false,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
  reporter: process.env.CI ? [['github'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]] : 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
    screenshot: 'only-on-failure',
    video: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  projects: [
    { name: 'webkit-iphone', use: { ...devices['iPhone 13'], browserName: 'webkit' } },
    { name: 'chromium-mobile', use: { ...devices['Pixel 7'], browserName: 'chromium' } },
  ],
  webServer: {
    command: 'npm run build && npm run preview -- --host 127.0.0.1',
    url: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    timeout: 120_000,
  },
});
```

The WebKit project is the primary truth for iPhone behavior. The Chromium project catches engine-specific layout and accessibility regressions and is the project used for Lighthouse later. Visual regression configuration and baselines live in the operations plan; this plan only adds behavioral journeys.

- [ ] **Step 2: Add the journey helpers**

`tests/e2e/helpers.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

export async function addFocusBlock(
  page: Page,
  input: { title: string; start: string; duration?: '15' | '30' | '45' | '60' | '90' },
) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add block' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Focus' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Title').fill(input.title);
  await page.getByLabel('Start time').fill(input.start);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `${input.duration ?? '30'} min` }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog')).toBeHidden();
}

export async function addTask(page: Page, title: string) {
  await page.getByLabel('Brain dump').fill(title);
  await page.getByLabel('Brain dump').press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: title })).toBeVisible();
}

export async function dragToTimeline(page: Page, sourceName: string, startMinute: number) {
  const source = page.getByRole('button', { name: sourceName });
  const box = await source.boundingBox();
  if (!box) throw new Error('Drag source has no box');

  const timeline = page.locator('#timeline');
  await timeline.evaluate((element) => {
    element.scrollTop = 0;
  });
  const target = await timeline.boundingBox();
  if (!target) throw new Error('Timeline has no box');

  const y = target.y + (startMinute / 60) * 88;
  await page.mouse.move(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await page.mouse.move(target.x + 80, y, { steps: 12 });
  await page.mouse.up();
}
```

`dragToTimeline` uses the actual 88 px hour height. It scrolls the timeline to the top first so the target coordinate is deterministic. The app still resolves the drop; the helper does not write a block directly.

`tests/e2e/first-run.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { addTask, dragToTimeline } from './helpers';

test('brain dump, drag to the timeline, and undo the placement', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date('2026-10-25T08:30:00') });
  await page.goto('/');

  await addTask(page, 'Write the brief');
  await dragToTimeline(page, 'Write the brief', 9 * 60);

  const block = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Write the brief.*9:00/ });
  await expect(block).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place Write the brief' })).toBeHidden();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Undo' }).click();
  await expect(block).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Write the brief' })).toBeVisible();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the reflow and undo journey**

`tests/e2e/reflow-undo.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { addFocusBlock } from './helpers';

test('overrun previews, applies, and undoes the shift', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date('2026-10-25T08:55:00') });
  await page.goto('/');

  await addFocusBlock(page, { title: 'Deep work', start: '09:00' });
  await addFocusBlock(page, { title: 'Review', start: '10:00' });

  await page.clock.fastForward('00:36');

  await expect(page.getByText('1 block needs to move.')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Apply' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Review.*10:30/ })).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Undo' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Review.*10:00/ })).toBeVisible();
});
```

This test depends on the overrun hook firing only for a planned block whose end is at or before now and that has a later planned block. A completed overrun block must not produce a preview; add that as a second test using the block detail **Done** action.

`tests/e2e/carry-over.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { addTask } from './helpers';

test('unfinished work returns quietly at midnight with a carried badge', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.clock.install({ time: new Date('2026-10-25T23:50:00') });
  await page.goto('/');

  await addTask(page, 'Unfinished draft');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Place Unfinished draft' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Start time').fill('23:00');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '30 min' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add' }).click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Unfinished draft.*23:00/ })).toBeVisible();
  await page.clock.fastForward('00:20');
  await page.reload();

  const carried = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Unfinished draft' });
  await expect(carried).toBeVisible();
  await expect(carried.getByText('Carried over')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: /Unfinished draft.*23:00/ })).toBeHidden();
});
```

The carried task is not auto-placed. The test asserts it is back in the tray, not on tomorrow's timeline. A second test skips a routine and places a break, advances past midnight, and asserts neither carries over.

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the backup round-trip journey**

`tests/e2e/backup.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { addTask } from './helpers';

test('export, reset, and import restores the local day', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  await addTask(page, 'Backup me');

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Data and backup' }).click();

  const downloadPromise = page.waitForEvent('download');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Export backup' }).click();
  const download = await downloadPromise;
  const path = await download.path();
  if (!path) throw new Error('Backup was not written');
  const backup = JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
  expect(backup.app).toBe('tempo');
  expect(backup.schemaVersion).toBe(1);

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset all data' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Backup me' })).toBeHidden();

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Data and backup' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Import backup').setInputFiles(path);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Replace' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Confirm replace' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }).click();

  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Backup me' })).toBeVisible();
});
```

The exact confirmation labels are part of the `ImportExport` contract; keep them stable. If the sheet closes after import, navigate back to the day and assert the restored task. The test must not depend on a real file system outside the Playwright download directory.

- [ ] **Step 6b: Add the security journey**

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/e2e/security.spec.ts`

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

test('the production shell has a strict CSP and no third-party requests', async ({ page }) => {
  const origins = new Set<string>();
  page.on('request', (request) => {
    const url = request.url();
    if (url.startsWith('http')) origins.add(new URL(url).origin);
  });

  await page.goto('/');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'TEMPO' })).toBeVisible();

  const csp = await page.locator('meta[http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy"]').getAttribute('content');
  expect(csp).toContain("default-src 'self'");
  expect(csp).toContain("script-src 'self'");
  expect(csp).toContain("style-src 'self'");
  expect(csp).not.toContain("'unsafe-inline'");
  expect(csp).not.toContain("'unsafe-eval'");
  expect(csp).toContain("object-src 'none'");
  expect(csp).toContain("base-uri 'none'");

  expect(await page.locator('script:not([src])').count()).toBe(0);
  expect(await page.locator('style').count()).toBe(0);
  expect([...origins]).toEqual(['http://127.0.0.1:4173']);
});

test('task text is rendered as text, never as markup', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/');
  const title = '<img src=x onerror="window.__tempoXss=1">';
  await page.getByLabel('Brain dump').fill(title);
  await page.getByLabel('Brain dump').press('Enter');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: title })).toBeVisible();
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as { __tempoXss?: number }).__tempoXss)).toBeUndefined();
});
```

The origin set is exact because the preview server is fixed. If Playwright requests `data:` or `blob:` URLs, they are not network origins and are ignored. A new external origin fails this test.

- [ ] **Step 7: Run the journeys**

```powershell
npm run test:e2e
```

Expected: all journeys pass in WebKit iPhone and Chromium mobile. The first run downloads the browser binaries if needed. Failures include a trace, screenshot, and video in `test-results/`.

If a journey is flaky, fix the product or the test; do not add arbitrary sleeps. Prefer `expect(...).toBeVisible()` and app-visible state over timeouts.


- [ ] **Step 1: Create the acceptance record**

`docs/quality/app-acceptance.md`:

```md
# TEMPO app acceptance

**Status:** awaiting human acceptance · **Plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-25-tempo-app.md`
**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-25-tempo-design.md`

This record is the evidence that the app plan is complete. The operations plan is separate and does not start until this record is accepted.

## Automated gates

| Gate | Command | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Typecheck | `npm run typecheck` | |
| Lint and boundaries | `npm run lint` | |
| Unit and integration tests | `npm run test` | |
| Coverage | `npm run test:coverage` | Domain ≥95%; overall ≥80% |
| Production build | `npm run build` | |
| PWA artifacts | `npm run check:pwa` | |
| Bundle budgets | `npm run size` | JS ≤180 KB; CSS ≤30 KB; font ≤40 KB |
| End-to-end journeys | `npm run test:e2e` | WebKit iPhone + Chromium mobile |
| Security journey | `npm run test:e2e -- tests/e2e/security.spec.ts` | Strict CSP, no inline script, no third-party origin, escaped task text |
| Encrypted backup | `npm run test -- src/infrastructure/export/encrypted-backup.test.ts` | Round-trip; wrong passphrase and tamper fail closed |
| Accessibility | `npm run test:a11y` | Zero serious or critical axe issues |
| Visual regression | operations plan | Dark and light; 375, 393, 440 |

A gate is not “passing” because it was run once locally. Paste the command output or link the CI run in the evidence column. The five `security-*` required checks are created by the operations plan; until then, record the local security journey and note the required checks as not yet active. Do not claim they are active.

## Device matrix

- iPhone 13, iOS 18.4, Safari, installed to the Home Screen.
- iPhone 15 Pro, iOS 26.x, Safari, installed to the Home Screen.
- WebKit at 375 × 812, 393 × 852, and 440 × 956 CSS pixels.
- Dark and light, following the system; explicit Dark; explicit Light.
- VoiceOver on, Reduce Motion on, Increase Contrast on, largest Dynamic Type.
- Low Power Mode and a cold offline launch.

## Manual journeys

- [ ] Capture five tasks in under 60 seconds without leaving the day.
- [ ] Place a task by drag; the ghost previews the landing; the drop is one undoable action.
- [ ] Place a task without dragging via the tray **Place** action and the Add sheet.
- [ ] Move a block earlier over an obstacle; it is refused with a plain message; the plan does not change.
- [ ] Let a focus block overrun; the preview names the number of blocks; Apply shifts them; Undo restores the exact prior plan.
- [ ] Complete a block; it is removed from the active plan and remains in history.
- [ ] Place a routine, skip a routine, and confirm neither is auto-placed.
- [ ] Close the day without changes; the plan stays.
- [ ] Keep a task for tomorrow; it returns to the tray, or stays planned when a future block exists.
- [ ] Let go of a task; it is archived and its future planned blocks are deleted.
- [ ] Advance past midnight; unfinished work carries over quietly with a badge; routines and breaks do not.
- [ ] Export a backup, reset all data, import the backup, and restore the day.
- [ ] Export `.ics`, import it into a calendar, and confirm the alarm choice.
- [ ] Add to Home Screen; launch offline; confirm the shell and data survive a reload.
- [ ] Confirm the app never asks for an account, never sends a network request, and never shows a fake native control.
- [ ] Confirm the now line is the only thing that moves on its own.

## Quality statements

- No backend, account, sync, push, analytics, tracking, paid API, or paid platform dependency.
- No auto-scheduler, calendar events, or notes in v1.
- No sticky app bar, translucent status bar, HTML5 drag-and-drop, or horizontal swipe action.
- Every interactive target is at least 44 × 44 pt with at least 8 px between adjacent targets.
- Time is 88 px per hour with tabular numerals.
- Dark “Midnight & royal” and light “Porcelain” both pass contrast and visual review.

## Known gaps

List anything not verified, with the reason and the follow-up issue. Do not mark the plan complete with an unlisted gap.
```

## App plan definition of done

The app plan is complete only when all of the following are true:

- Every task above is checked, or an unchecked task has a written, accepted reason.
- The automated gates pass on the final commit.
- A real iPhone has installed the app, used it offline, and completed the manual journeys.
- The design contract, decision log, and reference library still describe the app that was built. If they do not, the change is not done.
- No pre-existing user change was overwritten.
- The acceptance record lists what was not verified.
- The human has accepted the app plan.
